## `README.md`

````markdown
# Cutline

Cutline is a video editor for iPhone and iPad. It is written in Swift and SwiftUI and uses AVFoundation, Core Image, Metal, Speech and Vision. It needs iOS 17 or later.

There are no accounts, ads, watermarks or network calls. All processing happens on the device.

- **Unsigned IPA:** `dist/Cutline.ipa`. CI rebuilds it on every push.
- **Xcode project:** `Cutline.xcodeproj`. It needs Xcode 16 or later because it uses synchronized folders.
- **Full source listing:** `SOURCE.md` has the file tree followed by every file in full.

## Building

```bash
open Cutline.xcodeproj                      # then Product › Run (choose your team under Signing)
# or, unsigned from the command line:
xcodebuild -project Cutline.xcodeproj -scheme Cutline -configuration Release \
  -sdk iphoneos -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -derivedDataPath build \
  CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" build
mkdir -p Payload && cp -R build/Build/Products/Release-iphoneos/Cutline.app Payload/
zip -qry Cutline.ipa Payload
```

The workflow in `.github/workflows/build-ios.yml` runs the same steps on a macOS runner with Xcode 16.4 and the iOS 18.5 SDK. It uploads the IPA as an artifact and also commits it to `dist/`.

The IPA is **not signed**. To install it on a device, re-sign it with your own Apple ID or certificate, for example with Xcode, Sideloadly or AltStore.

`tools/make_xcodeproj.py` regenerates the Xcode project and `tools/make_icon.py` regenerates the app icon. Every file under `Cutline/` is added to the target automatically.

## Architecture (MVVM)

```
Views (SwiftUI)  ──▶  ViewModels (@Observable, @MainActor)  ──▶  Services / Rendering
HomeView              EditorViewModel (+Editing, +Media)         ProjectStore, AutosaveService
EditorView            PlaybackController                         MediaImportService, ThumbnailCache
TimelineView          TimelineInteraction (UIKit gestures)       WaveformGenerator, BeatDetector
Panels/*              ExportViewModel                            CaptionService, ExportService
                                                                 ProjectUndoManager, DerivedMediaService
                                                                 StabilizationService, VoiceoverRecorder
                                                                 CompositionBuilder → AVMutableComposition
                                                                 CutlineCompositor (AVVideoCompositing)
                                                                 FrameRenderer, FilterEngine, TextRenderer,
                                                                 TransitionRenderer, Kernels.metal
```

- **Model.** `Project` is a single `Codable` value. It holds the media, the main-track `clips`, `overlayClips` (picture in picture), `overlays` (photos, stickers and shapes), `texts`, `audioClips`, `captions`, the canvas settings, the whole-timeline filter and the editor state (playhead, zoom and selection).
- **Edits.** Every change goes through `EditorViewModel.perform(_:)`. That one function records an undo snapshot, autosaves and refreshes the preview. Slider drags and gestures use `beginInteractive` / `updateInteractive` / `endInteractive`, so a whole drag becomes one undo step while the preview updates live.
- **Rendering.** `CompositionBuilder` builds an `AVMutableComposition`:
  - A black "timing backbone" track covers the full timeline.
  - Clips are packed onto pooled tracks.
  - Speed segments are applied with `scaleTimeRange`. Speed ramps are split into 12 segments.
  - Transition handles are added, using a held frame when there is no spare footage.
  - An `AVMutableAudioMix` handles volume and fades.

  `CutlineCompositor` renders every frame with Core Image on a Metal-backed `CIContext`. That covers stabilization, chroma key (a Metal CI kernel), Vision person segmentation, filters, crop, rotate and flip, fit or fill with a blurred background, keyframes with easing, in/out motion, transitions, the global filter, overlays, text and captions.
- **Fast preview.** Changes that only affect looks (filters, text, transforms) update a shared `RenderStateBox` and re-render the current frame without rebuilding the composition. Videos larger than 1080p get a 720p proxy for editing. Export always renders from the originals.
- **Export.** Export reads the timeline with `AVAssetReader` and writes it with `AVAssetWriter`. You choose the codec (H.264 or HEVC), 720p / 1080p / 4K, 24/30/60 fps and a quality slider that sets the bitrate. Audio-only export produces an M4A. A still frame is rendered by `AVAssetImageGenerator` with the custom compositor.

## Feature map

| Area | Where |
|---|---|
| Projects grid, search, rename/duplicate/delete, templates | `Views/Home/HomeView.swift`, `Services/ProjectStore.swift` |
| Autosave (debounced 0.6 s, on background, backup copy, crash-session restore) | `Services/AutosaveService.swift`, `ProjectFileIO`, `HomeView.restoreSessionIfNeeded` |
| Timeline: fixed playhead, pinch zoom, momentum scrub, trim handles, long-press move, double-tap split, snapping + haptics, lanes, thumbnails, waveforms, beat markers | `Views/Timeline/*` |
| Trim/split/delete/duplicate/reorder/freeze/reverse | `EditorViewModel+Editing.swift`, `+Media.swift`, `DerivedMediaService` |
| Speed 0.1×–8× + ramps (pitch preserved) | `Clip.segments()`, `SpeedPanel` |
| Crop, rotate, flip, zoom/pan, fit/fill, 9:16 · 1:1 · 4:5 · 16:9, blurred fill | `CropPanel`, `CanvasPanel`, `FrameRenderer` |
| Keyframes (position, scale, rotation, opacity) with easing curves | `Transform.swift`, `AnimatePanel` |
| Audio: volume, fades, mute, detach, voiceover, music from Files, waveform, noise reduction | `VolumePanel`, `AudioPanel`, `VoiceoverRecorder`, `DerivedMediaService.denoise` |
| Auto beat markers + "Beat Cuts" | `BeatDetector`, `EditorViewModel.cutToBeats` |
| Text: system font picker with live preview, size, color, outline, shadow, box, alignment, fade/pop/slide/typewriter | `TextPanel`, `TextRenderer`, `FrameRenderer.textLayer` |
| Auto captions (on-device, word timing, editable, 3 styles) | `CaptionService`, `CaptionsPanel` |
| Overlays: photos, video PiP, stickers, shapes | `OverlayPanel`, `OverlayStylePanel` |
| 17 looks + 9 adjustments, per clip or whole timeline | `FilterEngine`, `EffectsPanel`, `GlobalFiltersPanel` |
| Transitions: cut, fade, dissolve, slide, zoom, spin, glitch | `TransitionRenderer`, `TransitionPanel` |
| Background removal (Vision), green screen (Metal kernel), stabilization (Vision) | `FrameRenderer`, `Kernels.metal`, `StabilizationService` |
| Copy / paste effects | `Clip.effectsPayload()`, `EffectsPanel` |
| Export + progress/cancel + background time + Photos/share, audio-only, still frame | `ExportService`, `ExportView` |
| Errors: unsupported formats, low storage, missing media, permission denials | `AppError`, `StorageGuard`, `MediaResolver`, `Permissions` |

## Notes

- Captions require on-device speech recognition for the chosen language (Settings › General › Keyboard › Dictation). Captions are never sent to a server.
- Reverse, noise reduction and stabilization write derived files into the project's `Cache/` folder. Undo/redo only switches which file a clip uses, so it's instant.
````

## `.github/workflows/build-ios.yml`

```yaml
name: Build unsigned IPA

on:
  push:
    branches: ["**"]
    paths-ignore:
      - "dist/**"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: macos-15
    timeout-minutes: 60
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Select Xcode
        run: |
          ls -d /Applications/Xcode*.app || true
          XCODE=$(ls -d /Applications/Xcode_16*.app 2>/dev/null | sort -V | tail -1)
          if [ -n "$XCODE" ]; then sudo xcode-select -s "$XCODE"; fi
          xcodebuild -version
          xcodebuild -showsdks | grep -i ios || true
          xcodebuild -downloadComponent MetalToolchain 2>/dev/null || true

      - name: Build (unsigned, Release, iphoneos)
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          xcodebuild \
            -project Cutline.xcodeproj \
            -scheme Cutline \
            -configuration Release \
            -sdk iphoneos \
            -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' \
            -derivedDataPath build \
            CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO \
            CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO \
            CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" \
            build 2>&1 | tee build.log | grep -E "error:|warning:|BUILD|\*\*" | grep -v "^ld: warning" | sort -u | head -400

      - name: Show compiler errors
        if: failure()
        run: |
          echo "==== ERRORS ===="
          grep -E "error:" build.log | sort -u | head -200 || true
          echo "==== CONTEXT ===="
          grep -B2 -A6 -E "error:" build.log | head -600 || true

      - name: Package IPA
        run: |
          APP=build/Build/Products/Release-iphoneos/Cutline.app
          test -d "$APP"
          rm -rf Payload dist
          mkdir -p Payload dist
          cp -R "$APP" Payload/
          zip -qry dist/Cutline.ipa Payload
          ls -la dist
          unzip -l dist/Cutline.ipa | tail -5

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: Cutline-unsigned-ipa
          path: dist/Cutline.ipa

      - name: Commit IPA back to the branch
        if: github.event_name == 'push'
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add -f dist/Cutline.ipa
          git commit -m "Build unsigned Cutline.ipa for ${GITHUB_SHA::7} [skip ci]" || exit 0
          git push origin "HEAD:${GITHUB_REF_NAME}"
```

## `Cutline/App/CutlineApp.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI
import AVFoundation

@main
struct CutlineApp: App {
    @State private var store = ProjectStore()

    init() {
        // Playback-category session so preview audio plays even with the silent switch on.
        try? AVAudioSession.sharedInstance().setCategory(.playback, mode: .moviePlayback)
        ExportService.cleanExports()
        UIScrollView.appearance().keyboardDismissMode = .interactive
    }

    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            HomeView()
                .environment(store)
                .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
                .tint(Theme.accent)
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/AudioClip.swift`

```swift
import Foundation

enum AudioKind: String, Codable, Hashable {
    case music, voiceover, extracted

    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .music: return "music.note"
        case .voiceover: return "mic.fill"
        case .extracted: return "waveform"
        }
    }
}

struct AudioClip: Codable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var mediaID: UUID
    var name: String
    var kind: AudioKind
    var timelineStart: Double
    var sourceStart: Double
    var sourceEnd: Double
    var speed: Double = 1
    /// True when the audio comes from the reversed version of a video.
    var reversed = false
    var volume: Double = 1
    var muted = false
    var fadeIn: Double = 0
    var fadeOut: Double = 0
    var denoise = false

    var sourceDuration: Double { max(sourceEnd - sourceStart, 0.01) }
    var duration: Double { sourceDuration / speed.clamped(0.1, 8) }
    var timelineEnd: Double { timelineStart + duration }

    func contains(_ time: Double) -> Bool { time >= timelineStart && time < timelineEnd }

    func sourceTime(atLocal local: Double) -> Double { sourceStart + local * speed.clamped(0.1, 8) }
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/Basics.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import CoreImage
import CoreMedia

/// A Codable RGBA color stored in sRGB components (0...1).
struct RGBAColor: Codable, Hashable {
    var r: Double
    var g: Double
    var b: Double
    var a: Double

    init(r: Double, g: Double, b: Double, a: Double = 1) {
        self.r = r
        self.g = g
        self.b = b
        self.a = a
    }

    init(_ color: Color) {
        let ui = UIColor(color)
        var rr: CGFloat = 0, gg: CGFloat = 0, bb: CGFloat = 0, aa: CGFloat = 0
        if !ui.getRed(&rr, green: &gg, blue: &bb, alpha: &aa) {
            var white: CGFloat = 0
            ui.getWhite(&white, alpha: &aa)
            rr = white; gg = white; bb = white
        }
        r = Double(min(max(rr, 0), 1))
        g = Double(min(max(gg, 0), 1))
        b = Double(min(max(bb, 0), 1))
        a = Double(min(max(aa, 0), 1))
    }

    var uiColor: UIColor { UIColor(red: r, green: g, blue: b, alpha: a) }
    var color: Color { Color(.sRGB, red: r, green: g, blue: b, opacity: a) }
    var cgColor: CGColor { uiColor.cgColor }
    var ciColor: CIColor { CIColor(red: r, green: g, blue: b, alpha: a) }

    func withAlpha(_ alpha: Double) -> RGBAColor { RGBAColor(r: r, g: g, b: b, a: alpha) }

    static let white = RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 1, b: 1)
    static let black = RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 0, b: 0)
    static let yellow = RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 0.86, b: 0.1)
    static let green = RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 1, b: 0)
    static let blue = RGBAColor(r: 0.15, g: 0.5, b: 1)
    static let red = RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 0.25, b: 0.3)
    static let pink = RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 0.35, b: 0.65)
    static let accent = RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 0.32, b: 0.36)

    static let palette: [RGBAColor] = [
        .white, .black, .yellow, .accent, .pink,
        RGBAColor(r: 1, g: 0.55, b: 0.1),
        RGBAColor(r: 0.2, g: 0.85, b: 0.45),
        .blue,
        RGBAColor(r: 0.55, g: 0.35, b: 1),
        RGBAColor(r: 0.3, g: 0.9, b: 0.95)
    ]
}

enum MediaKind: String, Codable, Hashable {
    case video
    case image
    case audio
}

enum AspectRatio: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case portrait9x16 = "9:16"
    case square1x1 = "1:1"
    case portrait4x5 = "4:5"
    case landscape16x9 = "16:9"

    var id: String { rawValue }

    /// width / height
    var ratio: CGFloat {
        switch self {
        case .portrait9x16: return 9.0 / 16.0
        case .square1x1: return 1
        case .portrait4x5: return 4.0 / 5.0
        case .landscape16x9: return 16.0 / 9.0
        }
    }

    var title: String { rawValue }

    var subtitle: String {
        switch self {
        case .portrait9x16: return "TikTok · Reels · Shorts"
        case .square1x1: return "Square"
        case .portrait4x5: return "Portrait post"
        case .landscape16x9: return "YouTube"
        }
    }

    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .portrait9x16: return "rectangle.portrait"
        case .square1x1: return "square"
        case .portrait4x5: return "rectangle.portrait"
        case .landscape16x9: return "rectangle"
        }
    }

    /// Size where the shorter side equals `shortSide`, rounded to even pixels.
    func size(shortSide: CGFloat) -> CGSize {
        func even(_ v: CGFloat) -> CGFloat { CGFloat(Int((v / 2).rounded()) * 2) }
        if ratio >= 1 {
            return CGSize(width: even(shortSide * ratio), height: even(shortSide))
        } else {
            return CGSize(width: even(shortSide), height: even(shortSide / ratio))
        }
    }

    static func bestMatch(for size: CGSize) -> AspectRatio {
        guard size.width > 0, size.height > 0 else { return .portrait9x16 }
        let r = size.width / size.height
        return AspectRatio.allCases.min(by: { abs(log($0.ratio) - log(r)) < abs(log($1.ratio) - log(r)) }) ?? .portrait9x16
    }
}

extension Double {
    var cmTime: CMTime { CMTime(value: CMTimeValue((self * 600).rounded()), timescale: 600) }

    func clamped(_ lo: Double, _ hi: Double) -> Double { Swift.min(Swift.max(self, lo), hi) }
}

extension CGFloat {
    func clamped(_ lo: CGFloat, _ hi: CGFloat) -> CGFloat { Swift.min(Swift.max(self, lo), hi) }
}

extension CMTime {
    var secondsOrZero: Double {
        let s = CMTimeGetSeconds(self)
        return s.isFinite ? s : 0
    }
}

enum TimeFormat {
    static func short(_ seconds: Double) -> String {
        let s = max(0, seconds)
        let minutes = Int(s) / 60
        let secs = Int(s) % 60
        let tenths = Int((s - floor(s)) * 10)
        return String(format: "%d:%02d.%d", minutes, secs, tenths)
    }

    static func clock(_ seconds: Double) -> String {
        let s = Int(max(0, seconds).rounded())
        return String(format: "%d:%02d", s / 60, s % 60)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/Captions.swift`

```swift
import Foundation

struct CaptionWord: Codable, Hashable {
    var text: String
    var start: Double
    var end: Double
}

struct CaptionSegment: Codable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var start: Double
    var end: Double
    var words: [CaptionWord]

    var text: String { words.map(\.text).joined(separator: " ") }

    func contains(_ time: Double) -> Bool { time >= start && time < end }

    /// Replaces the text, spreading new words evenly across the segment.
    mutating func setText(_ newText: String) {
        let tokens = newText.split(whereSeparator: { $0.isWhitespace || $0.isNewline }).map(String.init)
        guard !tokens.isEmpty else { words = []; return }
        let span = max(end - start, 0.1)
        let totalChars = tokens.reduce(0) { $0 + max($1.count, 1) }
        var cursor = start
        words = tokens.map { token in
            let d = span * Double(max(token.count, 1)) / Double(totalChars)
            defer { cursor += d }
            return CaptionWord(text: token, start: cursor, end: cursor + d)
        }
    }

    func activeWordIndex(at time: Double) -> Int? {
        guard !words.isEmpty else { return nil }
        if let i = words.firstIndex(where: { time >= $0.start && time < $0.end }) { return i }
        return words.lastIndex(where: { $0.start <= time })
    }
}

enum CaptionStyle: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case boldCenter, karaoke, minimalBottom

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .boldCenter: return "Bold Center"
        case .karaoke: return "Karaoke"
        case .minimalBottom: return "Minimal"
        }
    }

    var defaultVerticalPosition: Double {
        switch self {
        case .boldCenter: return 0.5
        case .karaoke: return 0.72
        case .minimalBottom: return 0.86
        }
    }
}

struct CaptionTrack: Codable, Hashable {
    var style: CaptionStyle = .boldCenter
    /// 0 = top, 1 = bottom of the canvas (center of the caption block).
    var verticalPosition: Double = 0.5
    var fontScale: Double = 1
    var highlightColor: RGBAColor = .yellow
    var textColor: RGBAColor = .white
    var segments: [CaptionSegment] = []

    func segment(at time: Double) -> CaptionSegment? {
        segments.first { $0.contains(time) }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/Clip.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics

enum FitMode: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case fit, fill
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var title: String { self == .fit ? "Fit" : "Fill" }
}

/// Normalized crop rectangle in the oriented source frame (origin top-left).
struct CropRect: Codable, Hashable {
    var x: Double = 0
    var y: Double = 0
    var width: Double = 1
    var height: Double = 1

    static let full = CropRect()
    var isFull: Bool { x <= 0.0001 && y <= 0.0001 && width >= 0.9999 && height >= 0.9999 }

    /// Largest centered crop of the given aspect (w/h) for a source of `sourceSize`.
    static func centered(aspect: Double, sourceSize: CGSize) -> CropRect {
        guard sourceSize.width > 0, sourceSize.height > 0 else { return .full }
        let srcAspect = Double(sourceSize.width / sourceSize.height)
        if srcAspect > aspect {
            let w = aspect / srcAspect
            return CropRect(x: (1 - w) / 2, y: 0, width: w, height: 1)
        } else {
            let h = srcAspect / aspect
            return CropRect(x: 0, y: (1 - h) / 2, width: 1, height: h)
        }
    }
}

enum SpeedRamp: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case none, montage, hero, bullet, flashIn, flashOut

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "None"
        case .montage: return "Montage"
        case .hero: return "Hero"
        case .bullet: return "Bullet"
        case .flashIn: return "Flash In"
        case .flashOut: return "Flash Out"
        }
    }

    /// Speed multiplier at normalized source progress `u` (0...1).
    func multiplier(at u: Double) -> Double {
        switch self {
        case .none:
            return 1
        case .montage:
            return 1.25 + 0.75 * sin(u * .pi * 4)
        case .hero:
            let g = exp(-pow((u - 0.5) / 0.15, 2))
            return 2.0 - 1.7 * g
        case .bullet:
            let g = exp(-pow((u - 0.5) / 0.2, 2))
            return 1.0 - 0.85 * g
        case .flashIn:
            return 1 + 3 * pow(1 - u, 2)
        case .flashOut:
            return 1 + 3 * u * u
        }
    }
}

struct SpeedSegment: Hashable {
    var sourceStart: Double
    var sourceDuration: Double
    var speed: Double
    var duration: Double { sourceDuration / speed }
}

enum TransitionType: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case cut, fade, dissolve, slide, zoom, spin, glitch

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .cut: return "Cut"
        case .fade: return "Fade"
        case .dissolve: return "Dissolve"
        case .slide: return "Slide"
        case .zoom: return "Zoom"
        case .spin: return "Spin"
        case .glitch: return "Glitch"
        }
    }

    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .cut: return "scissors"
        case .fade: return "circle.lefthalf.filled"
        case .dissolve: return "square.on.square.dashed"
        case .slide: return "rectangle.lefthalf.inset.filled.arrow.left"
        case .zoom: return "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right"
        case .spin: return "arrow.triangle.2.circlepath"
        case .glitch: return "waveform.path.ecg"
        }
    }
}

struct TransitionSettings: Codable, Hashable {
    var type: TransitionType = .cut
    var duration: Double = 0.5
}

/// A video or photo placed on the main track or on the overlay track.
struct Clip: Codable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var mediaID: UUID
    var kind: MediaKind
    var timelineStart: Double
    var sourceStart: Double
    var sourceEnd: Double
    var speed: Double = 1
    var ramp: SpeedRamp = .none
    var reversed = false

    var volume: Double = 1
    var muted = false
    var fadeIn: Double = 0
    var fadeOut: Double = 0
    var audioDetached = false
    var denoise = false

    var crop: CropRect = .full
    var quarterTurns: Int = 0
    var flipH = false
    var flipV = false
    var fit: FitMode = .fit

    var transform = Transform2D()
    var keyframes: [Keyframe] = []
    var animationIn: MotionPreset = .none
    var animationOut: MotionPreset = .none
    var animationDuration: Double = 0.5

    var filter = FilterSettings()
    var effects = EffectSettings()
    var transitionOut = TransitionSettings()

    init(media: MediaItem, at start: Double, imageDuration: Double = 3) {
        mediaID = media.id
        kind = media.kind == .image ? .image : .video
        timelineStart = start
        sourceStart = 0
        sourceEnd = media.kind == .image ? imageDuration : max(media.duration, 0.1)
    }

    var sourceDuration: Double { max(sourceEnd - sourceStart, 0.01) }

    var isImage: Bool { kind == .image }

    func segments() -> [SpeedSegment] {
        if isImage {
            return [SpeedSegment(sourceStart: sourceStart, sourceDuration: sourceDuration, speed: 1)]
        }
        let base = speed.clamped(0.1, 8)
        if ramp == .none {
            return [SpeedSegment(sourceStart: sourceStart, sourceDuration: sourceDuration, speed: base)]
        }
        let n = 12
        let segLen = sourceDuration / Double(n)
        return (0..<n).map { i in
            let u = (Double(i) + 0.5) / Double(n)
            let s = (base * ramp.multiplier(at: u)).clamped(0.1, 8)
            return SpeedSegment(sourceStart: sourceStart + Double(i) * segLen, sourceDuration: segLen, speed: s)
        }
    }

    /// Duration on the timeline (after speed changes).
    var duration: Double {
        segments().reduce(0) { $0 + $1.duration }
    }

    var timelineEnd: Double { timelineStart + duration }

    /// Average playback speed across the clip.
    var effectiveSpeed: Double { sourceDuration / max(duration, 0.0001) }

    /// Source-media time shown at `local` seconds after the clip starts on the timeline.
    func sourceTime(atLocal local: Double) -> Double {
        if isImage { return sourceStart + local }
        var acc = 0.0
        for seg in segments() {
            let d = seg.duration
            if local <= acc + d {
                return seg.sourceStart + max(local - acc, 0) * seg.speed
            }
            acc += d
        }
        return sourceEnd
    }

    /// Timeline-local time for a source time (inverse of `sourceTime(atLocal:)`).
    func localTime(forSource source: Double) -> Double {
        if isImage { return source - sourceStart }
        var acc = 0.0
        for seg in segments() {
            if source <= seg.sourceStart + seg.sourceDuration {
                return acc + max(source - seg.sourceStart, 0) / seg.speed
            }
            acc += seg.duration
        }
        return acc
    }

    func contains(_ time: Double) -> Bool {
        time >= timelineStart && time < timelineEnd
    }

    /// Copies "look" settings (effects, filters, speed, audio and motion) onto another clip.
    func effectsPayload() -> ClipEffectsPayload {
        ClipEffectsPayload(filter: filter, chromaKey: effects.chromaKey, removeBackground: effects.removeBackground,
                           speed: speed, ramp: ramp, volume: volume, fadeIn: fadeIn, fadeOut: fadeOut,
                           animationIn: animationIn, animationOut: animationOut, animationDuration: animationDuration,
                           fit: fit, flipH: flipH, flipV: flipV, quarterTurns: quarterTurns, denoise: denoise)
    }

    mutating func apply(_ p: ClipEffectsPayload) {
        filter = p.filter
        effects.chromaKey = p.chromaKey
        effects.removeBackground = p.removeBackground
        if !isImage {
            speed = p.speed
            ramp = p.ramp
            volume = p.volume
            fadeIn = p.fadeIn
            fadeOut = p.fadeOut
            denoise = p.denoise
        }
        animationIn = p.animationIn
        animationOut = p.animationOut
        animationDuration = p.animationDuration
        fit = p.fit
        flipH = p.flipH
        flipV = p.flipV
        quarterTurns = p.quarterTurns
    }
}

struct ClipEffectsPayload: Codable, Hashable {
    var filter: FilterSettings
    var chromaKey: ChromaKeySettings
    var removeBackground: Bool
    var speed: Double
    var ramp: SpeedRamp
    var volume: Double
    var fadeIn: Double
    var fadeOut: Double
    var animationIn: MotionPreset
    var animationOut: MotionPreset
    var animationDuration: Double
    var fit: FitMode
    var flipH: Bool
    var flipV: Bool
    var quarterTurns: Int
    var denoise: Bool
}
```

```swift
import Foundation

enum FilterPreset: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case none, vivid, warm, cool, mono, noir, fade, chrome, instant, process, transfer, sepia,
         cinematic, vintage, dramatic, film, pastel, teal

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "Original"
        case .vivid: return "Vivid"
        case .warm: return "Warm"
        case .cool: return "Cool"
        case .mono: return "Mono"
        case .noir: return "Noir"
        case .fade: return "Fade"
        case .chrome: return "Chrome"
        case .instant: return "Instant"
        case .process: return "Process"
        case .transfer: return "Transfer"
        case .sepia: return "Sepia"
        case .cinematic: return "Cinematic"
        case .vintage: return "Vintage"
        case .dramatic: return "Dramatic"
        case .film: return "Film"
        case .pastel: return "Pastel"
        case .teal: return "Teal Pop"
        }
    }
}

struct Adjustments: Codable, Hashable {
    /// -1...1
    var brightness: Double = 0
    /// -1...1
    var contrast: Double = 0
    /// -1...1
    var saturation: Double = 0
    /// -1...1
    var warmth: Double = 0
    /// -1...1
    var tint: Double = 0
    /// 0...1
    var sharpen: Double = 0
    /// 0...1
    var vignette: Double = 0
    /// 0...1
    var grain: Double = 0
    /// 0...1
    var blur: Double = 0

    var isIdentity: Bool { self == Adjustments() }
}

struct FilterSettings: Codable, Hashable {
    var preset: FilterPreset = .none
    var intensity: Double = 1
    var adjustments = Adjustments()

    var isIdentity: Bool { (preset == .none || intensity <= 0.001) && adjustments.isIdentity }
}

struct ChromaKeySettings: Codable, Hashable {
    var enabled = false
    var color: RGBAColor = .green
    /// 0...1
    var tolerance: Double = 0.35
    /// 0...1
    var softness: Double = 0.1
}

/// Per-frame stabilization offsets, normalized to the oriented source frame size.
struct StabilizationData: Codable, Hashable {
    var frameRate: Double
    var sourceStart: Double
    var dx: [Float]
    var dy: [Float]

    func offset(atSource time: Double) -> (Double, Double) {
        guard !dx.isEmpty else { return (0, 0) }
        let idx = Int(((time - sourceStart) * frameRate).rounded()).clamped(0, dx.count - 1)
        return (Double(dx[idx]), Double(dy[idx]))
    }
}

struct EffectSettings: Codable, Hashable {
    var chromaKey = ChromaKeySettings()
    var removeBackground = false
    var stabilize = false
    var stabilization: StabilizationData?
}

extension Int {
    func clamped(_ lo: Int, _ hi: Int) -> Int { Swift.min(Swift.max(self, lo), hi) }
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/Project.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics

struct MediaItem: Codable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    /// File name inside the project's media folder.
    var fileName: String
    /// Lower-resolution editing proxy inside the project's cache folder.
    var proxyFileName: String?
    var originalName: String
    var kind: MediaKind
    var duration: Double
    /// Oriented (display) size.
    var width: Double
    var height: Double
    var hasAudio: Bool
    var hasVideo: Bool
    var frameRate: Double = 30
    /// Beat times in source seconds (music only).
    var beats: [Double]?

    var size: CGSize { CGSize(width: width, height: height) }
}

enum BackgroundStyle: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case black, blur, color
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .black: return "Black"
        case .blur: return "Blur"
        case .color: return "Color"
        }
    }
}

struct CanvasSettings: Codable, Hashable {
    var aspect: AspectRatio = .portrait9x16
    var background: BackgroundStyle = .blur
    var backgroundColor: RGBAColor = RGBAColor(r: 0.08, g: 0.08, b: 0.1)
    /// 0...1
    var blurAmount: Double = 0.6
}

enum TimelineSelection: Codable, Hashable {
    case clip(UUID)
    case overlayClip(UUID)
    case overlay(UUID)
    case text(UUID)
    case audio(UUID)
    case caption(UUID)

    var id: UUID {
        switch self {
        case .clip(let id), .overlayClip(let id), .overlay(let id), .text(let id), .audio(let id), .caption(let id):
            return id
        }
    }
}

struct EditorState: Codable, Hashable {
    var playhead: Double = 0
    var pixelsPerSecond: Double = 60
    var verticalOffset: Double = 0
    var selection: TimelineSelection?
}

struct Project: Codable, Identifiable {
    var schemaVersion = 1
    var id = UUID()
    var name: String
    var createdAt = Date()
    var modifiedAt = Date()
    var canvas = CanvasSettings()
    var magnetic = true
    var media: [MediaItem] = []
    var clips: [Clip] = []
    var overlayClips: [Clip] = []
    var overlays: [OverlayItem] = []
    var texts: [TextItem] = []
    var audioClips: [AudioClip] = []
    var captions = CaptionTrack()
    var globalFilter = FilterSettings()
    var editorState = EditorState()

    init(name: String, aspect: AspectRatio) {
        self.name = name
        canvas.aspect = aspect
    }

    var duration: Double {
        var end = 0.0
        for c in clips { end = max(end, c.timelineEnd) }
        for c in overlayClips { end = max(end, c.timelineEnd) }
        for o in overlays { end = max(end, o.end) }
        for t in texts { end = max(end, t.end) }
        for a in audioClips { end = max(end, a.timelineEnd) }
        for s in captions.segments { end = max(end, s.end) }
        return end
    }

    var isEmpty: Bool {
        clips.isEmpty && overlayClips.isEmpty && overlays.isEmpty && texts.isEmpty && audioClips.isEmpty
    }

    func mediaItem(_ id: UUID?) -> MediaItem? {
        guard let id else { return nil }
        return media.first { $0.id == id }
    }

    func clip(_ id: UUID) -> Clip? {
        clips.first { $0.id == id } ?? overlayClips.first { $0.id == id }
    }

    /// Re-packs the main track so clips sit back to back from zero.
    mutating func packMainTrack() {
        clips.sort { $0.timelineStart < $1.timelineStart }
        var cursor = 0.0
        for i in clips.indices {
            clips[i].timelineStart = cursor
            cursor += clips[i].duration
        }
    }

    /// Keeps clips on the main track sorted and non-overlapping (pushes later clips right).
    mutating func resolveMainTrackOverlaps() {
        clips.sort { $0.timelineStart < $1.timelineStart }
        var cursor = 0.0
        for i in clips.indices {
            if clips[i].timelineStart < cursor { clips[i].timelineStart = cursor }
            clips[i].timelineStart = max(clips[i].timelineStart, 0)
            cursor = clips[i].timelineEnd
        }
    }

    mutating func normalizeMainTrack() {
        if magnetic { packMainTrack() } else { resolveMainTrackOverlaps() }
    }

    /// Beat markers converted to timeline time across all music clips.
    var timelineBeats: [Double] {
        var result: [Double] = []
        for a in audioClips {
            guard let beats = mediaItem(a.mediaID)?.beats else { continue }
            for b in beats where b >= a.sourceStart && b <= a.sourceEnd {
                result.append(a.timelineStart + (b - a.sourceStart) / a.speed.clamped(0.1, 8))
            }
        }
        return result.sorted()
    }

    /// Clamps transition durations so neighbouring transitions never overlap.
    func clampedTransitionDuration(after index: Int) -> Double {
        guard index >= 0, index + 1 < clips.count else { return 0 }
        let a = clips[index], b = clips[index + 1]
        guard a.transitionOut.type != .cut, abs(a.timelineEnd - b.timelineStart) < 0.02 else { return 0 }
        return min(a.transitionOut.duration, a.duration / 2, b.duration / 2, 2.0)
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit

enum TextAlign: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case left, center, right
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .left: return "text.alignleft"
        case .center: return "text.aligncenter"
        case .right: return "text.alignright"
        }
    }
    var nsAlignment: NSTextAlignment {
        switch self {
        case .left: return .left
        case .center: return .center
        case .right: return .right
        }
    }
}

enum TextAnimation: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case none, fade, pop, slide, typewriter
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "None"
        case .fade: return "Fade"
        case .pop: return "Pop"
        case .slide: return "Slide"
        case .typewriter: return "Typewriter"
        }
    }
    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "circle.slash"
        case .fade: return "circle.lefthalf.filled"
        case .pop: return "sparkle"
        case .slide: return "arrow.up"
        case .typewriter: return "keyboard"
        }
    }
}

struct TextItem: Codable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var text: String = "Your text"
    var start: Double
    var duration: Double = 3

    /// "System" uses the system font; otherwise a font family name from `UIFont.familyNames`.
    var fontFamily: String = "System"
    /// Point size relative to a 1080-pixel-wide canvas.
    var fontSize: Double = 72
    var bold = true
    var italic = false
    var color: RGBAColor = .white
    var outlineWidth: Double = 0
    var outlineColor: RGBAColor = .black
    var shadow = true
    var shadowColor: RGBAColor = RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0.6)
    var backgroundBox = false
    var boxColor: RGBAColor = RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0.6)
    var alignment: TextAlign = .center

    var animationIn: TextAnimation = .fade
    var animationOut: TextAnimation = .fade
    var animationDuration: Double = 0.4

    var transform = Transform2D()
    var keyframes: [Keyframe] = []

    var end: Double { start + duration }

    func contains(_ time: Double) -> Bool { time >= start && time < end }

    /// Fingerprint of everything that affects the rendered glyph image.
    var styleSignature: String {
        "\(text)|\(fontFamily)|\(fontSize)|\(bold)|\(italic)|\(color)|\(outlineWidth)|\(outlineColor)|\(shadow)|\(shadowColor)|\(backgroundBox)|\(boxColor)|\(alignment.rawValue)"
    }
}

enum FontResolver {
    static func font(family: String, size: CGFloat, bold: Bool, italic: Bool) -> UIFont {
        var base: UIFont
        if family == "System" || family.isEmpty {
            base = UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: size, weight: bold ? .heavy : .regular)
        } else {
            let descriptor = UIFontDescriptor(fontAttributes: [.family: family])
            base = UIFont(descriptor: descriptor, size: size)
            if bold, let d = base.fontDescriptor.withSymbolicTraits(.traitBold) {
                base = UIFont(descriptor: d, size: size)
            }
        }
        if italic {
            var traits = base.fontDescriptor.symbolicTraits
            traits.insert(.traitItalic)
            if let d = base.fontDescriptor.withSymbolicTraits(traits) {
                base = UIFont(descriptor: d, size: size)
            }
        }
        return base
    }

    static let families: [String] = ["System"] + UIFont.familyNames.sorted()
}
```

## `Cutline/Models/Transform.swift`

```swift
import Foundation

/// Placement of a visual layer on the canvas.
/// `x`/`y` are offsets from the canvas center as a fraction of canvas width/height (y grows downward).
/// `rotation` is in degrees (clockwise), `opacity` 0...1.
struct Transform2D: Codable, Hashable {
    var x: Double = 0
    var y: Double = 0
    var scale: Double = 1
    var rotation: Double = 0
    var opacity: Double = 1

    static let identity = Transform2D()

    static func lerp(_ a: Transform2D, _ b: Transform2D, _ t: Double) -> Transform2D {
        Transform2D(
            x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t,
            y: a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t,
            scale: a.scale + (b.scale - a.scale) * t,
            rotation: a.rotation + (b.rotation - a.rotation) * t,
            opacity: a.opacity + (b.opacity - a.opacity) * t
        )
    }
}

enum Easing: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case linear, easeIn, easeOut, easeInOut, smooth, back

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .linear: return "Linear"
        case .easeIn: return "Ease In"
        case .easeOut: return "Ease Out"
        case .easeInOut: return "Ease In-Out"
        case .smooth: return "Smooth"
        case .back: return "Overshoot"
        }
    }

    func apply(_ t: Double) -> Double {
        let x = t.clamped(0, 1)
        switch self {
        case .linear: return x
        case .easeIn: return x * x * x
        case .easeOut: let p = 1 - x; return 1 - p * p * p
        case .easeInOut: return x < 0.5 ? 4 * x * x * x : 1 - pow(-2 * x + 2, 3) / 2
        case .smooth: return x * x * x * (x * (x * 6 - 15) + 10)
        case .back:
            let c1 = 1.70158
            let c3 = c1 + 1
            return 1 + c3 * pow(x - 1, 3) + c1 * pow(x - 1, 2)
        }
    }
}

struct Keyframe: Codable, Hashable, Identifiable {
    var id = UUID()
    /// Seconds relative to the item's start on the timeline.
    var time: Double
    var transform: Transform2D
    /// Easing used from this keyframe to the next one.
    var easing: Easing = .easeInOut
}

enum KeyframeMath {
    static func transform(base: Transform2D, keyframes: [Keyframe], at time: Double) -> Transform2D {
        guard !keyframes.isEmpty else { return base }
        let sorted = keyframes.sorted { $0.time < $1.time }
        if time <= sorted[0].time { return sorted[0].transform }
        if let last = sorted.last, time >= last.time { return last.transform }
        for i in 0..<(sorted.count - 1) {
            let a = sorted[i], b = sorted[i + 1]
            if time >= a.time && time <= b.time {
                let span = max(b.time - a.time, 0.0001)
                let t = a.easing.apply((time - a.time) / span)
                return Transform2D.lerp(a.transform, b.transform, t)
            }
        }
        return base
    }

    static func index(near time: Double, in keyframes: [Keyframe], tolerance: Double = 1.0 / 30.0) -> Int? {
        keyframes.firstIndex { abs($0.time - time) <= tolerance }
    }

    /// Inserts or updates a keyframe at `time`.
    static func upsert(_ keyframes: inout [Keyframe], time: Double, transform: Transform2D) {
        if let i = index(near: time, in: keyframes) {
            keyframes[i].transform = transform
        } else {
            keyframes.append(Keyframe(time: time, transform: transform))
            keyframes.sort { $0.time < $1.time }
        }
    }

    /// Splits keyframes at `time`, returning (left, right) where right is re-based to start at 0.
    static func split(_ keyframes: [Keyframe], base: Transform2D, at time: Double) -> ([Keyframe], [Keyframe]) {
        guard !keyframes.isEmpty else { return ([], []) }
        let boundary = transform(base: base, keyframes: keyframes, at: time)
        var left = keyframes.filter { $0.time < time }
        var right = keyframes.filter { $0.time > time }.map { k -> Keyframe in
            var c = k
            c.id = UUID()
            c.time -= time
            return c
        }
        left.append(Keyframe(time: time, transform: boundary))
        right.insert(Keyframe(time: 0, transform: boundary), at: 0)
        return (left, right)
    }
}

/// Entrance / exit motion presets for clips, overlays and text.
enum MotionPreset: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case none, fade, zoomIn, zoomOut, slideLeft, slideRight, slideUp, slideDown, spin

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "None"
        case .fade: return "Fade"
        case .zoomIn: return "Zoom In"
        case .zoomOut: return "Zoom Out"
        case .slideLeft: return "Slide Left"
        case .slideRight: return "Slide Right"
        case .slideUp: return "Slide Up"
        case .slideDown: return "Slide Down"
        case .spin: return "Spin"
        }
    }

    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "circle.slash"
        case .fade: return "circle.lefthalf.filled"
        case .zoomIn: return "plus.magnifyingglass"
        case .zoomOut: return "minus.magnifyingglass"
        case .slideLeft: return "arrow.left"
        case .slideRight: return "arrow.right"
        case .slideUp: return "arrow.up"
        case .slideDown: return "arrow.down"
        case .spin: return "arrow.triangle.2.circlepath"
        }
    }
}

enum MotionMath {
    /// Applies in/out motion to a transform at `local` seconds into an item of `duration`.
    static func apply(inPreset: MotionPreset, outPreset: MotionPreset, animationDuration: Double,
                      itemDuration: Double, local: Double, to base: Transform2D) -> Transform2D {
        var t = base
        let maxAnim = (inPreset != .none && outPreset != .none) ? itemDuration / 2 : itemDuration
        let ad = min(max(animationDuration, 0.05), max(maxAnim, 0.05))
        if inPreset != .none && local < ad {
            let p = Easing.easeOut.apply(local / ad)
            t = adjust(inPreset, progress: p, entering: true, t)
        }
        let remaining = itemDuration - local
        if outPreset != .none && remaining < ad {
            let p = Easing.easeIn.apply(max(remaining, 0) / ad)
            t = adjust(outPreset, progress: p, entering: false, t)
        }
        return t
    }

    /// `progress` 0 = fully hidden, 1 = fully visible.
    static func adjust(_ preset: MotionPreset, progress p: Double, entering: Bool, _ input: Transform2D) -> Transform2D {
        var t = input
        let dir: Double = entering ? 1 : -1
        switch preset {
        case .none:
            break
        case .fade:
            t.opacity *= p
        case .zoomIn:
            t.scale *= 0.5 + 0.5 * p
            t.opacity *= p
        case .zoomOut:
            t.scale *= 1.5 - 0.5 * p
            t.opacity *= p
        case .slideLeft:
            t.x += (1 - p) * dir
        case .slideRight:
            t.x -= (1 - p) * dir
        case .slideUp:
            t.y += (1 - p) * dir
        case .slideDown:
            t.y -= (1 - p) * dir
        case .spin:
            t.rotation += (1 - p) * 360 * -dir
            t.scale *= max(p, 0.01)
        }
        return t
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/CompositionBuilder.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import CoreImage

struct BuildOptions {
    var isExport: Bool
    var renderSize: CGSize
    var frameRate: Int32
    var includeMusic = true

    static func preview(for project: Project) -> BuildOptions {
        BuildOptions(isExport: false, renderSize: project.canvas.aspect.size(shortSide: 720), frameRate: 30)
    }
}

struct BuiltComposition {
    let composition: AVMutableComposition
    let videoComposition: AVMutableVideoComposition
    let audioMix: AVMutableAudioMix
    let renderBox: RenderStateBox
    let duration: Double
    let missingMediaCount: Int
}

/// Tracks the end time of each composition track so clips can be packed onto free tracks.
private final class TrackPool {
    let composition: AVMutableComposition
    let mediaType: AVMediaType
    private(set) var tracks: [AVMutableCompositionTrack] = []

    init(composition: AVMutableComposition, mediaType: AVMediaType) {
        self.composition = composition
        self.mediaType = mediaType
    }

    static func end(of track: AVMutableCompositionTrack) -> CMTime {
        track.segments.last?.timeMapping.target.end ?? .zero
    }

    /// A track whose content ends at or before `start`, creating one if necessary.
    func track(availableAt start: CMTime) -> AVMutableCompositionTrack? {
        if let t = tracks.first(where: { Self.end(of: $0) <= start }) { return t }
        guard let t = composition.addMutableTrack(withMediaType: mediaType, preferredTrackID: kCMPersistentTrackID_Invalid) else {
            return nil
        }
        tracks.append(t)
        return t
    }

    func pad(_ track: AVMutableCompositionTrack, to time: CMTime) {
        let end = Self.end(of: track)
        if time > end {
            track.insertEmptyTimeRange(CMTimeRange(start: end, end: time))
        }
    }
}

enum CompositionBuilder {
    static func build(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, options: BuildOptions,
                      renderBox existingBox: RenderStateBox? = nil) async throws -> BuiltComposition {
        let composition = AVMutableComposition()
        let resolver = MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: project.media, preferProxy: !options.isExport)
        let totalSeconds = max(project.duration, 0.1)
        let total = totalSeconds.cmTime
        var missing = 0

        // Timing backbone: black video stretched over the whole timeline.
        let blankURL = try await BlankVideoProvider.shared.url()
        let blank = try await AssetCache.shared.info(for: blankURL)
        guard let blankTrack = blank.video,
              let baseTrack = composition.addMutableTrack(withMediaType: .video, preferredTrackID: kCMPersistentTrackID_Invalid) else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't prepare the timeline.")
        }
        let blankRange = CMTimeRange(start: .zero, duration: blank.videoRange.duration)
        try baseTrack.insertTimeRange(blankRange, of: blankTrack, at: .zero)
        baseTrack.scaleTimeRange(CMTimeRange(start: .zero, duration: blankRange.duration), toDuration: total)

        let videoPool = TrackPool(composition: composition, mediaType: .video)
        let overlayPool = TrackPool(composition: composition, mediaType: .video)
        let audioPool = TrackPool(composition: composition, mediaType: .audio)
        var audioParams: [CMPersistentTrackID: AVMutableAudioMixInputParameters] = [:]
        var layers: [LayerSpec] = []

        func params(for track: AVMutableCompositionTrack) -> AVMutableAudioMixInputParameters {
            if let p = audioParams[track.trackID] { return p }
            let p = AVMutableAudioMixInputParameters(track: track)
            p.audioTimePitchAlgorithm = .spectral
            audioParams[track.trackID] = p
            return p
        }

        func applyVolume(_ p: AVMutableAudioMixInputParameters, start: Double, end: Double, volume: Double, fadeIn: Double, fadeOut: Double) {
            let v = Float(volume.clamped(0, 1))
            let length = max(end - start, 0)
            let fi = min(fadeIn, length / 2)
            let fo = min(fadeOut, length / 2)
            if fi > 0.01 {
                p.setVolumeRamp(fromStartVolume: 0, toEndVolume: v, timeRange: CMTimeRange(start: start.cmTime, duration: fi.cmTime))
            } else {
                p.setVolume(v, at: start.cmTime)
            }
            if fo > 0.01 {
                p.setVolumeRamp(fromStartVolume: v, toEndVolume: 0, timeRange: CMTimeRange(start: (end - fo).cmTime, duration: fo.cmTime))
            }
        }

        /// Inserts speed segments of `source` onto `track` starting at `cursor`; returns the new cursor.
        func insertSegments(_ segments: [SpeedSegment], of source: AVAssetTrack, available: CMTimeRange,
                            into track: AVMutableCompositionTrack, at start: CMTime) throws -> CMTime {
            var cursor = start
            for seg in segments {
                var srcStart = seg.sourceStart.cmTime
                var srcDuration = seg.sourceDuration.cmTime
                if srcStart < available.start { srcStart = available.start }
                if srcStart + srcDuration > available.end { srcDuration = available.end - srcStart }
                guard srcDuration > .zero else { continue }
                try track.insertTimeRange(CMTimeRange(start: srcStart, duration: srcDuration), of: source, at: cursor)
                if abs(seg.speed - 1) > 0.0001 {
                    let scaled = CMTimeMultiplyByFloat64(srcDuration, multiplier: 1 / seg.speed)
                    track.scaleTimeRange(CMTimeRange(start: cursor, duration: srcDuration), toDuration: scaled)
                    cursor = cursor + scaled
                } else {
                    cursor = cursor + srcDuration
                }
            }
            return cursor
        }

        /// Inserts a transition handle: real footage if available, otherwise a held frame.
        func insertHandle(length: Double, sourceTime: Double, before: Bool, speed: Double, info: AssetCache.Info,
                          source: AVAssetTrack, into track: AVMutableCompositionTrack, at cursor: CMTime) throws -> CMTime {
            guard length > 0.001 else { return cursor }
            let target = length.cmTime
            let need = length * speed
            let available = info.videoRange
            let srcRange: CMTimeRange
            if before, sourceTime - need >= available.start.secondsOrZero {
                srcRange = CMTimeRange(start: (sourceTime - need).cmTime, duration: need.cmTime)
            } else if !before, sourceTime + need <= available.end.secondsOrZero {
                srcRange = CMTimeRange(start: sourceTime.cmTime, duration: need.cmTime)
            } else {
                let frame = info.frameDuration
                var frameStart = before ? sourceTime.cmTime : sourceTime.cmTime - frame
                if frameStart < available.start { frameStart = available.start }
                if frameStart + frame > available.end { frameStart = available.end - frame }
                srcRange = CMTimeRange(start: frameStart, duration: frame)
            }
            guard srcRange.duration > .zero else { return cursor }
            try track.insertTimeRange(srcRange, of: source, at: cursor)
            track.scaleTimeRange(CMTimeRange(start: cursor, duration: srcRange.duration), toDuration: target)
            return cursor + target
        }

        // MARK: Main track
        let clips = project.clips
        for (index, clip) in clips.enumerated() {
            let dIn = index > 0 ? project.clampedTransitionDuration(after: index - 1) : 0
            let dOut = project.clampedTransitionDuration(after: index)
            let hIn = dIn / 2, hOut = dOut / 2
            let coreStart = clip.timelineStart, coreEnd = clip.timelineEnd
            var spec = LayerSpec(clipID: clip.id, isOverlay: false, order: index, source: .missing, orientation: .up,
                                 visibleStart: coreStart - hIn, visibleEnd: coreEnd + hOut,
                                 coreStart: coreStart, coreEnd: coreEnd,
                                 transitionIn: dIn > 0 ? TransitionWindow(type: clips[index - 1].transitionOut.type, start: coreStart - hIn, end: coreStart + hIn) : nil,
                                 transitionOut: dOut > 0 ? TransitionWindow(type: clip.transitionOut.type, start: coreEnd - hOut, end: coreEnd + hOut) : nil)

            if clip.isImage {
                if let url = resolver.imageURL(for: clip.mediaID) {
                    spec.source = .image(url)
                } else {
                    missing += 1
                }
            } else if let url = resolver.videoURL(for: clip),
                      let info = try? await AssetCache.shared.info(for: url),
                      let source = info.video {
                let start = (coreStart - hIn).cmTime
                if let track = videoPool.track(availableAt: start) {
                    videoPool.pad(track, to: start)
                    let segs = clip.segments()
                    var cursor = start
                    cursor = try insertHandle(length: hIn, sourceTime: clip.sourceStart, before: true,
                                              speed: segs.first?.speed ?? 1, info: info, source: source, into: track, at: cursor)
                    cursor = try insertSegments(segs, of: source, available: info.videoRange, into: track, at: cursor)
                    _ = try insertHandle(length: hOut, sourceTime: clip.sourceEnd, before: false,
                                         speed: segs.last?.speed ?? 1, info: info, source: source, into: track, at: cursor)
                    spec.source = .track(track.trackID)
                    spec.orientation = info.orientation
                }
            } else {
                missing += 1
            }
            layers.append(spec)

            // Clip audio
            if !clip.isImage, !clip.muted, !clip.audioDetached, clip.volume > 0.001,
               let audioURL = resolver.audioURL(mediaID: clip.mediaID, reversed: clip.reversed, denoise: clip.denoise),
               let info = try? await AssetCache.shared.info(for: audioURL),
               let source = info.audio {
                let start = coreStart.cmTime
                if let track = audioPool.track(availableAt: start) {
                    audioPool.pad(track, to: start)
                    _ = try insertSegments(clip.segments(), of: source, available: info.audioRange, into: track, at: start)
                    applyVolume(params(for: track), start: coreStart, end: coreEnd, volume: clip.volume,
                                fadeIn: clip.fadeIn, fadeOut: clip.fadeOut)
                }
            }
        }

        // MARK: Overlay clips (picture in picture)
        for (index, clip) in project.overlayClips.enumerated() {
            var spec = LayerSpec(clipID: clip.id, isOverlay: true, order: index, source: .missing, orientation: .up,
                                 visibleStart: clip.timelineStart, visibleEnd: clip.timelineEnd,
                                 coreStart: clip.timelineStart, coreEnd: clip.timelineEnd,
                                 transitionIn: nil, transitionOut: nil)
            if clip.isImage {
                if let url = resolver.imageURL(for: clip.mediaID) { spec.source = .image(url) } else { missing += 1 }
            } else if let url = resolver.videoURL(for: clip),
                      let info = try? await AssetCache.shared.info(for: url),
                      let source = info.video {
                let start = clip.timelineStart.cmTime
                if let track = overlayPool.track(availableAt: start) {
                    overlayPool.pad(track, to: start)
                    _ = try insertSegments(clip.segments(), of: source, available: info.videoRange, into: track, at: start)
                    spec.source = .track(track.trackID)
                    spec.orientation = info.orientation
                }
            } else {
                missing += 1
            }
            layers.append(spec)

            if !clip.isImage, !clip.muted, !clip.audioDetached, clip.volume > 0.001,
               let audioURL = resolver.audioURL(mediaID: clip.mediaID, reversed: clip.reversed, denoise: clip.denoise),
               let info = try? await AssetCache.shared.info(for: audioURL),
               let source = info.audio {
                let start = clip.timelineStart.cmTime
                if let track = audioPool.track(availableAt: start) {
                    audioPool.pad(track, to: start)
                    _ = try insertSegments(clip.segments(), of: source, available: info.audioRange, into: track, at: start)
                    applyVolume(params(for: track), start: clip.timelineStart, end: clip.timelineEnd, volume: clip.volume,
                                fadeIn: clip.fadeIn, fadeOut: clip.fadeOut)
                }
            }
        }

        // MARK: Audio clips (music, voiceover, detached audio)
        for audio in project.audioClips where !audio.muted && audio.volume > 0.001 {
            if !options.includeMusic && audio.kind == .music { continue }
            guard let url = resolver.audioURL(mediaID: audio.mediaID, reversed: audio.reversed, denoise: audio.denoise),
                  let info = try? await AssetCache.shared.info(for: url),
                  let source = info.audio else {
                missing += 1
                continue
            }
            let start = audio.timelineStart.cmTime
            guard let track = audioPool.track(availableAt: start) else { continue }
            audioPool.pad(track, to: start)
            let seg = SpeedSegment(sourceStart: audio.sourceStart, sourceDuration: audio.sourceDuration, speed: audio.speed.clamped(0.1, 8))
            _ = try insertSegments([seg], of: source, available: info.audioRange, into: track, at: start)
            applyVolume(params(for: track), start: audio.timelineStart, end: audio.timelineEnd, volume: audio.volume,
                        fadeIn: audio.fadeIn, fadeOut: audio.fadeOut)
        }

        // MARK: Video composition
        let renderBox = existingBox ?? RenderStateBox(project: project, paths: paths, isExport: options.isExport)
        if existingBox != nil { renderBox.update(project) }

        let compDuration = composition.duration
        var ticks = Set<Int64>([0])
        let scale: Double = 600
        let endTicks = Int64((compDuration.secondsOrZero * scale).rounded(.down))
        for l in layers {
            for v in [l.visibleStart, l.visibleEnd] {
                let tk = Int64((v * scale).rounded())
                if tk > 0 && tk < endTicks { ticks.insert(tk) }
            }
        }
        let sortedTicks = ticks.sorted()
        var instructions: [CompositionInstruction] = []
        for (i, startTick) in sortedTicks.enumerated() {
            let start = CMTime(value: startTick, timescale: 600)
            let end = i + 1 < sortedTicks.count ? CMTime(value: sortedTicks[i + 1], timescale: 600) : compDuration
            guard end > start else { continue }
            let mid = (start.secondsOrZero + end.secondsOrZero) / 2
            let active = layers.filter { $0.visibleStart <= mid && mid < $0.visibleEnd }
            var trackIDs: [CMPersistentTrackID] = [baseTrack.trackID]
            for l in active {
                if case .track(let id) = l.source, !trackIDs.contains(id) { trackIDs.append(id) }
            }
            instructions.append(CompositionInstruction(timeRange: CMTimeRange(start: start, end: end), layers: active,
                                                       trackIDs: trackIDs, renderBox: renderBox))
        }

        let videoComposition = AVMutableVideoComposition()
        videoComposition.customVideoCompositorClass = CutlineCompositor.self
        videoComposition.renderSize = options.renderSize
        videoComposition.frameDuration = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: options.frameRate)
        videoComposition.instructions = instructions

        let audioMix = AVMutableAudioMix()
        audioMix.inputParameters = Array(audioParams.values)

        return BuiltComposition(composition: composition, videoComposition: videoComposition, audioMix: audioMix,
                                renderBox: renderBox, duration: compDuration.secondsOrZero, missingMediaCount: missing)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/CompositionInstruction.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import CoreImage

struct TransitionWindow: Hashable {
    var type: TransitionType
    var start: Double
    var end: Double

    func progress(at t: Double) -> Double {
        ((t - start) / max(end - start, 0.0001)).clamped(0, 1)
    }

    func contains(_ t: Double) -> Bool { t >= start && t < end }
}

/// Describes one visual clip layer inside the video composition.
struct LayerSpec: Hashable {
    enum Source: Hashable {
        case track(CMPersistentTrackID)
        case image(URL)
        case missing
    }

    var clipID: UUID
    var isOverlay: Bool
    var order: Int
    var source: Source
    var orientation: CGImagePropertyOrientation
    /// Range the layer is present (including transition handles).
    var visibleStart: Double
    var visibleEnd: Double
    /// The clip's own range on the timeline.
    var coreStart: Double
    var coreEnd: Double
    var transitionIn: TransitionWindow?
    var transitionOut: TransitionWindow?

    func isVisible(at t: Double) -> Bool { t >= visibleStart && t < visibleEnd }
}

/// Immutable view of the project used by the renderer for one or more frames.
struct RenderSnapshot {
    let project: Project
    let clips: [UUID: Clip]
    let resolver: MediaResolver
    let isExport: Bool

    init(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, isExport: Bool) {
        self.project = project
        var dict: [UUID: Clip] = [:]
        for c in project.clips { dict[c.id] = c }
        for c in project.overlayClips { dict[c.id] = c }
        clips = dict
        resolver = MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: project.media, preferProxy: !isExport)
        self.isExport = isExport
    }
}

/// Thread-safe holder of the latest snapshot, shared between the editor and the compositor.
/// Visual-only edits (filters, text, transforms) update this without rebuilding the composition.
final class RenderStateBox: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var _snapshot: RenderSnapshot
    let paths: ProjectPaths
    let isExport: Bool

    init(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, isExport: Bool) {
        self.paths = paths
        self.isExport = isExport
        _snapshot = RenderSnapshot(project: project, paths: paths, isExport: isExport)
    }

    var snapshot: RenderSnapshot {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return _snapshot
    }

    func update(_ project: Project) {
        let s = RenderSnapshot(project: project, paths: paths, isExport: isExport)
        lock.lock()
        _snapshot = s
        lock.unlock()
    }
}

final class CompositionInstruction: NSObject, AVVideoCompositionInstructionProtocol {
    let timeRange: CMTimeRange
    let enablePostProcessing = false
    let containsTweening = true
    let requiredSourceTrackIDs: [NSValue]?
    let passthroughTrackID: CMPersistentTrackID = kCMPersistentTrackID_Invalid

    let layers: [LayerSpec]
    let renderBox: RenderStateBox

    init(timeRange: CMTimeRange, layers: [LayerSpec], trackIDs: [CMPersistentTrackID], renderBox: RenderStateBox) {
        self.timeRange = timeRange
        self.layers = layers
        self.renderBox = renderBox
        self.requiredSourceTrackIDs = trackIDs.map { NSNumber(value: $0) }
        super.init()
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/CutlineCompositor.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import CoreImage

/// Custom AVVideoCompositing renderer: applies effects, transitions, keyframes, text and captions.
final class CutlineCompositor: NSObject, AVVideoCompositing {
    private let renderQueue = DispatchQueue(label: "app.cutline.compositor", qos: .userInteractive)
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var generation = 0
    private let renderer = FrameRenderer()

    var sourcePixelBufferAttributes: [String: any Sendable]? {
        [
            kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: [kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA],
            kCVPixelBufferMetalCompatibilityKey as String: true
        ]
    }

    var requiredPixelBufferAttributesForRenderContext: [String: any Sendable] {
        [
            kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: [kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA],
            kCVPixelBufferMetalCompatibilityKey as String: true
        ]
    }

    override required init() {
        super.init()
    }

    func renderContextChanged(_ newRenderContext: AVVideoCompositionRenderContext) {}

    private var currentGeneration: Int {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return generation
    }

    func startRequest(_ request: AVAsynchronousVideoCompositionRequest) {
        let requestGeneration = currentGeneration
        renderQueue.async { [weak self] in
            guard let self else {
                request.finishCancelledRequest()
                return
            }
            if requestGeneration != self.currentGeneration {
                request.finishCancelledRequest()
                return
            }
            autoreleasepool {
                guard let instruction = request.videoCompositionInstruction as? CompositionInstruction,
                      let output = request.renderContext.newPixelBuffer() else {
                    request.finish(with: AppError.processingFailed("Missing render instruction."))
                    return
                }
                let size = request.renderContext.size
                let time = request.compositionTime.secondsOrZero
                let image = self.renderer.render(time: time, instruction: instruction, canvasSize: size) { trackID in
                    request.sourceFrame(byTrackID: trackID)
                }
                FrameRenderer.context.render(image, to: output,
                                             bounds: CGRect(origin: .zero, size: size),
                                             colorSpace: FrameRenderer.colorSpace)
                request.finish(withComposedVideoFrame: output)
            }
        }
    }

    func cancelAllPendingVideoCompositionRequests() {
        lock.lock()
        generation += 1
        lock.unlock()
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/FilterEngine.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import CoreImage
import CoreImage.CIFilterBuiltins

/// Applies preset looks and manual adjustments with Core Image.
enum FilterEngine {
    static func apply(_ settings: FilterSettings, to input: CIImage, time: Double) -> CIImage {
        guard !settings.isIdentity else { return input }
        let extent = input.extent
        guard extent.width > 1, extent.height > 1, extent.width.isFinite, extent.height.isFinite else { return input }
        var image = input

        if settings.preset != .none && settings.intensity > 0.001 {
            let looked = preset(settings.preset, image: image, time: time).cropped(to: extent)
            if settings.intensity >= 0.999 {
                image = looked
            } else {
                let dissolve = CIFilter.dissolveTransition()
                dissolve.inputImage = image
                dissolve.targetImage = looked
                dissolve.time = Float(settings.intensity)
                image = dissolve.outputImage?.cropped(to: extent) ?? looked
            }
        }
        image = adjust(settings.adjustments, image: image, time: time)
        return image.cropped(to: extent)
    }

    static func adjust(_ a: Adjustments, image input: CIImage, time: Double) -> CIImage {
        guard !a.isIdentity else { return input }
        let extent = input.extent
        var image = input
        let minSide = min(extent.width, extent.height)

        if a.brightness != 0 || a.contrast != 0 || a.saturation != 0 {
            let f = CIFilter.colorControls()
            f.inputImage = image
            f.brightness = Float(a.brightness * 0.3)
            f.contrast = Float(1 + a.contrast * 0.5)
            f.saturation = Float(1 + a.saturation)
            image = f.outputImage ?? image
        }
        if a.warmth != 0 || a.tint != 0 {
            let f = CIFilter.temperatureAndTint()
            f.inputImage = image
            f.neutral = CIVector(x: CGFloat(6500 + a.warmth * 3000), y: CGFloat(a.tint * 60))
            f.targetNeutral = CIVector(x: 6500, y: 0)
            image = f.outputImage ?? image
        }
        if a.sharpen > 0 {
            let f = CIFilter.sharpenLuminance()
            f.inputImage = image
            f.sharpness = Float(a.sharpen * 1.5)
            f.radius = Float(max(1, minSide / 600))
            image = f.outputImage ?? image
        }
        if a.blur > 0 {
            let f = CIFilter.gaussianBlur()
            f.inputImage = image.clampedToExtent()
            f.radius = Float(a.blur * 0.03 * minSide)
            image = (f.outputImage ?? image).cropped(to: extent)
        }
        if a.vignette > 0 {
            let f = CIFilter.vignetteEffect()
            f.inputImage = image
            f.center = CGPoint(x: extent.midX, y: extent.midY)
            f.radius = Float(max(extent.width, extent.height) * 0.55)
            f.intensity = Float(a.vignette)
            f.falloff = 0.6
            image = f.outputImage ?? image
        }
        if a.grain > 0 {
            image = addGrain(image, amount: a.grain, time: time)
        }
        return image.cropped(to: extent)
    }

    static func addGrain(_ image: CIImage, amount: Double, time: Double) -> CIImage {
        let extent = image.extent
        guard let noiseBase = CIFilter.randomGenerator().outputImage else { return image }
        let frame = Int(time * 24)
        let offset = CGFloat((frame * 97) % 997)
        let grainScale = max(1, min(extent.width, extent.height) / 900)
        let noise = noiseBase
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: offset, y: offset * 0.7))
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: grainScale, y: grainScale))
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: extent.minX, y: extent.minY))
            .cropped(to: extent)
        let mono = CIFilter.colorControls()
        mono.inputImage = noise
        mono.saturation = 0
        mono.contrast = 1.2
        guard let monoNoise = mono.outputImage else { return image }
        let blend = CIFilter.overlayBlendMode()
        blend.inputImage = monoNoise
        blend.backgroundImage = image
        guard let grained = blend.outputImage?.cropped(to: extent) else { return image }
        let dissolve = CIFilter.dissolveTransition()
        dissolve.inputImage = image
        dissolve.targetImage = grained
        dissolve.time = Float(amount * 0.45)
        return dissolve.outputImage?.cropped(to: extent) ?? image
    }

    static func preset(_ p: FilterPreset, image: CIImage, time: Double) -> CIImage {
        switch p {
        case .none:
            return image
        case .vivid:
            return controls(image, saturation: 1.35, contrast: 1.1, brightness: 0.02)
        case .warm:
            return temperature(controls(image, saturation: 1.1, contrast: 1.03, brightness: 0.01), warmth: 0.55, tint: 0.1)
        case .cool:
            return temperature(controls(image, saturation: 1.0, contrast: 1.05, brightness: 0), warmth: -0.55, tint: -0.05)
        case .mono:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectMono")
        case .noir:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectNoir")
        case .fade:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectFade")
        case .chrome:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectChrome")
        case .instant:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectInstant")
        case .process:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectProcess")
        case .transfer:
            return image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectTransfer")
        case .sepia:
            let f = CIFilter.sepiaTone()
            f.inputImage = image
            f.intensity = 0.85
            return f.outputImage ?? image
        case .cinematic:
            let base = controls(image, saturation: 1.1, contrast: 1.18, brightness: -0.02)
            return colorMatrix(temperature(base, warmth: 0.2, tint: 0),
                               r: (1.05, 0, 0), g: (0, 1.0, 0.02), b: (0, 0.04, 1.08), bias: (0, 0.01, 0.04))
        case .vintage:
            let sepia = CIFilter.sepiaTone()
            sepia.inputImage = image
            sepia.intensity = 0.35
            let s = sepia.outputImage ?? image
            let faded = controls(s, saturation: 0.85, contrast: 0.9, brightness: 0.03)
            return adjust(Adjustments(vignette: 0.6), image: faded.cropped(to: image.extent), time: time)
        case .dramatic:
            let c = controls(image, saturation: 0.8, contrast: 1.4, brightness: -0.03)
            return adjust(Adjustments(vignette: 0.7), image: c.cropped(to: image.extent), time: time)
        case .film:
            let f = image.applyingFilter("CIPhotoEffectFade")
            let warm = temperature(f, warmth: 0.15, tint: 0)
            return addGrain(warm.cropped(to: image.extent), amount: 0.6, time: time)
        case .pastel:
            return controls(image, saturation: 0.7, contrast: 0.85, brightness: 0.06)
        case .teal:
            let base = controls(image, saturation: 1.25, contrast: 1.1, brightness: 0)
            return colorMatrix(base, r: (1.0, 0, 0), g: (0, 1.02, 0.05), b: (0, 0.08, 1.1), bias: (-0.02, 0.02, 0.05))
        }
    }

    private static func controls(_ image: CIImage, saturation: Float, contrast: Float, brightness: Float) -> CIImage {
        let f = CIFilter.colorControls()
        f.inputImage = image
        f.saturation = saturation
        f.contrast = contrast
        f.brightness = brightness
        return f.outputImage ?? image
    }

    private static func temperature(_ image: CIImage, warmth: Double, tint: Double) -> CIImage {
        let f = CIFilter.temperatureAndTint()
        f.inputImage = image
        f.neutral = CIVector(x: CGFloat(6500 + warmth * 3000), y: CGFloat(tint * 60))
        f.targetNeutral = CIVector(x: 6500, y: 0)
        return f.outputImage ?? image
    }

    private static func colorMatrix(_ image: CIImage, r: (CGFloat, CGFloat, CGFloat), g: (CGFloat, CGFloat, CGFloat),
                                    b: (CGFloat, CGFloat, CGFloat), bias: (CGFloat, CGFloat, CGFloat)) -> CIImage {
        let f = CIFilter.colorMatrix()
        f.inputImage = image
        f.rVector = CIVector(x: r.0, y: r.1, z: r.2, w: 0)
        f.gVector = CIVector(x: g.0, y: g.1, z: g.2, w: 0)
        f.bVector = CIVector(x: b.0, y: b.1, z: b.2, w: 0)
        f.aVector = CIVector(x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1)
        f.biasVector = CIVector(x: bias.0, y: bias.1, z: bias.2, w: 0)
        return f.outputImage ?? image
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/FrameRenderer.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import CoreImage
import CoreImage.CIFilterBuiltins
import Vision
import Metal

/// Person segmentation for background removal (Vision).
final class PersonSegmenter {
    private let request: VNGeneratePersonSegmentationRequest

    init(accurate: Bool) {
        request = VNGeneratePersonSegmentationRequest()
        request.qualityLevel = accurate ? .accurate : .balanced
        request.outputPixelFormat = kCVPixelFormatType_OneComponent8
    }

    func mask(for image: CIImage) -> CIImage? {
        let handler = VNImageRequestHandler(ciImage: image, options: [:])
        do {
            try handler.perform([request])
        } catch {
            return nil
        }
        guard let buffer = request.results?.first?.pixelBuffer else { return nil }
        let mask = CIImage(cvPixelBuffer: buffer)
        let sx = image.extent.width / max(mask.extent.width, 1)
        let sy = image.extent.height / max(mask.extent.height, 1)
        return mask
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: sx, y: sy))
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: image.extent.minX, y: image.extent.minY))
    }
}

/// Builds the final frame for a moment on the timeline with Core Image.
final class FrameRenderer {
    static let context: CIContext = {
        if let device = MetalKernels.device {
            return CIContext(mtlDevice: device, options: [.cacheIntermediates: false, .name: "Cutline"])
        }
        return CIContext(options: [.cacheIntermediates: false])
    }()

    static let colorSpace = CGColorSpace(name: CGColorSpace.sRGB) ?? CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB()

    private var segmenter: PersonSegmenter?

    func render(time t: Double, instruction: CompositionInstruction, canvasSize: CGSize,
                sourceFrame: (CMPersistentTrackID) -> CVPixelBuffer?) -> CIImage {
        let snapshot = instruction.renderBox.snapshot
        let project = snapshot.project
        let canvas = CGRect(origin: .zero, size: canvasSize)
        let black = CIImage(color: .black).cropped(to: canvas)

        // Resolve the source image for a layer (oriented + per-clip effects, in source pixel space).
        var processedCache: [UUID: CIImage] = [:]
        func processed(_ layer: LayerSpec) -> (CIImage, Clip)? {
            guard let clip = snapshot.clips[layer.clipID] else { return nil }
```
