```swift
            if let hit = processedCache[layer.clipID] { return (hit, clip) }
            var image: CIImage?
            switch layer.source {
            case .track(let id):
                if let pb = sourceFrame(id) {
                    var img = CIImage(cvPixelBuffer: pb).oriented(layer.orientation)
                    img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -img.extent.minX, y: -img.extent.minY))
                    image = img
                }
            case .image(let url):
                if let img = TextRenderer.photo(url: url, maxPixel: snapshot.isExport ? 4096 : 1600) {
                    image = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -img.extent.minX, y: -img.extent.minY))
                }
            case .missing:
                image = missingPattern()
            }
            guard var img = image else { return nil }
            let local = (t - clip.timelineStart).clamped(0, clip.duration)
            img = applyClipEffects(img, clip: clip, sourceTime: clip.sourceTime(atLocal: local), time: t,
                                   accurate: snapshot.isExport)
            processedCache[layer.clipID] = img
            return (img, clip)
        }

        // Place a processed layer on the canvas with fit/fill, keyframes and motion presets.
        func placed(_ layer: LayerSpec) -> CIImage? {
            guard let pr = processed(layer) else { return nil }
            let img = pr.0, clip = pr.1
            let ext = img.extent
            guard ext.width > 0, ext.height > 0 else { return nil }
            let fitScale = clip.fit == .fit
                ? min(canvas.width / ext.width, canvas.height / ext.height)
                : max(canvas.width / ext.width, canvas.height / ext.height)
            let local = (t - clip.timelineStart).clamped(0, clip.duration)
            var tf = KeyframeMath.transform(base: clip.transform, keyframes: clip.keyframes, at: local)
            tf = MotionMath.apply(inPreset: clip.animationIn, outPreset: clip.animationOut,
                                  animationDuration: clip.animationDuration, itemDuration: clip.duration,
                                  local: t - clip.timelineStart, to: tf)
            return Self.place(img, canvas: canvasSize, transform: tf, baseScale: fitScale)
        }

        let mains = instruction.layers.filter { !$0.isOverlay && $0.isVisible(at: t) }.sorted { $0.order < $1.order }
        let primary = mains.first { t >= $0.coreStart && t < $0.coreEnd } ?? mains.first

        // Background
        var background = black
        switch project.canvas.background {
        case .black:
            break
        case .color:
            background = CIImage(color: project.canvas.backgroundColor.ciColor).cropped(to: canvas)
        case .blur:
            if let primary, let pr = processed(primary), pr.1.fit == .fit {
                background = blurredFill(pr.0, canvas: canvas, amount: project.canvas.blurAmount)
            }
        }

        var output = background
        if mains.count >= 2,
           let outLayer = mains.first(where: { $0.transitionOut?.contains(t) == true }),
           let window = outLayer.transitionOut,
           let inLayer = mains.first(where: { $0.order == outLayer.order + 1 }) {
            let a = placed(outLayer) ?? CIImage.empty()
            let b = placed(inLayer) ?? CIImage.empty()
            output = TransitionRenderer.blend(type: window.type, progress: window.progress(at: t), from: a, to: b,
                                              background: background, canvas: canvas,
                                              seed: Int(t * 30))
        } else if let primary, let img = placed(primary) {
            output = img.composited(over: background)
        }

        // Overlay clips (picture-in-picture), in project order.
        let overlays = instruction.layers.filter { $0.isOverlay && $0.isVisible(at: t) }.sorted { $0.order < $1.order }
        for layer in overlays {
            if let img = placed(layer) { output = img.composited(over: output) }
        }
        output = output.cropped(to: canvas)

        // Whole-timeline look.
        if !project.globalFilter.isIdentity {
            output = FilterEngine.apply(project.globalFilter, to: output, time: t)
        }

        // Photos, stickers and shapes.
        for item in project.overlays where item.contains(t) {
            if let img = overlayImage(item, canvas: canvasSize, resolver: snapshot.resolver, isExport: snapshot.isExport) {
                let local = t - item.start
                var tf = KeyframeMath.transform(base: item.transform, keyframes: item.keyframes, at: local)
                tf = MotionMath.apply(inPreset: item.animationIn, outPreset: item.animationOut,
                                      animationDuration: item.animationDuration, itemDuration: item.duration,
                                      local: local, to: tf)
                output = Self.place(img, canvas: canvasSize, transform: tf, baseScale: 1).composited(over: output)
            }
        }

        // Text.
        for item in project.texts where item.contains(t) {
            if let img = textLayer(item, t: t, canvas: canvasSize) {
                output = img.composited(over: output)
            }
        }

        // Captions.
        if let segment = project.captions.segment(at: t) {
            let highlight = segment.activeWordIndex(at: t)
            if let img = TextRenderer.captionImage(segment, track: project.captions, highlightIndex: highlight, canvasWidth: canvasSize.width) {
                var tf = Transform2D()
                tf.y = project.captions.verticalPosition - 0.5
                output = Self.place(img, canvas: canvasSize, transform: tf, baseScale: 1).composited(over: output)
            }
        }

        return output.cropped(to: canvas)
    }

    // MARK: Pieces

    static func place(_ image: CIImage, canvas: CGSize, transform tf: Transform2D, baseScale: CGFloat) -> CIImage {
        let e = image.extent
        guard e.width.isFinite, e.height.isFinite, e.width > 0, e.height > 0 else { return CIImage.empty() }
        let s = baseScale * CGFloat(max(tf.scale, 0.001))
        let m = CGAffineTransform(translationX: -e.midX, y: -e.midY)
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(scaleX: s, y: s))
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(rotationAngle: -CGFloat(tf.rotation) * .pi / 180))
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(translationX: canvas.width / 2 + CGFloat(tf.x) * canvas.width,
                                             y: canvas.height / 2 - CGFloat(tf.y) * canvas.height))
        let out = image.transformed(by: m)
        return TransitionRenderer.withAlpha(out, tf.opacity)
    }

    private func applyClipEffects(_ input: CIImage, clip: Clip, sourceTime: Double, time: Double, accurate: Bool) -> CIImage {
        var img = input
        var ext = img.extent

        if clip.effects.stabilize, let data = clip.effects.stabilization, !clip.isImage {
            let (dx, dy) = data.offset(atSource: sourceTime)
            let zoom: CGFloat = 1.12
            let m = CGAffineTransform(translationX: CGFloat(dx) * ext.width, y: CGFloat(dy) * ext.height)
                .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(translationX: -ext.midX, y: -ext.midY))
                .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(scaleX: zoom, y: zoom))
                .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(translationX: ext.midX, y: ext.midY))
            img = img.clampedToExtent().transformed(by: m).cropped(to: ext)
        }

        if clip.effects.chromaKey.enabled {
            img = MetalKernels.chromaKey(img, settings: clip.effects.chromaKey)
        }

        if clip.effects.removeBackground {
            if segmenter == nil { segmenter = PersonSegmenter(accurate: accurate) }
            if let mask = segmenter?.mask(for: img) {
                let f = CIFilter.blendWithMask()
                f.inputImage = img
                f.backgroundImage = CIImage.empty()
                f.maskImage = mask
                img = f.outputImage?.cropped(to: ext) ?? img
            }
        }

        if !clip.filter.isIdentity {
            img = FilterEngine.apply(clip.filter, to: img, time: time)
        }

        if !clip.crop.isFull {
            let c = clip.crop
            let rect = CGRect(x: CGFloat(c.x) * ext.width,
                              y: CGFloat(1 - c.y - c.height) * ext.height,
                              width: CGFloat(c.width) * ext.width,
                              height: CGFloat(c.height) * ext.height).integral
            if rect.width > 2, rect.height > 2 {
                img = img.cropped(to: rect)
                img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -rect.minX, y: -rect.minY))
                ext = img.extent
            }
        }

        let turns = ((clip.quarterTurns % 4) + 4) % 4
        if turns != 0 {
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(rotationAngle: -CGFloat(turns) * .pi / 2))
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -img.extent.minX, y: -img.extent.minY))
        }
        if clip.flipH {
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: -1, y: 1))
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -img.extent.minX, y: 0))
        }
        if clip.flipV {
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: 1, y: -1))
            img = img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: 0, y: -img.extent.minY))
        }
        return img
    }

    private func blurredFill(_ source: CIImage, canvas: CGRect, amount: Double) -> CIImage {
        let e = source.extent
        guard e.width > 0, e.height > 0 else { return CIImage(color: .black).cropped(to: canvas) }
        let down: CGFloat = 0.25
        let fill = max(canvas.width / e.width, canvas.height / e.height) * down
        let small = source
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -e.midX, y: -e.midY))
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: fill, y: fill))
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: canvas.midX * down, y: canvas.midY * down))
        let smallCanvas = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: canvas.width * down, height: canvas.height * down)
        let blur = CIFilter.gaussianBlur()
        blur.inputImage = small.clampedToExtent()
        blur.radius = Float(max(canvas.width, canvas.height) * down * CGFloat(0.02 + amount * 0.06))
        let blurred = (blur.outputImage ?? small).cropped(to: smallCanvas)
        let dim = CIFilter.colorControls()
        dim.inputImage = blurred
        dim.brightness = -0.12
        dim.saturation = 1.1
        let up = (dim.outputImage ?? blurred).transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: 1 / down, y: 1 / down))
        return up.cropped(to: canvas)
    }

    private func overlayImage(_ item: OverlayItem, canvas: CGSize, resolver: MediaResolver, isExport: Bool) -> CIImage? {
        switch item.kind {
        case .sticker:
            return TextRenderer.stickerImage(item.sticker, canvasSize: canvas)
        case .shape:
            return TextRenderer.shapeImage(item.shape, color: item.color, filled: item.filled, canvasSize: canvas)
        case .image:
            guard let url = resolver.imageURL(for: item.imageMediaID),
                  let img = TextRenderer.photo(url: url, maxPixel: isExport ? 2048 : 1024) else { return nil }
            let e = img.extent
            let box = min(canvas.width, canvas.height) * 0.45
            let s = min(box / max(e.width, 1), box / max(e.height, 1))
            return img.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: s, y: s))
        }
    }

    private func textLayer(_ item: TextItem, t: Double, canvas: CGSize) -> CIImage? {
        let local = t - item.start
        let ad = min(max(item.animationDuration, 0.05), item.duration / 2)
        var visibleChars: Int?
        var tf = KeyframeMath.transform(base: item.transform, keyframes: item.keyframes, at: local)
        let count = item.text.count

        if local < ad {
            let p = local / ad
            switch item.animationIn {
            case .none: break
            case .fade: tf.opacity *= Easing.easeOut.apply(p)
            case .pop:
                tf.scale *= max(0.01, Easing.back.apply(p))
                tf.opacity *= min(1, p * 2)
            case .slide:
                tf.y += (1 - Easing.easeOut.apply(p)) * 0.12
                tf.opacity *= Easing.easeOut.apply(p)
            case .typewriter:
                break
            }
        }
        if item.animationIn == .typewriter {
            let typeDuration = min(max(Double(count) * 0.06, 0.3), item.duration * 0.6)
            if local < typeDuration {
                visibleChars = Int((local / typeDuration) * Double(count))
            }
        }
        let remaining = item.duration - local
        if remaining < ad {
            let p = max(remaining, 0) / ad
            switch item.animationOut {
            case .none: break
            case .fade: tf.opacity *= Easing.easeIn.apply(p)
            case .pop:
                tf.scale *= max(0.01, Easing.easeIn.apply(p))
                tf.opacity *= p
            case .slide:
                tf.y += (1 - Easing.easeIn.apply(p)) * 0.12
                tf.opacity *= Easing.easeIn.apply(p)
            case .typewriter:
                break
            }
        }
        if item.animationOut == .typewriter {
            let eraseDuration = min(max(Double(count) * 0.04, 0.25), item.duration * 0.4)
            if remaining < eraseDuration {
                let shown = Int((max(remaining, 0) / eraseDuration) * Double(count))
                visibleChars = min(visibleChars ?? count, shown)
            }
        }
        if let v = visibleChars, v <= 0 { return nil }
        guard let img = TextRenderer.textImage(item, canvasWidth: canvas.width, visibleCharacters: visibleChars) else { return nil }
        return Self.place(img, canvas: canvas, transform: tf, baseScale: 1)
    }

    private func missingPattern() -> CIImage {
        let checker = CIFilter.checkerboardGenerator()
        checker.color0 = CIColor(red: 0.35, green: 0.05, blue: 0.08)
        checker.color1 = CIColor(red: 0.15, green: 0.02, blue: 0.04)
        checker.width = 40
        checker.center = .zero
        checker.sharpness = 1
        return (checker.outputImage ?? CIImage(color: .red)).cropped(to: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1280, height: 720))
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/Kernels.metal`

```cpp
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
#include <CoreImage/CoreImage.h>

extern "C" {
namespace coreimage {

    /// Chroma key: removes pixels close to `key` in CbCr space, with a soft edge
    /// and spill suppression on the remaining pixels.
    float4 chromaKey(sample_t s, float3 key, float tolerance, float softness) {
        float4 c = unpremultiply(s);
        float3 rgb = c.rgb;

        float cb = -0.168736 * rgb.r - 0.331264 * rgb.g + 0.5 * rgb.b;
        float cr = 0.5 * rgb.r - 0.418688 * rgb.g - 0.081312 * rgb.b;
        float kcb = -0.168736 * key.r - 0.331264 * key.g + 0.5 * key.b;
        float kcr = 0.5 * key.r - 0.418688 * key.g - 0.081312 * key.b;

        float d = distance(float2(cb, cr), float2(kcb, kcr));
        float lo = tolerance * 0.45;
        float hi = lo + softness * 0.35 + 0.002;
        float a = smoothstep(lo, hi, d);

        // Spill suppression: pull the dominant key channel toward the average of the others.
        float3 outRGB = rgb;
        if (key.g >= key.r && key.g >= key.b) {
            float limit = (rgb.r + rgb.b) * 0.5;
            outRGB.g = mix(min(rgb.g, limit), rgb.g, a);
        } else if (key.b >= key.r && key.b >= key.g) {
            float limit = (rgb.r + rgb.g) * 0.5;
            outRGB.b = mix(min(rgb.b, limit), rgb.b, a);
        }
        return premultiply(float4(outRGB, c.a * a));
    }

    /// Per-channel multiply used by the glitch transition (RGB split).
    float4 channelTint(sample_t s, float3 mask) {
        return float4(s.rgb * mask, s.a);
    }
}
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/MetalKernels.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import CoreImage
import Metal

/// Loads Core Image kernels compiled from Kernels.metal, with pure Core Image fallbacks.
enum MetalKernels {
    static let device: MTLDevice? = MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice()

    private static let library: Data? = {
        guard let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "default", withExtension: "metallib") else { return nil }
        return try? Data(contentsOf: url)
    }()

    static let chromaKeyKernel: CIColorKernel? = {
        guard let data = library else { return nil }
        return try? CIColorKernel(functionName: "chromaKey", fromMetalLibraryData: data)
    }()

    static let channelTintKernel: CIColorKernel? = {
        guard let data = library else { return nil }
        return try? CIColorKernel(functionName: "channelTint", fromMetalLibraryData: data)
    }()

    static func chromaKey(_ image: CIImage, settings: ChromaKeySettings) -> CIImage {
        let key = settings.color
        if let kernel = chromaKeyKernel,
           let out = kernel.apply(extent: image.extent, arguments: [
               image,
               CIVector(x: CGFloat(key.r), y: CGFloat(key.g), z: CGFloat(key.b)),
               Float(settings.tolerance),
               Float(settings.softness)
           ]) {
            return out
        }
        return fallbackChromaKey(image, settings: settings)
    }

    static func tint(_ image: CIImage, r: CGFloat, g: CGFloat, b: CGFloat) -> CIImage {
        if let kernel = channelTintKernel,
           let out = kernel.apply(extent: image.extent, arguments: [image, CIVector(x: r, y: g, z: b)]) {
            return out
        }
        return image.applyingFilter("CIColorMatrix", parameters: [
            "inputRVector": CIVector(x: r, y: 0, z: 0, w: 0),
            "inputGVector": CIVector(x: 0, y: g, z: 0, w: 0),
            "inputBVector": CIVector(x: 0, y: 0, z: b, w: 0)
        ])
    }

    // MARK: Fallback (color cube)

    private static let cubeLock = NSLock()
    private static var cubeCache: (ChromaKeySettings, Data)?

    private static func fallbackChromaKey(_ image: CIImage, settings: ChromaKeySettings) -> CIImage {
        let size = 32
        cubeLock.lock()
        var data: Data
        if let cached = cubeCache, cached.0 == settings {
            data = cached.1
        } else {
            data = makeCube(size: size, settings: settings)
            cubeCache = (settings, data)
        }
        cubeLock.unlock()
        return image.applyingFilter("CIColorCube", parameters: [
            "inputCubeDimension": size,
            "inputCubeData": data
        ])
    }

    private static func makeCube(size: Int, settings: ChromaKeySettings) -> Data {
        var values = [Float](repeating: 0, count: size * size * size * 4)
        let key = settings.color
        let kcb = -0.168736 * key.r - 0.331264 * key.g + 0.5 * key.b
        let kcr = 0.5 * key.r - 0.418688 * key.g - 0.081312 * key.b
        let lo = settings.tolerance * 0.45
        let hi = lo + settings.softness * 0.35 + 0.002
        var offset = 0
        for z in 0..<size {
            let b = Double(z) / Double(size - 1)
            for y in 0..<size {
                let g = Double(y) / Double(size - 1)
                for x in 0..<size {
                    let r = Double(x) / Double(size - 1)
                    let cb = -0.168736 * r - 0.331264 * g + 0.5 * b
                    let cr = 0.5 * r - 0.418688 * g - 0.081312 * b
                    let d = sqrt(pow(cb - kcb, 2) + pow(cr - kcr, 2))
                    let t = ((d - lo) / (hi - lo)).clamped(0, 1)
                    let a = t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
                    values[offset] = Float(r * a)
                    values[offset + 1] = Float(g * a)
                    values[offset + 2] = Float(b * a)
                    values[offset + 3] = Float(a)
                    offset += 4
                }
            }
        }
        return values.withUnsafeBufferPointer { Data(buffer: $0) }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/TextRenderer.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit
import CoreImage
import ImageIO

/// Rasterizes text, captions, stickers, shapes and photos into cached CIImages.
enum TextRenderer {
    private static let cache: NSCache<NSString, CIImage> = {
        let c = NSCache<NSString, CIImage>()
        c.countLimit = 300
        return c
    }()

    private static func render(size: CGSize, _ draw: (CGContext) -> Void) -> CIImage? {
        guard size.width >= 1, size.height >= 1, size.width < 8192, size.height < 8192 else { return nil }
        let format = UIGraphicsImageRendererFormat()
        format.scale = 1
        format.opaque = false
        let image = UIGraphicsImageRenderer(size: size, format: format).image { ctx in
            draw(ctx.cgContext)
        }
        guard let cg = image.cgImage else { return nil }
        return CIImage(cgImage: cg)
    }

    // MARK: Text

    static func attributes(for item: TextItem, scale: CGFloat) -> [NSAttributedString.Key: Any] {
        let font = FontResolver.font(family: item.fontFamily, size: CGFloat(item.fontSize) * scale, bold: item.bold, italic: item.italic)
        let para = NSMutableParagraphStyle()
        para.alignment = item.alignment.nsAlignment
        para.lineBreakMode = .byWordWrapping
        var attrs: [NSAttributedString.Key: Any] = [
            .font: font,
            .foregroundColor: item.color.uiColor,
            .paragraphStyle: para
        ]
        if item.outlineWidth > 0 {
            attrs[.strokeColor] = item.outlineColor.uiColor
            attrs[.strokeWidth] = -item.outlineWidth * 1.6
        }
        if item.shadow {
            let s = NSShadow()
            s.shadowColor = item.shadowColor.uiColor
            s.shadowBlurRadius = 8 * scale
            s.shadowOffset = CGSize(width: 0, height: 3 * scale)
            attrs[.shadow] = s
        }
        return attrs
    }

    static func textImage(_ item: TextItem, canvasWidth: CGFloat, visibleCharacters: Int?) -> CIImage? {
        let key = "t|\(item.styleSignature)|\(Int(canvasWidth))|\(visibleCharacters ?? -1)" as NSString
        if let hit = cache.object(forKey: key) { return hit }
        let scale = canvasWidth / 1080
        let attrs = attributes(for: item, scale: scale)
        let font = (attrs[.font] as? UIFont) ?? UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: 40)
        let fullText = item.text.isEmpty ? " " : item.text
        let shown: String
        if let n = visibleCharacters { shown = String(fullText.prefix(max(n, 0))) } else { shown = fullText }

        let full = NSAttributedString(string: fullText, attributes: attrs)
        let maxWidth = canvasWidth * 0.9
        let bounds = full.boundingRect(with: CGSize(width: maxWidth, height: .greatestFiniteMagnitude),
                                       options: [.usesLineFragmentOrigin, .usesFontLeading], context: nil).integral
        let pad = item.backgroundBox ? font.pointSize * 0.35 : font.pointSize * 0.2 + CGFloat(item.outlineWidth) * scale
        let size = CGSize(width: ceil(bounds.width + pad * 2), height: ceil(bounds.height + pad * 2))
        let drawn = NSAttributedString(string: shown.isEmpty ? " " : shown, attributes: attrs)
        let image = render(size: size) { ctx in
            if item.backgroundBox {
                let path = UIBezierPath(roundedRect: CGRect(origin: .zero, size: size), cornerRadius: font.pointSize * 0.3)
                ctx.setFillColor(item.boxColor.cgColor)
                ctx.addPath(path.cgPath)
                ctx.fillPath()
            }
            drawn.draw(with: CGRect(x: pad, y: pad, width: bounds.width + 1, height: bounds.height + 1),
                       options: [.usesLineFragmentOrigin, .usesFontLeading], context: nil)
        }
        if let image { cache.setObject(image, forKey: key) }
        return image
    }

    // MARK: Captions

    static func captionImage(_ segment: CaptionSegment, track: CaptionTrack, highlightIndex: Int?, canvasWidth: CGFloat) -> CIImage? {
        let key = "c|\(segment.id)|\(segment.text)|\(track.style.rawValue)|\(track.fontScale)|\(track.textColor)|\(track.highlightColor)|\(highlightIndex ?? -1)|\(Int(canvasWidth))" as NSString
        if let hit = cache.object(forKey: key) { return hit }
        let scale = canvasWidth / 1080 * CGFloat(track.fontScale)
        let para = NSMutableParagraphStyle()
        para.alignment = .center
        para.lineBreakMode = .byWordWrapping

        let font: UIFont
        var base: [NSAttributedString.Key: Any] = [.paragraphStyle: para, .foregroundColor: track.textColor.uiColor]
        let shadow = NSShadow()
        shadow.shadowColor = UIColor.black.withAlphaComponent(0.7)
        shadow.shadowBlurRadius = 10 * scale
        shadow.shadowOffset = CGSize(width: 0, height: 3 * scale)
        var drawBox = false
        switch track.style {
        case .boldCenter:
            font = UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: 76 * scale, weight: .black)
            base[.strokeColor] = UIColor.black
            base[.strokeWidth] = -7.0
            base[.shadow] = shadow
        case .karaoke:
            font = UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: 62 * scale, weight: .heavy)
            base[.strokeColor] = UIColor.black
            base[.strokeWidth] = -4.0
            drawBox = true
        case .minimalBottom:
            font = UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: 46 * scale, weight: .semibold)
            base[.shadow] = shadow
        }
        base[.font] = font

        let result = NSMutableAttributedString()
        for (i, word) in segment.words.enumerated() {
            var attrs = base
            var text = word.text
            if track.style == .boldCenter { text = text.uppercased() }
            if let h = highlightIndex {
                if track.style == .karaoke && i == h {
                    attrs[.foregroundColor] = track.highlightColor.uiColor
                } else if track.style == .boldCenter && i == h {
                    attrs[.foregroundColor] = track.highlightColor.uiColor
                }
            }
            result.append(NSAttributedString(string: text, attributes: attrs))
            if i < segment.words.count - 1 {
                result.append(NSAttributedString(string: " ", attributes: base))
            }
        }
        if result.length == 0 { return nil }
        let maxWidth = canvasWidth * 0.84
        let bounds = result.boundingRect(with: CGSize(width: maxWidth, height: .greatestFiniteMagnitude),
                                         options: [.usesLineFragmentOrigin, .usesFontLeading], context: nil).integral
        let pad = font.pointSize * (drawBox ? 0.4 : 0.25)
        let size = CGSize(width: ceil(bounds.width + pad * 2), height: ceil(bounds.height + pad * 2))
        let image = render(size: size) { ctx in
            if drawBox {
                let path = UIBezierPath(roundedRect: CGRect(origin: .zero, size: size), cornerRadius: font.pointSize * 0.3)
                ctx.setFillColor(UIColor.black.withAlphaComponent(0.55).cgColor)
                ctx.addPath(path.cgPath)
                ctx.fillPath()
            }
            result.draw(with: CGRect(x: pad, y: pad, width: bounds.width + 1, height: bounds.height + 1),
                        options: [.usesLineFragmentOrigin, .usesFontLeading], context: nil)
        }
        if let image { cache.setObject(image, forKey: key) }
        return image
    }

    // MARK: Stickers & shapes

    static func stickerImage(_ emoji: String, canvasSize: CGSize) -> CIImage? {
        let side = min(canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height) * 0.28
        let key = "s|\(emoji)|\(Int(side))" as NSString
        if let hit = cache.object(forKey: key) { return hit }
        let font = UIFont.systemFont(ofSize: side * 0.8)
        let str = NSAttributedString(string: emoji, attributes: [.font: font])
        let b = str.boundingRect(with: CGSize(width: side * 4, height: side * 4), options: [.usesLineFragmentOrigin], context: nil).integral
        let size = CGSize(width: max(b.width, 1) + 8, height: max(b.height, 1) + 8)
        let image = render(size: size) { _ in
            str.draw(at: CGPoint(x: 4, y: 4))
        }
        if let image { cache.setObject(image, forKey: key) }
        return image
    }

    static func shapePath(_ kind: ShapeKind, in rect: CGRect) -> UIBezierPath {
        let w = rect.width, h = rect.height, x = rect.minX, y = rect.minY
        switch kind {
        case .rectangle:
            return UIBezierPath(rect: rect)
        case .roundedRect:
            return UIBezierPath(roundedRect: rect, cornerRadius: min(w, h) * 0.2)
        case .circle:
            return UIBezierPath(ovalIn: rect)
        case .triangle:
            let p = UIBezierPath()
            p.move(to: CGPoint(x: x + w / 2, y: y))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w, y: y + h))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y + h))
            p.close()
            return p
        case .star:
            let p = UIBezierPath()
            let c = CGPoint(x: rect.midX, y: rect.midY)
            let outer = min(w, h) / 2
            let inner = outer * 0.42
            for i in 0..<10 {
                let r = i % 2 == 0 ? outer : inner
                let a = -CGFloat.pi / 2 + CGFloat(i) * .pi / 5
                let pt = CGPoint(x: c.x + cos(a) * r, y: c.y + sin(a) * r)
                if i == 0 { p.move(to: pt) } else { p.addLine(to: pt) }
            }
            p.close()
            return p
        case .heart:
            let p = UIBezierPath()
            p.move(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.5, y: y + h * 0.92))
            p.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.04, y: y + h * 0.36),
                       controlPoint1: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.22, y: y + h * 0.76),
                       controlPoint2: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.04, y: y + h * 0.58))
            p.addArc(withCenter: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.27, y: y + h * 0.32), radius: w * 0.23,
                     startAngle: .pi * 1.05, endAngle: 0, clockwise: true)
            p.addArc(withCenter: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.73, y: y + h * 0.32), radius: w * 0.23,
                     startAngle: .pi, endAngle: -.pi * 0.05, clockwise: true)
            p.addCurve(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.5, y: y + h * 0.92),
                       controlPoint1: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.96, y: y + h * 0.58),
                       controlPoint2: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.78, y: y + h * 0.76))
            p.close()
            return p
        case .arrow:
            let p = UIBezierPath()
            let shaft = h * 0.3
            p.move(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y + h / 2 - shaft / 2))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.6, y: y + h / 2 - shaft / 2))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.6, y: y))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w, y: y + h / 2))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.6, y: y + h))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x + w * 0.6, y: y + h / 2 + shaft / 2))
            p.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y + h / 2 + shaft / 2))
            p.close()
            return p
        case .line:
            return UIBezierPath(roundedRect: rect, cornerRadius: h / 2)
        }
    }

    static func shapeSize(_ kind: ShapeKind, side: CGFloat) -> CGSize {
        switch kind {
        case .rectangle: return CGSize(width: side * 1.5, height: side)
        case .arrow: return CGSize(width: side * 1.4, height: side * 0.8)
        case .line: return CGSize(width: side * 1.6, height: max(side * 0.06, 4))
        default: return CGSize(width: side, height: side)
        }
    }

    static func shapeImage(_ kind: ShapeKind, color: RGBAColor, filled: Bool, canvasSize: CGSize) -> CIImage? {
        let side = min(canvasSize.width, canvasSize.height) * 0.3
        let key = "p|\(kind.rawValue)|\(color)|\(filled)|\(Int(side))" as NSString
        if let hit = cache.object(forKey: key) { return hit }
        let shapeSize = shapeSize(kind, side: side)
        let lineWidth = max(side * 0.05, 3)
        let inset = filled ? 1 : lineWidth
        let size = CGSize(width: shapeSize.width + inset * 2, height: shapeSize.height + inset * 2)
        let image = render(size: size) { ctx in
            let path = shapePath(kind, in: CGRect(x: inset, y: inset, width: shapeSize.width, height: shapeSize.height))
            if filled || kind == .line {
                ctx.setFillColor(color.cgColor)
                ctx.addPath(path.cgPath)
                ctx.fillPath()
            } else {
                ctx.setStrokeColor(color.cgColor)
                ctx.setLineWidth(lineWidth)
                ctx.setLineJoin(.round)
                ctx.addPath(path.cgPath)
                ctx.strokePath()
            }
        }
        if let image { cache.setObject(image, forKey: key) }
        return image
    }

    // MARK: Photos

    /// Decoded, oriented photo limited to `maxPixel`.
    static func photo(url: URL, maxPixel: Int = 2048) -> CIImage? {
        let key = "i|\(url.path)|\(maxPixel)" as NSString
        if let hit = cache.object(forKey: key) { return hit }
        guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil) else { return nil }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: maxPixel,
            kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true
        ]
        guard let cg = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(src, 0, options as CFDictionary) else { return nil }
        let image = CIImage(cgImage: cg)
        cache.setObject(image, forKey: key)
        return image
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Rendering/TransitionRenderer.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import CoreImage
import CoreImage.CIFilterBuiltins

enum TransitionRenderer {
    static func withAlpha(_ image: CIImage, _ alpha: Double) -> CIImage {
        if alpha >= 0.999 { return image }
        return image.applyingFilter("CIColorMatrix", parameters: [
            "inputAVector": CIVector(x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: CGFloat(max(alpha, 0)))
        ])
    }

    private static func around(_ canvas: CGRect, scale: CGFloat, rotation: CGFloat) -> CGAffineTransform {
        CGAffineTransform(translationX: -canvas.midX, y: -canvas.midY)
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(scaleX: scale, y: scale))
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(rotationAngle: rotation))
            .concatenating(CGAffineTransform(translationX: canvas.midX, y: canvas.midY))
    }

    /// Blends two fully placed layer images (transparent outside their content) for a transition.
    /// `background` is what shows behind both (canvas background).
    static func blend(type: TransitionType, progress raw: Double, from a: CIImage, to b: CIImage,
                      background: CIImage, canvas: CGRect, seed: Int) -> CIImage {
        let p = raw.clamped(0, 1)
        let black = CIImage(color: .black).cropped(to: canvas)
        switch type {
        case .cut:
            return (p < 0.5 ? a : b).composited(over: background)
        case .fade:
            if p < 0.5 {
                return withAlpha(a.composited(over: background), 1 - p * 2).composited(over: black)
            } else {
                return withAlpha(b.composited(over: background), p * 2 - 1).composited(over: black)
            }
        case .dissolve:
            let f = CIFilter.dissolveTransition()
            f.inputImage = a.composited(over: background)
            f.targetImage = b.composited(over: background)
            f.time = Float(Easing.easeInOut.apply(p))
            return f.outputImage?.cropped(to: canvas) ?? b
        case .slide:
            let e = CGFloat(Easing.easeInOut.apply(p))
            let w = canvas.width
            let movedA = a.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -e * w, y: 0))
            let movedB = b.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: (1 - e) * w, y: 0))
            return movedB.composited(over: movedA).composited(over: black).cropped(to: canvas)
        case .zoom:
            let e = CGFloat(Easing.easeInOut.apply(p))
            let za = a.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                .transformed(by: around(canvas, scale: 1 + e * 0.6, rotation: 0))
            let zb = b.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                .transformed(by: around(canvas, scale: 0.7 + 0.3 * e, rotation: 0))
            return withAlpha(zb, Double(e)).composited(over: withAlpha(za, Double(1 - e))).composited(over: black).cropped(to: canvas)
        case .spin:
            let e = CGFloat(Easing.easeInOut.apply(p))
            if e < 0.5 {
                let k = e * 2
                let sa = a.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                    .transformed(by: around(canvas, scale: 1 - k * 0.9, rotation: -k * .pi))
                return sa.composited(over: black).cropped(to: canvas)
            } else {
                let k = (1 - e) * 2
                let sb = b.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas)
                    .transformed(by: around(canvas, scale: 1 - k * 0.9, rotation: k * .pi))
                return sb.composited(over: black).cropped(to: canvas)
            }
        case .glitch:
            return glitch(p: p, a: a.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas),
                          b: b.composited(over: background).cropped(to: canvas), canvas: canvas, seed: seed)
        }
    }

    private static func glitch(p: Double, a: CIImage, b: CIImage, canvas: CGRect, seed: Int) -> CIImage {
        var rng = SeededRandom(seed: UInt64(seed &* 7919 &+ 17))
        let flicker = rng.next() < 0.25
        let base = (p < 0.5) != flicker ? a : b
        let strength = CGFloat(sin(p * .pi))
        let shift = strength * canvas.width * 0.025

        let red = MetalKernels.tint(base, r: 1, g: 0, b: 0)
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: shift, y: 0))
        let green = MetalKernels.tint(base, r: 0, g: 1, b: 0)
        let blue = MetalKernels.tint(base, r: 0, g: 0, b: 1)
            .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -shift, y: 0))
        let add = CIFilter.additionCompositing()
        add.inputImage = red
        add.backgroundImage = green
        let rg = add.outputImage ?? base
        let add2 = CIFilter.additionCompositing()
        add2.inputImage = blue
        add2.backgroundImage = rg
        var result = (add2.outputImage ?? base).cropped(to: canvas)

        // Displaced horizontal slices.
        let slices = Int(3 + strength * 6)
        for _ in 0..<slices {
            let h = canvas.height * CGFloat(0.02 + rng.next() * 0.08)
            let y = canvas.minY + CGFloat(rng.next()) * (canvas.height - h)
            let dx = (CGFloat(rng.next()) - 0.5) * canvas.width * 0.15 * strength
            let band = base.cropped(to: CGRect(x: canvas.minX, y: y, width: canvas.width, height: h))
                .transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: dx, y: 0))
            result = band.composited(over: result)
        }
        return result.composited(over: CIImage(color: .black).cropped(to: canvas)).cropped(to: canvas)
    }
}

struct SeededRandom {
    private var state: UInt64
    init(seed: UInt64) { state = seed == 0 ? 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 : seed }

    mutating func next() -> Double {
        state ^= state << 13
        state ^= state >> 7
        state ^= state << 17
        return Double(state % 1_000_000) / 1_000_000
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/AppError.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit

enum PermissionKind: String {
    case microphone = "Microphone"
    case speech = "Speech Recognition"
    case photos = "Photos"
}

enum AppError: LocalizedError {
    case unsupportedFormat(String)
    case lowStorage(needed: Int64, available: Int64)
    case missingMedia(String)
    case permissionDenied(PermissionKind)
    case importFailed(String)
    case exportFailed(String)
    case speechUnavailable(String)
    case corruptedProject
    case processingFailed(String)
    case cancelled

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .unsupportedFormat(let name):
            return "“\(name)” isn't a supported video, photo or audio format."
        case .lowStorage(let needed, let available):
            let f = ByteCountFormatter()
            f.countStyle = .file
            return "Not enough storage. This needs about \(f.string(fromByteCount: needed)) but only \(f.string(fromByteCount: max(available, 0))) is free."
        case .missingMedia(let name):
            return "The media file “\(name)” can't be found."
        case .permissionDenied(let kind):
            return "Cutline doesn't have access to \(kind.rawValue)."
        case .importFailed(let reason):
            return "Import failed: \(reason)"
        case .exportFailed(let reason):
            return "Export failed: \(reason)"
        case .speechUnavailable(let reason):
            return reason
        case .corruptedProject:
            return "This project file is damaged and couldn't be opened."
        case .processingFailed(let reason):
            return reason
        case .cancelled:
            return "Cancelled."
        }
    }

    var recoverySuggestion: String? {
        switch self {
        case .unsupportedFormat:
            return "Try a different file, or convert it to MP4, MOV, JPEG, PNG, HEIC, M4A, MP3 or WAV."
        case .lowStorage:
            return "Free up space in Settings › General › iPhone Storage, then try again."
        case .missingMedia:
            return "Re-import the media or delete the clip."
        case .permissionDenied:
            return "You can turn access on in Settings."
        case .speechUnavailable:
            return "Captions are created on your device. Download the language in Settings › General › Keyboard › Dictation, then try again."
        case .corruptedProject:
            return "A backup copy was not available. You can delete this project."
        default:
            return nil
        }
    }

    var opensSettings: Bool {
        if case .permissionDenied = self { return true }
        return false
    }
}

struct AlertInfo: Identifiable {
    let id = UUID()
    var title: String
    var message: String
    var showsSettings = false

    init(title: String = "Something went wrong", message: String, showsSettings: Bool = false) {
        self.title = title
        self.message = message
        self.showsSettings = showsSettings
    }

    init(error: Error) {
        if let app = error as? AppError {
            title = "Can't complete that"
            message = [app.errorDescription, app.recoverySuggestion].compactMap { $0 }.joined(separator: "\n\n")
            showsSettings = app.opensSettings
        } else {
            title = "Something went wrong"
            message = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }
}

enum Haptics {
    static func snap() {
        let g = UISelectionFeedbackGenerator()
        g.selectionChanged()
    }

    static func tap() {
        let g = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .light)
        g.impactOccurred()
    }

    static func action() {
        let g = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .medium)
        g.impactOccurred()
    }

    static func success() {
        let g = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
        g.notificationOccurred(.success)
    }

    static func warning() {
        let g = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
        g.notificationOccurred(.warning)
    }
}

enum StorageGuard {
    static func availableBytes() -> Int64 {
        let url = URL(fileURLWithPath: NSHomeDirectory())
        if let values = try? url.resourceValues(forKeys: [.volumeAvailableCapacityForImportantUsageKey]),
           let cap = values.volumeAvailableCapacityForImportantUsage {
            return cap
        }
        return Int64.max
    }

    /// Throws `lowStorage` unless `bytes` plus a 150 MB safety margin is available.
    static func ensure(_ bytes: Int64) throws {
        let available = availableBytes()
        let margin: Int64 = 150 * 1024 * 1024
        if available < bytes + margin {
            throw AppError.lowStorage(needed: bytes + margin, available: available)
        }
    }

    static func fileSize(_ url: URL) -> Int64 {
        let attrs = try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: url.path)
        return (attrs?[.size] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? 0
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/AutosaveService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit

/// Debounced, ordered, crash-safe project saving.
@MainActor
final class AutosaveService {
    private var pending: Task<Void, Never>?
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "app.cutline.autosave", qos: .utility)
    private let paths: ProjectPaths
    private let provider: () -> Project
    var onSaved: (() -> Void)?

    init(paths: ProjectPaths, provider: @escaping () -> Project) {
        self.paths = paths
        self.provider = provider
    }

    /// Saves after `delay` seconds of inactivity (always under one second).
    func schedule(delay: Double = 0.6) {
        pending?.cancel()
        pending = Task { [weak self] in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64(delay * 1_000_000_000))
            guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
            self?.saveNow()
        }
    }

    func saveNow() {
        pending?.cancel()
        pending = nil
        var project = provider()
        project.modifiedAt = Date()
        guard let data = try? ProjectFileIO.encode(project) else { return }
        let paths = self.paths
        queue.async { [weak self] in
            try? ProjectFileIO.write(data, to: paths)
            Task { @MainActor in self?.onSaved?() }
        }
    }

    /// Writes synchronously; used when the app is backgrounding or the editor closes.
    func flush() {
        pending?.cancel()
        pending = nil
        var project = provider()
        project.modifiedAt = Date()
        guard let data = try? ProjectFileIO.encode(project) else { return }
        let paths = self.paths
        let app = UIApplication.shared
        let taskID = app.beginBackgroundTask(withName: "Cutline autosave", expirationHandler: nil)
        queue.sync {
            try? ProjectFileIO.write(data, to: paths)
        }
        if taskID != .invalid { app.endBackgroundTask(taskID) }
    }
}
```
