## `Cutline/Services/CaptionService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Speech

/// On-device speech-to-text with word-level timing.
enum CaptionService {
    /// Languages that can be transcribed without a network connection on this device.
    static func onDeviceLocales() -> [Locale] {
        SFSpeechRecognizer.supportedLocales()
            .filter { SFSpeechRecognizer(locale: $0)?.supportsOnDeviceRecognition == true }
            .sorted { $0.identifier < $1.identifier }
    }

    static func defaultLocale() -> Locale {
        let current = Locale.current
        let available = onDeviceLocales()
        if available.contains(where: { $0.identifier == current.identifier }) { return current }
        if let lang = current.language.languageCode?.identifier,
           let match = available.first(where: { $0.language.languageCode?.identifier == lang }) {
            return match
        }
        return available.first ?? Locale(identifier: "en-US")
    }

    static func transcribe(audioURL: URL, locale: Locale) async throws -> [CaptionWord] {
        guard await Permissions.speech() else { throw AppError.permissionDenied(.speech) }
        guard let recognizer = SFSpeechRecognizer(locale: locale) else {
            throw AppError.speechUnavailable("Speech recognition isn't available for \(locale.localizedString(forIdentifier: locale.identifier) ?? locale.identifier).")
        }
        guard recognizer.supportsOnDeviceRecognition else {
            throw AppError.speechUnavailable("On-device captions aren't available for \(locale.localizedString(forIdentifier: locale.identifier) ?? locale.identifier) on this device.")
        }
        let request = SFSpeechURLRecognitionRequest(url: audioURL)
        request.requiresOnDeviceRecognition = true
        request.shouldReportPartialResults = false
        request.addsPunctuation = true
        request.taskHint = .dictation

        let box = ResumeOnce()
        return try await withTaskCancellationHandler {
            try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<[CaptionWord], Error>) in
                let task = recognizer.recognitionTask(with: request) { result, error in
                    if let result, result.isFinal {
                        let words = result.bestTranscription.segments.map {
                            CaptionWord(text: $0.substring, start: $0.timestamp, end: $0.timestamp + max($0.duration, 0.05))
                        }
                        if box.claim() { continuation.resume(returning: words) }
                    } else if let error {
                        let ns = error as NSError
                        // 1110 = no speech detected
                        if ns.code == 1110 || ns.code == 203 {
                            if box.claim() { continuation.resume(returning: []) }
                        } else if box.claim() {
                            continuation.resume(throwing: AppError.speechUnavailable("Couldn't transcribe the audio: \(error.localizedDescription)"))
                        }
                    }
                }
                box.task = task
            }
        } onCancel: {
            box.task?.cancel()
        }
    }

    /// Groups words into readable caption lines.
    static func segments(from words: [CaptionWord], maxWords: Int = 5, maxDuration: Double = 2.6) -> [CaptionSegment] {
        var result: [CaptionSegment] = []
        var current: [CaptionWord] = []
        func flush() {
            guard let first = current.first, let last = current.last else { return }
            result.append(CaptionSegment(start: first.start, end: last.end, words: current))
            current.removeAll()
        }
        for word in words {
            let trimmed = word.text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            guard !trimmed.isEmpty else { continue }
            if let first = current.first {
                let gap = word.start - (current.last?.end ?? word.start)
                if current.count >= maxWords || word.end - first.start > maxDuration || gap > 0.8 {
                    flush()
                }
            }
            current.append(CaptionWord(text: trimmed, start: word.start, end: word.end))
            if trimmed.hasSuffix(".") || trimmed.hasSuffix("?") || trimmed.hasSuffix("!") {
                flush()
            }
        }
        flush()
        // Close tiny gaps so captions don't flicker between lines.
        for i in 0..<max(0, result.count - 1) where result[i + 1].start - result[i].end < 0.3 {
            result[i].end = result[i + 1].start
        }
        return result
    }
}

private final class ResumeOnce: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var done = false
    var task: SFSpeechRecognitionTask?

    func claim() -> Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        if done { return false }
        done = true
        return true
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/DerivedMediaService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import CoreImage
import Accelerate

/// PCM decode/encode helpers.
enum PCMIO {
    static func readInterleaved(url: URL, sampleRate: Double = 44100, channels: Int = 2) async throws -> [Float] {
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
        guard let track = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio).first else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("This clip has no audio.")
        }
        let reader = try AVAssetReader(asset: asset)
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
            AVSampleRateKey: sampleRate,
            AVNumberOfChannelsKey: channels,
            AVLinearPCMBitDepthKey: 32,
            AVLinearPCMIsFloatKey: true,
            AVLinearPCMIsBigEndianKey: false,
            AVLinearPCMIsNonInterleaved: false
        ]
        let output = AVAssetReaderTrackOutput(track: track, outputSettings: settings)
        output.alwaysCopiesSampleData = false
        reader.add(output)
        guard reader.startReading() else { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't read audio.") }
        var samples: [Float] = []
        while let sb = output.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            guard let block = CMSampleBufferGetDataBuffer(sb) else { continue }
            let length = CMBlockBufferGetDataLength(block)
            let count = length / MemoryLayout<Float>.size
            guard count > 0 else { continue }
            var chunk = [Float](repeating: 0, count: count)
            chunk.withUnsafeMutableBytes { raw in
                if let base = raw.baseAddress {
                    _ = CMBlockBufferCopyDataBytes(block, atOffset: 0, dataLength: length, destination: base)
                }
            }
            samples.append(contentsOf: chunk)
        }
        if reader.status == .failed { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't read audio.") }
        return samples
    }

    static func writeAAC(interleaved: [Float], sampleRate: Double = 44100, channels: Int = 2, to url: URL) throws {
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
        guard let format = AVAudioFormat(standardFormatWithSampleRate: sampleRate, channels: AVAudioChannelCount(channels)) else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("Unsupported audio format.")
        }
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatMPEG4AAC,
            AVSampleRateKey: sampleRate,
            AVNumberOfChannelsKey: channels,
            AVEncoderBitRateKey: 192_000
        ]
        let file = try AVAudioFile(forWriting: url, settings: settings, commonFormat: .pcmFormatFloat32, interleaved: false)
        let frames = interleaved.count / channels
        let chunk = 32768
        var offset = 0
        while offset < frames {
            let count = min(chunk, frames - offset)
            guard let buffer = AVAudioPCMBuffer(pcmFormat: format, frameCapacity: AVAudioFrameCount(count)),
                  let data = buffer.floatChannelData else { break }
            buffer.frameLength = AVAudioFrameCount(count)
            for ch in 0..<channels {
                let dst = data[ch]
                for i in 0..<count {
                    dst[i] = interleaved[(offset + i) * channels + ch]
                }
            }
            try file.write(from: buffer)
            offset += count
        }
    }
}

enum DerivedMediaService {
    // MARK: Reverse

    /// Writes a reversed (video-only) copy of `source`.
    static func reverseVideo(source: URL, destination: URL, progress: @escaping @Sendable (Double) -> Void) async throws {
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: destination)
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: source)
        guard let track = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video).first else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("This clip has no video to reverse.")
        }
        let duration = try await asset.load(.duration)
        let (natural, transform, nominalFPS) = try await track.load(.naturalSize, .preferredTransform, .nominalFrameRate)
        let fps = Double(nominalFPS > 0 ? min(nominalFPS, 60) : 30)
        let width = Int(natural.width), height = Int(natural.height)
        try StorageGuard.ensure(Int64(Double(width * height) * fps * 0.15 / 8 * duration.secondsOrZero))

        let writer = try AVAssetWriter(outputURL: destination, fileType: .mov)
        let bitrate = max(2_000_000, Int(Double(width * height) * fps * 0.15))
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVVideoCodecKey: AVVideoCodecType.h264,
            AVVideoWidthKey: width,
            AVVideoHeightKey: height,
            AVVideoCompressionPropertiesKey: [AVVideoAverageBitRateKey: bitrate]
        ]
        let input = AVAssetWriterInput(mediaType: .video, outputSettings: settings)
        input.transform = transform
        input.expectsMediaDataInRealTime = false
        let adaptor = AVAssetWriterInputPixelBufferAdaptor(assetWriterInput: input, sourcePixelBufferAttributes: nil)
        writer.add(input)
        guard writer.startWriting() else { throw writer.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't reverse clip.") }
        writer.startSession(atSourceTime: .zero)

        let total = duration.secondsOrZero
        let frameDuration = CMTime(value: 1000, timescale: CMTimeScale(fps * 1000))
        let chunkLength = 12.0 / fps
        var chunkEnd = total
        var lastWritten = CMTime.negativeInfinity

        while chunkEnd > 0.0001 {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            let chunkStart = max(0, chunkEnd - chunkLength)
            let reader = try AVAssetReader(asset: asset)
            reader.timeRange = CMTimeRange(start: CMTime(seconds: chunkStart, preferredTimescale: 60000),
                                           end: CMTime(seconds: chunkEnd, preferredTimescale: 60000))
            let output = AVAssetReaderTrackOutput(track: track, outputSettings: [
                kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: kCVPixelFormatType_420YpCbCr8BiPlanarVideoRange
            ])
            reader.add(output)
            guard reader.startReading() else { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't reverse clip.") }
            var frames: [(CVPixelBuffer, CMTime)] = []
            while let sb = output.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
                guard let pb = CMSampleBufferGetImageBuffer(sb) else { continue }
                let pts = CMSampleBufferGetPresentationTimeStamp(sb)
                let s = pts.secondsOrZero
                if s >= chunkStart - 0.0001 && s < chunkEnd - 0.0001 {
                    frames.append((pb, pts))
                }
            }
            reader.cancelReading()
            for (pb, pts) in frames.reversed() {
                var newTime = CMTimeSubtract(duration, CMTimeAdd(pts, frameDuration))
                if newTime < .zero { newTime = .zero }
                if newTime <= lastWritten { continue }
                while !input.isReadyForMoreMediaData {
                    try await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 2_000_000)
                }
                if !adaptor.append(pb, withPresentationTime: newTime) {
                    throw writer.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't reverse clip.")
                }
                lastWritten = newTime
            }
            chunkEnd = chunkStart
            progress(total > 0 ? 1 - chunkEnd / total : 1)
        }
        input.markAsFinished()
        await writer.finishWriting()
        if writer.status != .completed {
            throw writer.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't reverse clip.")
        }
    }

    static func reverseAudio(source: URL, destination: URL) async throws {
        var samples = try await PCMIO.readInterleaved(url: source)
        let channels = 2
        let frames = samples.count / channels
        var i = 0, j = frames - 1
        while i < j {
            for c in 0..<channels {
                samples.swapAt(i * channels + c, j * channels + c)
            }
            i += 1
            j -= 1
        }
        try PCMIO.writeAAC(interleaved: samples, to: destination)
    }

    // MARK: Noise reduction

    /// Basic noise reduction: 80 Hz high-pass, then a smoothed spectral-floor noise gate.
    static func denoise(source: URL, destination: URL) async throws {
        let channels = 2
        let sampleRate = 44100.0
        var samples = try await PCMIO.readInterleaved(url: source, sampleRate: sampleRate, channels: channels)
        let frames = samples.count / channels
        guard frames > 2048 else {
            try PCMIO.writeAAC(interleaved: samples, to: destination)
            return
        }

        // High-pass biquad (RBJ cookbook), per channel.
        let f0 = 80.0, q = 0.707
        let w0 = 2 * Double.pi * f0 / sampleRate
        let alpha = sin(w0) / (2 * q)
        let cw = cos(w0)
        let a0 = 1 + alpha
        let b0 = Float(((1 + cw) / 2) / a0), b1 = Float(-(1 + cw) / a0), b2 = Float(((1 + cw) / 2) / a0)
        let a1 = Float((-2 * cw) / a0), a2 = Float((1 - alpha) / a0)
        for c in 0..<channels {
            var x1: Float = 0, x2: Float = 0, y1: Float = 0, y2: Float = 0
            for n in 0..<frames {
                let idx = n * channels + c
                let x0 = samples[idx]
                let y0 = b0 * x0 + b1 * x1 + b2 * x2 - a1 * y1 - a2 * y2
                x2 = x1; x1 = x0
                y2 = y1; y1 = y0
                samples[idx] = y0
            }
        }

        // Noise gate driven by block RMS relative to the estimated noise floor.
        let block = 1024
        let blocks = frames / block
        var rms = [Float](repeating: 0, count: max(blocks, 1))
        for b in 0..<blocks {
            var sum: Float = 0
            for n in (b * block)..<((b + 1) * block) {
                for c in 0..<channels {
                    let v = samples[n * channels + c]
                    sum += v * v
                }
            }
            rms[b] = sqrt(sum / Float(block * channels))
        }
        let sorted = rms.sorted()
        let floor = max(sorted[Int(Double(sorted.count) * 0.1)], 1e-5)
        let threshold = floor * 2.5
        var gains = [Float](repeating: 1, count: max(blocks, 1))
        for b in 0..<blocks {
            let r = rms[b]
            gains[b] = r >= threshold ? 1 : max(0.12, pow(r / threshold, 2))
        }
        // Attack/release smoothing between blocks.
        for b in 1..<max(blocks, 1) {
            if gains[b] < gains[b - 1] {
                gains[b] = gains[b - 1] * 0.7 + gains[b] * 0.3
            }
        }
        for n in 0..<frames {
            let pos = Double(n) / Double(block) - 0.5
            let b0i = Int(Swift.max(0, Swift.min(Double(blocks - 1), pos.rounded(.down))))
            let b1i = Swift.min(blocks - 1, b0i + 1)
            let t = Float((pos - Double(b0i)).clamped(0, 1))
            let g = blocks > 0 ? gains[b0i] * (1 - t) + gains[b1i] * t : 1
            for c in 0..<channels {
                samples[n * channels + c] *= g
            }
        }
        try PCMIO.writeAAC(interleaved: samples, sampleRate: sampleRate, channels: channels, to: destination)
    }

    // MARK: Frames

    /// Full-resolution, correctly oriented frame grab.
    static func frame(url: URL, at time: Double) async throws -> CGImage {
        let generator = AVAssetImageGenerator(asset: AVURLAsset(url: url))
        generator.appliesPreferredTrackTransform = true
        generator.requestedTimeToleranceBefore = .zero
        generator.requestedTimeToleranceAfter = .zero
        let result = try await generator.image(at: time.cmTime)
        return result.image
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/ExportService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
@preconcurrency import AVFoundation
import Photos
import UIKit

enum ExportResolution: Int, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case p720 = 720
    case p1080 = 1080
    case p2160 = 2160

    var id: Int { rawValue }
    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .p720: return "720p"
        case .p1080: return "1080p"
        case .p2160: return "4K"
        }
    }
}

enum ExportCodec: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Hashable {
    case h264, hevc
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var title: String { self == .h264 ? "H.264" : "HEVC" }
    var avCodec: AVVideoCodecType { self == .h264 ? .h264 : .hevc }
}

struct ExportSettings: Hashable {
    var resolution: ExportResolution = .p1080
    var frameRate: Int = 30
    var codec: ExportCodec = .h264
    /// 0...1
    var quality: Double = 0.7

    func renderSize(for aspect: AspectRatio) -> CGSize {
        aspect.size(shortSide: CGFloat(resolution.rawValue))
    }

    func videoBitrate(for aspect: AspectRatio) -> Int {
        let size = renderSize(for: aspect)
        let bitsPerPixel = 0.05 + quality * 0.17
        var rate = Double(size.width * size.height) * Double(frameRate) * bitsPerPixel
        if codec == .hevc { rate *= 0.6 }
        return max(1_000_000, Int(rate))
    }

    static let audioBitrate = 192_000

    func estimatedBytes(duration: Double, aspect: AspectRatio) -> Int64 {
        let bits = Double(videoBitrate(for: aspect) + Self.audioBitrate) * duration
        return Int64(bits / 8 * 1.03)
    }
}

/// Renders the timeline at full quality with AVAssetReader → AVAssetWriter.
final class ExportService: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var reader: AVAssetReader?
    private var writer: AVAssetWriter?
    private var cancelled = false

    static func exportsDirectory() -> URL {
        let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("Exports", isDirectory: true)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        return dir
    }

    static func cleanExports() {
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: exportsDirectory())
    }

    static func fileName(for project: Project, ext: String) -> String {
        let safe = project.name.components(separatedBy: CharacterSet.alphanumerics.union(.whitespaces).inverted).joined()
        let base = safe.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty ? "Cutline" : safe.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.dateFormat = "HHmmss"
        return "\(base) \(f.string(from: Date())).\(ext)"
    }

    func cancel() {
        lock.lock()
        cancelled = true
        let r = reader, w = writer
        lock.unlock()
        r?.cancelReading()
        w?.cancelWriting()
    }

    private var isCancelled: Bool {
        lock.withLock { cancelled }
    }

    // MARK: Video

    func exportVideo(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, settings: ExportSettings,
                     progress: @escaping @Sendable (Double) -> Void) async throws -> URL {
        let duration = project.duration
        guard duration > 0.05 else { throw AppError.exportFailed("The timeline is empty.") }
        try StorageGuard.ensure(settings.estimatedBytes(duration: duration, aspect: project.canvas.aspect))

        let renderSize = settings.renderSize(for: project.canvas.aspect)
        let options = BuildOptions(isExport: true, renderSize: renderSize, frameRate: Int32(settings.frameRate))
        let built = try await CompositionBuilder.build(project: project, paths: paths, options: options)
        let composition = built.composition

        let url = Self.exportsDirectory().appendingPathComponent(Self.fileName(for: project, ext: "mp4"))
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)

        let reader = try AVAssetReader(asset: composition)
        let videoTracks = try await composition.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video)
        let audioTracks = try await composition.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio)

        let videoOutput = AVAssetReaderVideoCompositionOutput(videoTracks: videoTracks, videoSettings: [
            kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA
        ])
        videoOutput.videoComposition = built.videoComposition
        videoOutput.alwaysCopiesSampleData = false
        guard reader.canAdd(videoOutput) else { throw AppError.exportFailed("Couldn't read the timeline.") }
        reader.add(videoOutput)

        var audioOutput: AVAssetReaderAudioMixOutput?
        if !audioTracks.isEmpty {
            let out = AVAssetReaderAudioMixOutput(audioTracks: audioTracks, audioSettings: [
                AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
                AVSampleRateKey: 44100,
                AVNumberOfChannelsKey: 2,
                AVLinearPCMBitDepthKey: 16,
                AVLinearPCMIsFloatKey: false,
                AVLinearPCMIsBigEndianKey: false,
                AVLinearPCMIsNonInterleaved: false
            ])
            out.audioMix = built.audioMix
            out.audioTimePitchAlgorithm = .spectral
            out.alwaysCopiesSampleData = false
            if reader.canAdd(out) {
                reader.add(out)
                audioOutput = out
            }
        }

        let writer = try AVAssetWriter(outputURL: url, fileType: .mp4)
        writer.shouldOptimizeForNetworkUse = true
        var compression: [String: Any] = [
            AVVideoAverageBitRateKey: settings.videoBitrate(for: project.canvas.aspect),
            AVVideoExpectedSourceFrameRateKey: settings.frameRate,
            AVVideoMaxKeyFrameIntervalKey: settings.frameRate * 2
        ]
        if settings.codec == .h264 {
            compression[AVVideoProfileLevelKey] = AVVideoProfileLevelH264HighAutoLevel
        }
        let videoInput = AVAssetWriterInput(mediaType: .video, outputSettings: [
            AVVideoCodecKey: settings.codec.avCodec,
            AVVideoWidthKey: Int(renderSize.width),
            AVVideoHeightKey: Int(renderSize.height),
            AVVideoCompressionPropertiesKey: compression
        ])
        videoInput.expectsMediaDataInRealTime = false
        guard writer.canAdd(videoInput) else { throw AppError.exportFailed("This device can't encode \(settings.codec.title) at \(settings.resolution.title).") }
        writer.add(videoInput)

        var audioInput: AVAssetWriterInput?
        if audioOutput != nil {
            let input = AVAssetWriterInput(mediaType: .audio, outputSettings: [
                AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatMPEG4AAC,
                AVSampleRateKey: 44100,
                AVNumberOfChannelsKey: 2,
                AVEncoderBitRateKey: ExportSettings.audioBitrate
            ])
            input.expectsMediaDataInRealTime = false
            if writer.canAdd(input) {
                writer.add(input)
                audioInput = input
            }
        }

        let wasCancelled = lock.withLock { () -> Bool in
            self.reader = reader
            self.writer = writer
            return cancelled
        }
        if wasCancelled { throw AppError.cancelled }

        guard reader.startReading() else { throw AppError.exportFailed(reader.error?.localizedDescription ?? "Couldn't read the timeline.") }
        guard writer.startWriting() else { throw AppError.exportFailed(writer.error?.localizedDescription ?? "Couldn't write the file.") }
        writer.startSession(atSourceTime: .zero)

        let total = max(built.duration, 0.001)
        let group = DispatchGroup()
        let videoQueue = DispatchQueue(label: "app.cutline.export.video")
        let audioQueue = DispatchQueue(label: "app.cutline.export.audio")

        group.enter()
        let videoDone = OnceFlag()
        videoInput.requestMediaDataWhenReady(on: videoQueue) {
            while videoInput.isReadyForMoreMediaData {
                if let sample = videoOutput.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
                    let t = CMSampleBufferGetPresentationTimeStamp(sample).secondsOrZero
                    if !videoInput.append(sample) {
                        reader.cancelReading()
                        videoInput.markAsFinished()
                        if videoDone.set() { group.leave() }
                        return
                    }
                    progress(min(t / total, 0.99))
                } else {
                    videoInput.markAsFinished()
                    if videoDone.set() { group.leave() }
                    return
                }
            }
        }

        if let audioInput, let audioOutput {
            group.enter()
            let audioDone = OnceFlag()
            audioInput.requestMediaDataWhenReady(on: audioQueue) {
                while audioInput.isReadyForMoreMediaData {
                    if let sample = audioOutput.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
                        if !audioInput.append(sample) {
                            audioInput.markAsFinished()
                            if audioDone.set() { group.leave() }
                            return
                        }
                    } else {
                        audioInput.markAsFinished()
                        if audioDone.set() { group.leave() }
                        return
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        await withCheckedContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>) in
            group.notify(queue: .global()) { continuation.resume() }
        }

        if isCancelled || reader.status == .cancelled {
            writer.cancelWriting()
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
            throw AppError.cancelled
        }
        if reader.status == .failed {
            writer.cancelWriting()
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
            throw AppError.exportFailed(reader.error?.localizedDescription ?? "Rendering failed.")
        }
        await writer.finishWriting()
        guard writer.status == .completed else {
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
            throw AppError.exportFailed(writer.error?.localizedDescription ?? "Couldn't finish the file.")
        }
        progress(1)
        return url
    }

    // MARK: Audio only

    func exportAudio(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, includeMusic: Bool = true) async throws -> URL {
        guard project.duration > 0.05 else { throw AppError.exportFailed("The timeline is empty.") }
        let options = BuildOptions(isExport: true, renderSize: project.canvas.aspect.size(shortSide: 360), frameRate: 30, includeMusic: includeMusic)
        let built = try await CompositionBuilder.build(project: project, paths: paths, options: options)
        let audioTracks = try await built.composition.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio)
        guard !audioTracks.isEmpty else { throw AppError.exportFailed("There's no audio in this project.") }
        try StorageGuard.ensure(Int64(Double(ExportSettings.audioBitrate) * project.duration / 8))
        let url = Self.exportsDirectory().appendingPathComponent(Self.fileName(for: project, ext: "m4a"))
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
        guard let session = AVAssetExportSession(asset: built.composition, presetName: AVAssetExportPresetAppleM4A) else {
            throw AppError.exportFailed("Audio export isn't available.")
        }
        session.outputURL = url
        session.outputFileType = .m4a
        session.audioMix = built.audioMix
        session.audioTimePitchAlgorithm = .spectral
        if isCancelled { throw AppError.cancelled }
        await withCheckedContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>) in
            session.exportAsynchronously { continuation.resume() }
        }
        guard session.status == .completed else {
            throw AppError.exportFailed(session.error?.localizedDescription ?? "Audio export failed.")
        }
        return url
    }

    // MARK: Still frame

    func exportStill(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, at time: Double, resolution: ExportResolution) async throws -> URL {
        guard project.duration > 0.01 else { throw AppError.exportFailed("The timeline is empty.") }
        let size = project.canvas.aspect.size(shortSide: CGFloat(resolution.rawValue))
        let options = BuildOptions(isExport: true, renderSize: size, frameRate: 30)
        let built = try await CompositionBuilder.build(project: project, paths: paths, options: options)
        let generator = AVAssetImageGenerator(asset: built.composition)
        generator.videoComposition = built.videoComposition
        generator.requestedTimeToleranceBefore = .zero
        generator.requestedTimeToleranceAfter = .zero
        generator.maximumSize = size
        let t = min(max(time, 0), max(project.duration - 0.02, 0))
        let result = try await generator.image(at: t.cmTime)
        guard let data = UIImage(cgImage: result.image).jpegData(compressionQuality: 0.95) else {
            throw AppError.exportFailed("Couldn't encode the frame.")
        }
        let url = Self.exportsDirectory().appendingPathComponent(Self.fileName(for: project, ext: "jpg"))
        try data.write(to: url, options: .atomic)
        return url
    }

    // MARK: Saving

    static func saveToPhotos(_ url: URL) async throws {
        guard await Permissions.photosAddOnly() else { throw AppError.permissionDenied(.photos) }
        let ext = url.pathExtension.lowercased()
        try await PHPhotoLibrary.shared().performChanges {
            if ext == "jpg" || ext == "jpeg" || ext == "png" {
                _ = PHAssetCreationRequest.creationRequestForAssetFromImage(atFileURL: url)
            } else {
                _ = PHAssetCreationRequest.creationRequestForAssetFromVideo(atFileURL: url)
            }
        }
    }
}

final class OnceFlag: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var done = false

    func set() -> Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        if done { return false }
        done = true
        return true
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/MediaImportService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import PhotosUI
import SwiftUI
import UniformTypeIdentifiers
import ImageIO
import UIKit

/// A movie file received from the Photos picker, copied into a temporary location.
struct PickedMovie: Transferable {
    let url: URL

    static var transferRepresentation: some TransferRepresentation {
        FileRepresentation(contentType: .movie) { movie in
            SentTransferredFile(movie.url)
        } importing: { received in
            let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("Imports", isDirectory: true)
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            let ext = received.file.pathExtension.isEmpty ? "mov" : received.file.pathExtension
            let dest = dir.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString).appendingPathExtension(ext)
            try FileManager.default.copyItem(at: received.file, to: dest)
            return PickedMovie(url: dest)
        }
    }
}

struct ImportResult {
    var items: [MediaItem] = []
    var failures: [String] = []
}

enum MediaImportService {
    static let proxyThreshold: Double = 1920 * 1088

    // MARK: Photos picker

    static func importPickerItems(_ items: [PhotosPickerItem], into paths: ProjectPaths,
                                  progress: @escaping @MainActor (Int, Int) -> Void) async -> ImportResult {
        var result = ImportResult()
        for (index, item) in items.enumerated() {
            await progress(index, items.count)
            do {
                let isMovie = item.supportedContentTypes.contains { $0.conforms(to: .movie) || $0.conforms(to: .video) }
                if isMovie {
                    guard let movie = try await item.loadTransferable(type: PickedMovie.self) else {
                        throw AppError.unsupportedFormat("Video \(index + 1)")
                    }
                    let media = try await importVideo(from: movie.url, originalName: "Video \(index + 1)", into: paths, move: true)
                    result.items.append(media)
                } else {
                    guard let data = try await item.loadTransferable(type: Data.self) else {
                        throw AppError.unsupportedFormat("Photo \(index + 1)")
                    }
                    let media = try importImage(data: data, originalName: "Photo \(index + 1)", into: paths)
                    result.items.append(media)
                }
            } catch {
                let message = (error as? AppError)?.errorDescription ?? error.localizedDescription
                result.failures.append(message)
            }
        }
        await progress(items.count, items.count)
        return result
    }

    // MARK: Video

    static func importVideo(from url: URL, originalName: String, into paths: ProjectPaths, move: Bool) async throws -> MediaItem {
        try paths.ensureDirectories()
        try StorageGuard.ensure(StorageGuard.fileSize(url))
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
        let playable = (try? await asset.load(.isPlayable)) ?? false
        let videoTracks = (try? await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video)) ?? []
        guard playable, let track = videoTracks.first else {
            throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(originalName)
        }
        let duration = try await asset.load(.duration).secondsOrZero
        guard duration > 0.05 else { throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(originalName) }
        let (natural, transform) = try await track.load(.naturalSize, .preferredTransform)
        let fps = (try? await track.load(.nominalFrameRate)) ?? 30
        let oriented = natural.applying(transform)
        let hasAudio = !((try? await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio)) ?? []).isEmpty

        let ext = url.pathExtension.isEmpty ? "mov" : url.pathExtension.lowercased()
        let id = UUID()
        let fileName = "\(id.uuidString).\(ext)"
        let dest = paths.media(fileName)
        if move {
            try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: url, to: dest)
        } else {
            try FileManager.default.copyItem(at: url, to: dest)
        }
        return MediaItem(id: id, fileName: fileName, proxyFileName: nil, originalName: originalName, kind: .video,
                         duration: duration, width: Double(abs(oriented.width)), height: Double(abs(oriented.height)),
                         hasAudio: hasAudio, hasVideo: true, frameRate: Double(fps > 0 ? fps : 30), beats: nil)
    }

    /// Creates a 720p editing proxy for large videos. Returns the proxy file name, or nil if not needed.
    static func makeProxyIfNeeded(for media: MediaItem, paths: ProjectPaths) async -> String? {
        guard media.kind == .video, media.width * media.height > proxyThreshold else { return nil }
        let name = MediaResolver.proxyName(media)
        let dest = paths.cache(name)
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: dest.path) { return name }
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: paths.media(media.fileName))
        guard let session = AVAssetExportSession(asset: asset, presetName: AVAssetExportPreset1280x720) else { return nil }
        session.outputURL = dest
        session.outputFileType = .mp4
        session.shouldOptimizeForNetworkUse = false
        await withCheckedContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>) in
            session.exportAsynchronously { continuation.resume() }
        }
        if session.status == .completed { return name }
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: dest)
        return nil
    }

    // MARK: Image

    static func importImage(data: Data, originalName: String, into paths: ProjectPaths) throws -> MediaItem {
        try paths.ensureDirectories()
        try StorageGuard.ensure(Int64(data.count))
        guard let source = CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, nil) else {
            throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(originalName)
        }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: 4096
        ]
        guard let cg = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(source, 0, options as CFDictionary) else {
            throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(originalName)
        }
        let image = UIImage(cgImage: cg)
        let hasAlpha: Bool = {
            switch cg.alphaInfo {
            case .first, .last, .premultipliedFirst, .premultipliedLast: return true
            default: return false
            }
        }()
        let id = UUID()
        let fileName: String
        let encoded: Data?
        if hasAlpha {
            fileName = "\(id.uuidString).png"
            encoded = image.pngData()
        } else {
            fileName = "\(id.uuidString).jpg"
            encoded = image.jpegData(compressionQuality: 0.92)
        }
        guard let out = encoded else { throw AppError.importFailed("Couldn't read “\(originalName)”.") }
        try out.write(to: paths.media(fileName), options: .atomic)
        return MediaItem(id: id, fileName: fileName, proxyFileName: nil, originalName: originalName, kind: .image,
                         duration: 3, width: Double(cg.width), height: Double(cg.height),
                         hasAudio: false, hasVideo: true, frameRate: 30, beats: nil)
    }

    static func importImage(cgImage: CGImage, originalName: String, into paths: ProjectPaths) throws -> MediaItem {
        guard let data = UIImage(cgImage: cgImage).pngData() else { throw AppError.importFailed("Couldn't save frame.") }
        return try importImage(data: data, originalName: originalName, into: paths)
    }

    // MARK: Audio

    static func importAudio(from url: URL, into paths: ProjectPaths, move: Bool) async throws -> MediaItem {
        try paths.ensureDirectories()
        let scoped = url.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
        defer { if scoped { url.stopAccessingSecurityScopedResource() } }
        try StorageGuard.ensure(StorageGuard.fileSize(url))
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
        let audioTracks = (try? await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio)) ?? []
        guard !audioTracks.isEmpty else { throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(url.lastPathComponent) }
        let duration = try await asset.load(.duration).secondsOrZero
        guard duration > 0.05 else { throw AppError.unsupportedFormat(url.lastPathComponent) }
        let id = UUID()
        let ext = url.pathExtension.isEmpty ? "m4a" : url.pathExtension.lowercased()
        let fileName = "\(id.uuidString).\(ext)"
        let dest = paths.media(fileName)
        if move {
            try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: url, to: dest)
        } else {
            try FileManager.default.copyItem(at: url, to: dest)
        }
        let name = url.deletingPathExtension().lastPathComponent
        return MediaItem(id: id, fileName: fileName, proxyFileName: nil, originalName: name, kind: .audio,
                         duration: duration, width: 0, height: 0, hasAudio: true, hasVideo: false, frameRate: 0, beats: nil)
    }
}

/// Resolves which file on disk backs a clip, honoring proxies and derived (reversed / denoised) media.
struct MediaResolver {
    let paths: ProjectPaths
    let media: [UUID: MediaItem]
    let preferProxy: Bool

    init(paths: ProjectPaths, media: [MediaItem], preferProxy: Bool) {
        self.paths = paths
        self.preferProxy = preferProxy
        var dict: [UUID: MediaItem] = [:]
        for m in media { dict[m.id] = m }
        self.media = dict
    }

    static func proxyName(_ m: MediaItem) -> String { "proxy_\(m.id.uuidString).mp4" }
    static func reversedVideoName(_ m: MediaItem) -> String { "rev_\(m.id.uuidString).mov" }
    static func reversedProxyName(_ m: MediaItem) -> String { "revproxy_\(m.id.uuidString).mov" }
    static func reversedAudioName(_ m: MediaItem) -> String { "revaudio_\(m.id.uuidString).m4a" }
    static func denoisedName(_ m: MediaItem, reversed: Bool) -> String { "dn_\(reversed ? "rev_" : "")\(m.id.uuidString).m4a" }

    private func exists(_ url: URL) -> Bool { FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) }

    func originalURL(_ m: MediaItem) -> URL? {
        let u = paths.media(m.fileName)
        return exists(u) ? u : nil
    }

    func isMissing(_ mediaID: UUID) -> Bool {
        guard let m = media[mediaID] else { return true }
        return originalURL(m) == nil
    }

    func videoURL(for clip: Clip) -> URL? {
        guard let m = media[clip.mediaID], m.kind == .video else { return nil }
        if clip.reversed {
            if preferProxy {
                let rp = paths.cache(Self.reversedProxyName(m))
                if exists(rp) { return rp }
            }
            let r = paths.cache(Self.reversedVideoName(m))
            if exists(r) { return r }
        }
        if preferProxy {
            let pu = paths.cache(Self.proxyName(m))
            if exists(pu) { return pu }
        }
        return originalURL(m)
    }

    /// The best file for thumbnails (proxy when available).
    func thumbnailURL(for mediaID: UUID, reversed: Bool) -> URL? {
        guard let m = media[mediaID] else { return nil }
        if m.kind == .image { return originalURL(m) }
        if reversed {
            let rp = paths.cache(Self.reversedProxyName(m))
            if exists(rp) { return rp }
            let r = paths.cache(Self.reversedVideoName(m))
            if exists(r) { return r }
        }
        let pu = paths.cache(Self.proxyName(m))
        if exists(pu) { return pu }
        return originalURL(m)
    }

    func imageURL(for mediaID: UUID?) -> URL? {
        guard let id = mediaID, let m = media[id] else { return nil }
        return originalURL(m)
    }

    func audioURL(mediaID: UUID, reversed: Bool, denoise: Bool) -> URL? {
        guard let m = media[mediaID], m.hasAudio else { return nil }
        if denoise {
            let d = paths.cache(Self.denoisedName(m, reversed: reversed))
            if exists(d) { return d }
        }
        if reversed {
            let r = paths.cache(Self.reversedAudioName(m))
            if exists(r) { return r }
        }
        return originalURL(m)
    }
}

/// Caches loaded AVURLAssets and their track info.
final class AssetCache: @unchecked Sendable {
    struct Info {
        let asset: AVURLAsset
        let video: AVAssetTrack?
        let audio: AVAssetTrack?
        let duration: CMTime
        let videoRange: CMTimeRange
        let audioRange: CMTimeRange
        let orientation: CGImagePropertyOrientation
        let frameDuration: CMTime
    }

    static let shared = AssetCache()
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var cache: [URL: Info] = [:]

    func info(for url: URL) async throws -> Info {
        if let hit = lock.withLock({ cache[url] }) {
            return hit
        }

        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url, options: [AVURLAssetPreferPreciseDurationAndTimingKey: true])
        let duration = try await asset.load(.duration)
        let video = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video).first
        let audio = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio).first
        var orientation: CGImagePropertyOrientation = .up
        var videoRange = CMTimeRange(start: .zero, duration: duration)
        var frameDuration = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 30)
        if let v = video {
            let (transform, range, fps) = try await v.load(.preferredTransform, .timeRange, .nominalFrameRate)
            orientation = Self.orientation(from: transform)
            videoRange = range
            if fps > 0 { frameDuration = CMTime(value: 1000, timescale: CMTimeScale(max(1, (fps * 1000).rounded()))) }
        }
        var audioRange = CMTimeRange(start: .zero, duration: duration)
        if let a = audio {
            audioRange = try await a.load(.timeRange)
        }
        let info = Info(asset: asset, video: video, audio: audio, duration: duration, videoRange: videoRange,
                        audioRange: audioRange, orientation: orientation, frameDuration: frameDuration)
        lock.withLock { cache[url] = info }
        return info
    }

    func invalidate(_ url: URL) {
        lock.lock()
        cache[url] = nil
        lock.unlock()
    }

    static func orientation(from t: CGAffineTransform) -> CGImagePropertyOrientation {
        let a = t.a.rounded(), b = t.b.rounded(), c = t.c.rounded(), d = t.d.rounded()
        switch (a, b, c, d) {
        case (0, 1, -1, 0): return .right
        case (0, -1, 1, 0): return .left
        case (-1, 0, 0, -1): return .down
        case (-1, 0, 0, 1): return .upMirrored
        case (1, 0, 0, -1): return .downMirrored
        case (0, 1, 1, 0): return .leftMirrored
        case (0, -1, -1, 0): return .rightMirrored
        default: return .up
        }
    }
}

/// Generates a tiny black video used as the composition's timing backbone,
/// so photos, text and gaps always have frames to render on.
actor BlankVideoProvider {
    static let shared = BlankVideoProvider()
    private var cached: URL?

    func url() async throws -> URL {
        if let cached, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: cached.path) { return cached }
        let caches = FileManager.default.urls(for: .cachesDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
        let url = caches.appendingPathComponent("cutline_blank_v2.mov")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: url.path) {
            let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
            if let tracks = try? await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video), !tracks.isEmpty {
                cached = url
                return url
            }
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
        }
        try await make(url)
        cached = url
        return url
    }

    private func make(_ url: URL) async throws {
        let writer = try AVAssetWriter(outputURL: url, fileType: .mov)
        let size = 64
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVVideoCodecKey: AVVideoCodecType.h264,
            AVVideoWidthKey: size,
            AVVideoHeightKey: size
        ]
        let input = AVAssetWriterInput(mediaType: .video, outputSettings: settings)
        input.expectsMediaDataInRealTime = false
        let adaptor = AVAssetWriterInputPixelBufferAdaptor(assetWriterInput: input, sourcePixelBufferAttributes: [
            kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA,
            kCVPixelBufferWidthKey as String: size,
            kCVPixelBufferHeightKey as String: size
        ])
        writer.add(input)
        guard writer.startWriting() else { throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't prepare the renderer.") }
        writer.startSession(atSourceTime: .zero)

        var buffer: CVPixelBuffer?
        CVPixelBufferCreate(kCFAllocatorDefault, size, size, kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA, nil, &buffer)
        guard let pb = buffer else { throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't prepare the renderer.") }
        CVPixelBufferLockBaseAddress(pb, [])
        if let base = CVPixelBufferGetBaseAddress(pb) {
            memset(base, 0, CVPixelBufferGetBytesPerRow(pb) * size)
        }
        CVPixelBufferUnlockBaseAddress(pb, [])

        for frame in 0..<30 {
            while !input.isReadyForMoreMediaData {
                try await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 2_000_000)
            }
            adaptor.append(pb, withPresentationTime: CMTime(value: CMTimeValue(frame), timescale: 30))
        }
        input.markAsFinished()
        writer.endSession(atSourceTime: CMTime(value: 30, timescale: 30))
        await writer.finishWriting()
        if writer.status != .completed {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't prepare the renderer.")
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/Permissions.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Speech
import Photos
import UIKit

enum Permissions {
    static func microphone() async -> Bool {
        switch AVAudioApplication.shared.recordPermission {
        case .granted: return true
        case .denied: return false
        default: return await AVAudioApplication.requestRecordPermission()
        }
    }

    static func speech() async -> Bool {
        switch SFSpeechRecognizer.authorizationStatus() {
        case .authorized: return true
        case .denied, .restricted: return false
        default:
            return await withCheckedContinuation { continuation in
                SFSpeechRecognizer.requestAuthorization { status in
                    continuation.resume(returning: status == .authorized)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    static func photosAddOnly() async -> Bool {
        let current = PHPhotoLibrary.authorizationStatus(for: .addOnly)
        if current == .authorized || current == .limited { return true }
        if current == .denied || current == .restricted { return false }
        let status = await PHPhotoLibrary.requestAuthorization(for: .addOnly)
        return status == .authorized || status == .limited
    }

    @MainActor
    static func openSettings() {
        if let url = URL(string: UIApplication.openSettingsURLString) {
            UIApplication.shared.open(url)
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/ProjectStore.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import Observation
import UIKit

struct ProjectPaths: Hashable, Sendable {
    let root: URL

    var projectFile: URL { root.appendingPathComponent("project.json") }
    var backupFile: URL { root.appendingPathComponent("project.backup.json") }
    var mediaDir: URL { root.appendingPathComponent("Media", isDirectory: true) }
    var cacheDir: URL { root.appendingPathComponent("Cache", isDirectory: true) }
    var thumbnailFile: URL { root.appendingPathComponent("thumbnail.jpg") }

    func media(_ name: String) -> URL { mediaDir.appendingPathComponent(name) }
    func cache(_ name: String) -> URL { cacheDir.appendingPathComponent(name) }

    func ensureDirectories() throws {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try fm.createDirectory(at: mediaDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try fm.createDirectory(at: cacheDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
    }
}

struct ProjectSummary: Identifiable, Hashable, Sendable {
    let id: UUID
    var name: String
    var modifiedAt: Date
    var aspect: AspectRatio
    var duration: Double
    var clipCount: Int
    var thumbnailURL: URL
    var isCorrupted: Bool
}

/// Reads and writes project files. All methods are safe to call from any thread.
enum ProjectFileIO {
    static func encode(_ project: Project) throws -> Data {
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        return try encoder.encode(project)
    }

    static func decode(_ data: Data) throws -> Project {
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        return try decoder.decode(Project.self, from: data)
    }

    /// Atomically writes the project, keeping the previous version as a backup.
    static func write(_ data: Data, to paths: ProjectPaths) throws {
        try paths.ensureDirectories()
        let fm = FileManager.default
        if fm.fileExists(atPath: paths.projectFile.path),
           let current = try? Data(contentsOf: paths.projectFile),
           (try? decode(current)) != nil {
            try? current.write(to: paths.backupFile, options: .atomic)
        }
        try data.write(to: paths.projectFile, options: .atomic)
    }

    /// Loads a project, falling back to the backup if the main file is damaged.
    static func read(_ paths: ProjectPaths, id: UUID) throws -> (Project, Bool) {
        if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: paths.projectFile), var p = try? decode(data) {
            p.id = id
            return (p, false)
        }
        if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: paths.backupFile), var p = try? decode(data) {
            p.id = id
            try? data.write(to: paths.projectFile, options: .atomic)
            return (p, true)
        }
        throw AppError.corruptedProject
    }

    static func scanSummaries(root: URL) -> [ProjectSummary] {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        guard let dirs = try? fm.contentsOfDirectory(at: root, includingPropertiesForKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey], options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) else {
            return []
        }
        var result: [ProjectSummary] = []
        for dir in dirs {
            guard let id = UUID(uuidString: dir.lastPathComponent) else { continue }
            let paths = ProjectPaths(root: dir)
            if let loaded = try? read(paths, id: id) {
                let p = loaded.0
                result.append(ProjectSummary(id: id, name: p.name, modifiedAt: p.modifiedAt, aspect: p.canvas.aspect,
                                             duration: p.duration, clipCount: p.clips.count + p.overlayClips.count,
                                             thumbnailURL: paths.thumbnailFile, isCorrupted: false))
            } else {
                let date = (try? dir.resourceValues(forKeys: [.contentModificationDateKey]).contentModificationDate) ?? Date.distantPast
                result.append(ProjectSummary(id: id, name: "Damaged project", modifiedAt: date, aspect: .portrait9x16,
                                             duration: 0, clipCount: 0, thumbnailURL: paths.thumbnailFile, isCorrupted: true))
            }
        }
        return result.sorted { $0.modifiedAt > $1.modifiedAt }
    }
}

@MainActor
@Observable
final class ProjectStore {
    private(set) var summaries: [ProjectSummary] = []
    private(set) var hasLoaded = false
    /// Bumped whenever thumbnails are rewritten, so cards reload them.
    private(set) var thumbnailRevision = 0

    let rootURL: URL

    init() {
        let docs = FileManager.default.urls(for: .documentDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
        rootURL = docs.appendingPathComponent("Projects", isDirectory: true)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: rootURL, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
    }

    func paths(for id: UUID) -> ProjectPaths {
        ProjectPaths(root: rootURL.appendingPathComponent(id.uuidString, isDirectory: true))
    }

    func refresh() async {
        let root = rootURL
        let list = await Task.detached(priority: .userInitiated) { ProjectFileIO.scanSummaries(root: root) }.value
        summaries = list
        hasLoaded = true
    }

    func noteThumbnailChanged() {
        thumbnailRevision += 1
    }

    func nextProjectName() -> String {
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.dateFormat = "MMM d"
        let base = "Edit · \(f.string(from: Date()))"
        let existing = Set(summaries.map(\.name))
        if !existing.contains(base) { return base }
        var n = 2
        while existing.contains("\(base) (\(n))") { n += 1 }
        return "\(base) (\(n))"
    }

    func createProject(aspect: AspectRatio) throws -> Project {
        let project = Project(name: nextProjectName(), aspect: aspect)
        let paths = paths(for: project.id)
        try paths.ensureDirectories()
        try ProjectFileIO.write(ProjectFileIO.encode(project), to: paths)
        return project
    }

    func load(_ id: UUID) throws -> (Project, Bool) {
        try ProjectFileIO.read(paths(for: id), id: id)
    }

    func rename(_ id: UUID, to name: String) async {
        let trimmed = name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty, let loaded = try? load(id) else { return }
        var project = loaded.0
        project.name = trimmed
        project.modifiedAt = Date()
        if let data = try? ProjectFileIO.encode(project) {
            try? ProjectFileIO.write(data, to: paths(for: id))
        }
        await refresh()
    }

    func duplicate(_ id: UUID) async throws {
        let source = paths(for: id)
        let newID = UUID()
        let dest = paths(for: newID)
        let fm = FileManager.default
        try StorageGuard.ensure(directorySize(source.root))
        try fm.copyItem(at: source.root, to: dest.root)
        var (project, _) = try ProjectFileIO.read(dest, id: newID)
        project.id = newID
        project.name = project.name + " copy"
        project.createdAt = Date()
        project.modifiedAt = Date()
        try ProjectFileIO.write(ProjectFileIO.encode(project), to: dest)
        await refresh()
    }

    func delete(_ id: UUID) async {
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: paths(for: id).root)
        await refresh()
    }

    func exists(_ id: UUID) -> Bool {
        FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: paths(for: id).root.path)
    }

    private func directorySize(_ url: URL) -> Int64 {
        guard let e = FileManager.default.enumerator(at: url, includingPropertiesForKeys: [.fileSizeKey]) else { return 0 }
        var total: Int64 = 0
        for case let file as URL in e {
            total += Int64((try? file.resourceValues(forKeys: [.fileSizeKey]).fileSize) ?? 0)
        }
        return total
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/ProjectUndoManager.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

/// Unlimited snapshot-based undo/redo for project edits.
@Observable
final class ProjectUndoManager {
    private struct Entry {
        let name: String
        let project: Project
    }

    private var undoStack: [Entry] = []
    private var redoStack: [Entry] = []
    @ObservationIgnored private var lastCoalesceKey: String?
    @ObservationIgnored private var lastRecordDate = Date.distantPast

    var canUndo: Bool { !undoStack.isEmpty }
    var canRedo: Bool { !redoStack.isEmpty }
    var undoName: String? { undoStack.last?.name }
    var redoName: String? { redoStack.last?.name }

    /// Records the state *before* an edit. Rapid edits with the same coalesce key
    /// (e.g. dragging a slider) collapse into one undo step.
    func record(_ before: Project, name: String, coalesceKey: String? = nil) {
        let now = Date()
        if let key = coalesceKey, key == lastCoalesceKey, now.timeIntervalSince(lastRecordDate) < 1.5 {
            lastRecordDate = now
            return
        }
        undoStack.append(Entry(name: name, project: before))
        redoStack.removeAll()
        lastCoalesceKey = coalesceKey
        lastRecordDate = now
    }

    func breakCoalescing() {
        lastCoalesceKey = nil
    }

    func undo(current: Project) -> (Project, String)? {
        guard let entry = undoStack.popLast() else { return nil }
        redoStack.append(Entry(name: entry.name, project: current))
        lastCoalesceKey = nil
        return (entry.project, entry.name)
    }

    func redo(current: Project) -> (Project, String)? {
        guard let entry = redoStack.popLast() else { return nil }
        undoStack.append(Entry(name: entry.name, project: current))
        lastCoalesceKey = nil
        return (entry.project, entry.name)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/StabilizationService.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Vision
import CoreImage

/// Estimates camera shake with Vision image registration and produces smoothing offsets.
enum StabilizationService {
    static func analyze(url: URL, sourceStart: Double, sourceEnd: Double,
                        progress: @escaping @Sendable (Double) -> Void) async throws -> StabilizationData {
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
        guard let track = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .video).first else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("This clip has no video to stabilize.")
        }
        let (transform, nominalFPS) = try await track.load(.preferredTransform, .nominalFrameRate)
        let orientation = AssetCache.orientation(from: transform)
        let fps = Double(nominalFPS > 0 ? nominalFPS : 30)

        let reader = try AVAssetReader(asset: asset)
        reader.timeRange = CMTimeRange(start: CMTime(seconds: sourceStart, preferredTimescale: 60000),
                                       end: CMTime(seconds: sourceEnd, preferredTimescale: 60000))
        let output = AVAssetReaderTrackOutput(track: track, outputSettings: [
            kCVPixelBufferPixelFormatTypeKey as String: kCVPixelFormatType_32BGRA
        ])
        output.alwaysCopiesSampleData = false
        reader.add(output)
        guard reader.startReading() else { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't analyze clip.") }

        var times: [Double] = []
        var pathX: [Double] = []
        var pathY: [Double] = []
        var previous: CIImage?
        var accX = 0.0, accY = 0.0
        var analysisWidth: CGFloat = 320
        var analysisHeight: CGFloat = 320
        let span = max(sourceEnd - sourceStart, 0.01)

        while let sb = output.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            guard let pb = CMSampleBufferGetImageBuffer(sb) else { continue }
            let t = CMSampleBufferGetPresentationTimeStamp(sb).secondsOrZero
            var image = CIImage(cvPixelBuffer: pb).oriented(orientation)
            image = image.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(translationX: -image.extent.minX, y: -image.extent.minY))
            let scale = 320 / max(image.extent.width, 1)
            let small = image.transformed(by: CGAffineTransform(scaleX: scale, y: scale))
            analysisWidth = small.extent.width
            analysisHeight = small.extent.height

            if let prev = previous {
                let request = VNTranslationalImageRegistrationRequest(targetedCIImage: small, options: [:])
                let handler = VNImageRequestHandler(ciImage: prev, options: [:])
                try? handler.perform([request])
                if let obs = request.results?.first {
                    // The transform aligns the current frame onto the previous one,
                    // i.e. it is the negative of the content's motion.
                    accX -= Double(obs.alignmentTransform.tx)
                    accY -= Double(obs.alignmentTransform.ty)
                }
            }
            previous = small
            times.append(t)
            pathX.append(accX)
            pathY.append(accY)
            progress(((t - sourceStart) / span).clamped(0, 1))
        }
        guard !times.isEmpty else { throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't analyze clip.") }

        // Smooth the camera path with a centered moving average and correct toward it.
        let radius = max(5, Int(fps * 0.5))
        let n = pathX.count
        var dx = [Float](repeating: 0, count: n)
        var dy = [Float](repeating: 0, count: n)
        for i in 0..<n {
            let lo = max(0, i - radius), hi = min(n - 1, i + radius)
            var sx = 0.0, sy = 0.0
            for j in lo...hi {
                sx += pathX[j]
                sy += pathY[j]
            }
            let count = Double(hi - lo + 1)
            let smoothX = sx / count, smoothY = sy / count
            let maxShift = 0.06
            dx[i] = Float(((smoothX - pathX[i]) / Double(analysisWidth)).clamped(-maxShift, maxShift))
            dy[i] = Float(((smoothY - pathY[i]) / Double(analysisHeight)).clamped(-maxShift, maxShift))
        }
        return StabilizationData(frameRate: fps, sourceStart: times[0], dx: dx, dy: dy)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/ThumbnailCache.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import UIKit
import ImageIO
import Observation

/// Asynchronous, deduplicated, memory-bounded thumbnail cache for the timeline.
/// Reads are cheap and synchronous; misses are queued and generated off the main thread.
@Observable
final class ThumbnailCache: @unchecked Sendable {
    /// Bumped (on the main thread) whenever new thumbnails arrive so the timeline redraws.
    private(set) var version = 0

    @ObservationIgnored private let lock = NSLock()
    @ObservationIgnored private var images: [String: UIImage] = [:]
    @ObservationIgnored private var order: [String] = []
    @ObservationIgnored private var inflight = Set<String>()
    @ObservationIgnored private var queue: [(key: String, url: URL, time: Double, isImage: Bool)] = []
    @ObservationIgnored private var active = 0
    @ObservationIgnored private var generators: [URL: AVAssetImageGenerator] = [:]
    @ObservationIgnored private var bumpScheduled = false
    @ObservationIgnored private let maxImages = 500
    @ObservationIgnored private let maxConcurrent = 3

    static func key(_ url: URL, _ time: Double) -> String {
        "\(url.lastPathComponent)@\(Int((time * 100).rounded()))"
    }

    /// Returns a cached thumbnail, or nil after queueing generation.
    func image(url: URL, time: Double, isImage: Bool) -> UIImage? {
        let t = isImage ? 0 : time
        let key = Self.key(url, t)
        lock.lock()
        if let img = images[key] {
            lock.unlock()
            return img
        }
        if !inflight.contains(key) {
            inflight.insert(key)
            queue.append((key, url, t, isImage))
            if queue.count > 120 {
                let dropped = queue.removeFirst()
                inflight.remove(dropped.key)
            }
        }
        lock.unlock()
        pump()
        return nil
    }

    /// Returns any cached frame for the URL near `time` to avoid blank tiles while generating.
    func nearest(url: URL, time: Double) -> UIImage? {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        let prefix = url.lastPathComponent + "@"
        var best: (Double, UIImage)?
        for (k, img) in images where k.hasPrefix(prefix) {
            if let v = Double(k.dropFirst(prefix.count)) {
                let d = abs(v / 100 - time)
                if best == nil || d < best!.0 { best = (d, img) }
            }
        }
        return best?.1
    }

    func clearMemory() {
        lock.lock()
        images.removeAll()
        order.removeAll()
        generators.removeAll()
        lock.unlock()
    }

    private func pump() {
        while true {
            lock.lock()
            guard active < maxConcurrent, !queue.isEmpty else {
                lock.unlock()
                return
            }
            // Most recent requests first: they're what's on screen now.
            let req = queue.removeLast()
            active += 1
            let generator: AVAssetImageGenerator?
            if req.isImage {
                generator = nil
            } else if let g = generators[req.url] {
                generator = g
            } else {
                let asset = AVURLAsset(url: req.url)
                let g = AVAssetImageGenerator(asset: asset)
                g.appliesPreferredTrackTransform = true
                g.maximumSize = CGSize(width: 180, height: 180)
                g.requestedTimeToleranceBefore = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 10)
                g.requestedTimeToleranceAfter = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 10)
                generators[req.url] = g
                generator = g
            }
            lock.unlock()

            Task.detached(priority: .userInitiated) { [weak self] in
                var result: UIImage?
                if req.isImage {
                    result = Self.downsample(url: req.url, maxPixel: 180)
                } else if let generator {
                    if let grabbed = try? await generator.image(at: req.time.cmTime) {
                        result = UIImage(cgImage: grabbed.image)
                    }
                }
                self?.finish(key: req.key, image: result)
            }
        }
    }

    private func finish(key: String, image: UIImage?) {
        lock.lock()
        active -= 1
        inflight.remove(key)
        if let image {
            images[key] = image
            order.append(key)
            if order.count > maxImages {
                let overflow = order.count - maxImages
                for k in order.prefix(overflow) { images[k] = nil }
                order.removeFirst(overflow)
            }
        }
        let shouldBump = !bumpScheduled && image != nil
        if shouldBump { bumpScheduled = true }
        lock.unlock()
        if shouldBump {
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.05) { [weak self] in
                guard let self else { return }
                self.lock.lock()
                self.bumpScheduled = false
                self.lock.unlock()
                self.version += 1
            }
        }
        pump()
    }

    static func downsample(url: URL, maxPixel: Int) -> UIImage? {
        guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil) else { return nil }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: maxPixel
        ]
        guard let cg = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(src, 0, options as CFDictionary) else { return nil }
        return UIImage(cgImage: cg)
    }

    /// One-off frame grab for project cards.
    static func posterFrame(url: URL, time: Double, isImage: Bool, maxPixel: CGFloat = 480) async -> UIImage? {
        if isImage { return downsample(url: url, maxPixel: Int(maxPixel)) }
        let g = AVAssetImageGenerator(asset: AVURLAsset(url: url))
        g.appliesPreferredTrackTransform = true
        g.maximumSize = CGSize(width: maxPixel, height: maxPixel)
        g.requestedTimeToleranceBefore = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 10)
        g.requestedTimeToleranceAfter = CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 10)
        guard let grabbed = try? await g.image(at: time.cmTime) else { return nil }
        return UIImage(cgImage: grabbed.image)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/VoiceoverRecorder.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Observation

/// Records voiceovers with AVAudioRecorder while the timeline plays muted.
@MainActor
@Observable
final class VoiceoverRecorder: NSObject {
    private(set) var isRecording = false
    private(set) var level: Float = 0
    private(set) var elapsed: Double = 0

    @ObservationIgnored private var recorder: AVAudioRecorder?
    @ObservationIgnored private var meterTimer: Timer?
    @ObservationIgnored private var startDate = Date()
    @ObservationIgnored private var url: URL?

    func start(to url: URL) throws {
        let session = AVAudioSession.sharedInstance()
        try session.setCategory(.playAndRecord, mode: .default, options: [.defaultToSpeaker, .allowBluetoothA2DP])
        try session.setActive(true)
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatMPEG4AAC,
            AVSampleRateKey: 44100,
            AVNumberOfChannelsKey: 1,
            AVEncoderAudioQualityKey: AVAudioQuality.high.rawValue
        ]
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url)
        let recorder = try AVAudioRecorder(url: url, settings: settings)
        recorder.isMeteringEnabled = true
        guard recorder.record() else {
            throw AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't start recording. Another app may be using the microphone.")
        }
        self.recorder = recorder
        self.url = url
        startDate = Date()
        elapsed = 0
        isRecording = true
        meterTimer = Timer.scheduledTimer(withTimeInterval: 1.0 / 20.0, repeats: true) { [weak self] _ in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                guard let self, let r = self.recorder else { return }
                r.updateMeters()
                let db = r.averagePower(forChannel: 0)
                self.level = max(0, min(1, (db + 50) / 50))
                self.elapsed = Date().timeIntervalSince(self.startDate)
            }
        }
    }

    /// Stops recording and returns the file and its duration.
    func stop() -> (URL, Double)? {
        meterTimer?.invalidate()
        meterTimer = nil
        guard let recorder, let url else { return nil }
        let duration = recorder.currentTime
        recorder.stop()
        self.recorder = nil
        self.url = nil
        isRecording = false
        level = 0
        Self.restorePlaybackSession()
        return duration > 0.2 ? (url, duration) : nil
    }

    static func restorePlaybackSession() {
        let session = AVAudioSession.sharedInstance()
        try? session.setCategory(.playback, mode: .moviePlayback)
        try? session.setActive(true)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Services/WaveformGenerator.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Observation

/// Decodes audio to a compact amplitude envelope (100 values per second, 0...1).
enum WaveformGenerator {
    static let envelopeRate: Double = 100

    static func envelope(url: URL) async throws -> [Float] {
        let asset = AVURLAsset(url: url)
        guard let track = try await asset.loadTracks(withMediaType: .audio).first else { return [] }
        let reader = try AVAssetReader(asset: asset)
        let sampleRate = 8000.0
        let settings: [String: Any] = [
            AVFormatIDKey: kAudioFormatLinearPCM,
            AVSampleRateKey: sampleRate,
            AVNumberOfChannelsKey: 1,
            AVLinearPCMBitDepthKey: 32,
            AVLinearPCMIsFloatKey: true,
            AVLinearPCMIsBigEndianKey: false,
            AVLinearPCMIsNonInterleaved: false
        ]
        let output = AVAssetReaderTrackOutput(track: track, outputSettings: settings)
        output.alwaysCopiesSampleData = false
        guard reader.canAdd(output) else { return [] }
        reader.add(output)
        guard reader.startReading() else { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't read audio.") }

        let binSize = Int(sampleRate / envelopeRate)
        var envelope: [Float] = []
        var binPeak: Float = 0
        var binCount = 0
        while let sample = output.copyNextSampleBuffer() {
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            guard let block = CMSampleBufferGetDataBuffer(sample) else { continue }
            let length = CMBlockBufferGetDataLength(block)
            let count = length / MemoryLayout<Float>.size
            guard count > 0 else { continue }
            var floats = [Float](repeating: 0, count: count)
            floats.withUnsafeMutableBytes { raw in
                if let base = raw.baseAddress {
                    _ = CMBlockBufferCopyDataBytes(block, atOffset: 0, dataLength: length, destination: base)
                }
            }
            for v in floats {
                let a = abs(v)
                if a > binPeak { binPeak = a }
                binCount += 1
                if binCount >= binSize {
                    envelope.append(binPeak)
                    binPeak = 0
                    binCount = 0
                }
            }
        }
        if binCount > 0 { envelope.append(binPeak) }
        if reader.status == .failed { throw reader.error ?? AppError.processingFailed("Couldn't read audio.") }
        let maxValue = envelope.max() ?? 0
        if maxValue > 0 {
            for i in envelope.indices { envelope[i] /= maxValue }
        }
        return envelope
    }
}

/// Caches envelopes in memory and on disk; notifies the timeline when new ones arrive.
@Observable
final class WaveformStore: @unchecked Sendable {
    private(set) var version = 0
    @ObservationIgnored private let lock = NSLock()
    @ObservationIgnored private var envelopes: [URL: [Float]] = [:]
    @ObservationIgnored private var inflight = Set<URL>()

    func envelope(for url: URL, cacheFile: URL) -> [Float]? {
        lock.lock()
        if let e = envelopes[url] {
            lock.unlock()
            return e
        }
        if inflight.contains(url) {
            lock.unlock()
            return nil
        }
        inflight.insert(url)
        lock.unlock()

        Task.detached(priority: .utility) { [weak self] in
            var result: [Float] = []
            if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: cacheFile), !data.isEmpty {
                result = data.withUnsafeBytes { Array($0.bindMemory(to: Float.self)) }
            } else if let env = try? await WaveformGenerator.envelope(url: url) {
                result = env
                let data = env.withUnsafeBufferPointer { Data(buffer: $0) }
                try? data.write(to: cacheFile, options: .atomic)
            }
            guard let self else { return }
            self.store(result, for: url)
        }
        return nil
    }

    private func store(_ envelope: [Float], for url: URL) {
        lock.lock()
        envelopes[url] = envelope
        inflight.remove(url)
        lock.unlock()
        DispatchQueue.main.async { self.version += 1 }
    }
}

/// Finds musical beats from an amplitude envelope using onset strength + tempo autocorrelation.
enum BeatDetector {
    static func detect(envelope: [Float], rate: Double = WaveformGenerator.envelopeRate) -> [Double] {
        let n = envelope.count
        guard n > Int(rate * 3) else { return [] }

        // Onset strength: positive change in log-amplitude, lightly smoothed.
        var onset = [Float](repeating: 0, count: n)
        var prev = log(Float(1e-3) + envelope[0])
        for i in 1..<n {
            let cur = log(Float(1e-3) + envelope[i])
            onset[i] = max(0, cur - prev)
            prev = cur
        }
        var smooth = onset
        for i in 1..<(n - 1) {
            smooth[i] = (onset[i - 1] + 2 * onset[i] + onset[i + 1]) / 4
        }

        // Tempo: autocorrelation over 60...180 BPM, biased toward ~120 BPM.
        let analysisLength = min(n, Int(rate * 60))
        let minLag = Int(rate * 60 / 180)
        let maxLag = Int(rate * 60 / 60)
        var bestLag = Int(rate * 0.5)
        var bestScore: Float = -1
        for lag in minLag...maxLag where lag < analysisLength {
            var score: Float = 0
            var i = lag
            while i < analysisLength {
                score += smooth[i] * smooth[i - lag]
                i += 1
            }
            let bpm = 60 * rate / Double(lag)
            let weight = Float(exp(-0.5 * pow(log2(bpm / 120) / 0.9, 2)))
            score *= weight
            if score > bestScore {
                bestScore = score
                bestLag = lag
            }
        }
        guard bestScore > 0 else { return [] }

        // Phase: offset whose comb best aligns with onsets.
        var bestPhase = 0
        var bestPhaseScore: Float = -1
        for phase in 0..<bestLag {
            var s: Float = 0
            var i = phase
            while i < n {
                s += smooth[i]
                i += bestLag
            }
            if s > bestPhaseScore {
                bestPhaseScore = s
                bestPhase = phase
            }
        }
```
