```swift
        // Place beats on the grid, snapping each to the strongest nearby onset.
        var beats: [Double] = []
        var i = bestPhase
        let window = max(2, bestLag / 8)
        while i < n {
            var bestIndex = i
            var bestValue: Float = -1
            for j in max(0, i - window)...min(n - 1, i + window) where smooth[j] > bestValue {
                bestValue = smooth[j]
                bestIndex = j
            }
            beats.append(Double(bestIndex) / rate)
            i += bestLag
        }
        return beats
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/ViewModels/EditorViewModel+Editing.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI

enum TrimEdge {
    case left, right
}

/// Pure editing operations. Each public method is one undoable step.
extension EditorViewModel {
    // MARK: Generic updates

    func updateClip(_ id: UUID, _ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout Clip) -> Void) {
        perform(name, coalesce: coalesce) { p in
            if let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                change(&p.clips[i])
                p.normalizeMainTrack()
            } else if let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                change(&p.overlayClips[i])
            }
        }
    }

    func updateSelectedClip(_ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout Clip) -> Void) {
        guard let clip = selectedClip else { return }
        updateClip(clip.id, name, coalesce: coalesce.map { "\($0)-\(clip.id)" }, change)
    }

    func updateText(_ id: UUID, _ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout TextItem) -> Void) {
        perform(name, coalesce: coalesce.map { "\($0)-\(id)" }) { p in
            if let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { change(&p.texts[i]) }
        }
    }

    func updateOverlay(_ id: UUID, _ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout OverlayItem) -> Void) {
        perform(name, coalesce: coalesce.map { "\($0)-\(id)" }) { p in
            if let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { change(&p.overlays[i]) }
        }
    }

    func updateAudio(_ id: UUID, _ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout AudioClip) -> Void) {
        perform(name, coalesce: coalesce.map { "\($0)-\(id)" }) { p in
            if let i = p.audioClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { change(&p.audioClips[i]) }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Visual transform (clips, overlays, text)

    struct VisualInfo {
        var transform: Transform2D
        var keyframes: [Keyframe]
        var start: Double
        var duration: Double
    }

    var selectedVisual: VisualInfo? {
        switch selection {
        case .some(.clip), .some(.overlayClip):
            guard let c = selectedClip else { return nil }
            return VisualInfo(transform: c.transform, keyframes: c.keyframes, start: c.timelineStart, duration: c.duration)
        case .some(.overlay):
            guard let o = selectedOverlay else { return nil }
            return VisualInfo(transform: o.transform, keyframes: o.keyframes, start: o.start, duration: o.duration)
        case .some(.text):
            guard let t = selectedText else { return nil }
            return VisualInfo(transform: t.transform, keyframes: t.keyframes, start: t.start, duration: t.duration)
        default:
            return nil
        }
    }

    /// Transform shown at the playhead (keyframes resolved).
    var selectedResolvedTransform: Transform2D? {
        guard let v = selectedVisual else { return nil }
        let local = (currentTime - v.start).clamped(0, v.duration)
        return KeyframeMath.transform(base: v.transform, keyframes: v.keyframes, at: local)
    }

    private static func mutateVisual(_ p: inout Project, _ sel: TimelineSelection,
                                     _ body: (inout Transform2D, inout [Keyframe], Double, Double) -> Void) {
        switch sel {
        case .clip(let id):
            if let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                var c = p.clips[i]
                let start = c.timelineStart, duration = c.duration
                body(&c.transform, &c.keyframes, start, duration)
                p.clips[i] = c
            }
        case .overlayClip(let id):
            if let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                var c = p.overlayClips[i]
                let start = c.timelineStart, duration = c.duration
                body(&c.transform, &c.keyframes, start, duration)
                p.overlayClips[i] = c
            }
        case .overlay(let id):
            if let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                var o = p.overlays[i]
                let start = o.start, duration = o.duration
                body(&o.transform, &o.keyframes, start, duration)
                p.overlays[i] = o
            }
        case .text(let id):
            if let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                var t = p.texts[i]
                let start = t.start, duration = t.duration
                body(&t.transform, &t.keyframes, start, duration)
                p.texts[i] = t
            }
        default:
            break
        }
    }

    /// Edits the selected item's transform. With keyframes present, edits (or adds) the keyframe at the playhead.
    func editTransform(_ name: String = "Transform", coalesce: String = "transform", _ change: @escaping (inout Transform2D) -> Void) {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        let time = currentTime
        perform(name, coalesce: "\(coalesce)-\(sel.id)") { p in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { base, keyframes, start, duration in
                let local = (time - start).clamped(0, duration)
                if keyframes.isEmpty {
                    change(&base)
                } else {
                    var resolved = KeyframeMath.transform(base: base, keyframes: keyframes, at: local)
                    change(&resolved)
                    KeyframeMath.upsert(&keyframes, time: local, transform: resolved)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Live transform edit used by preview gestures; `change` receives the transform at gesture start.
    func interactiveTransform(_ change: @escaping (inout Transform2D) -> Void) {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        let time = currentTime
        beginInteractive()
        updateInteractive { p, _ in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { base, keyframes, start, duration in
                let local = (time - start).clamped(0, duration)
                if keyframes.isEmpty {
                    change(&base)
                } else {
                    var resolved = KeyframeMath.transform(base: base, keyframes: keyframes, at: local)
                    change(&resolved)
                    KeyframeMath.upsert(&keyframes, time: local, transform: resolved)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    var hasKeyframeAtPlayhead: Bool {
        guard let v = selectedVisual else { return false }
        return KeyframeMath.index(near: currentTime - v.start, in: v.keyframes) != nil
    }

    func toggleKeyframe() {
        guard let sel = selection, let v = selectedVisual else { return }
        let local = (currentTime - v.start).clamped(0, v.duration)
        let exists = KeyframeMath.index(near: local, in: v.keyframes) != nil
        perform(exists ? "Remove Keyframe" : "Add Keyframe") { p in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { base, keyframes, _, _ in
                if let i = KeyframeMath.index(near: local, in: keyframes) {
                    let removed = keyframes.remove(at: i)
                    if keyframes.isEmpty { base = removed.transform }
                } else {
                    let resolved = KeyframeMath.transform(base: base, keyframes: keyframes, at: local)
                    KeyframeMath.upsert(&keyframes, time: local, transform: resolved)
                }
            }
        }
        Haptics.action()
    }

    func setKeyframeEasing(_ easing: Easing) {
        guard let sel = selection, let v = selectedVisual else { return }
        let local = (currentTime - v.start).clamped(0, v.duration)
        perform("Keyframe Easing") { p in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { _, keyframes, _, _ in
                if let i = KeyframeMath.index(near: local, in: keyframes) {
                    keyframes[i].easing = easing
                } else if let i = keyframes.lastIndex(where: { $0.time <= local }) {
                    keyframes[i].easing = easing
                }
            }
        }
    }

    func clearKeyframes() {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        perform("Clear Keyframes") { p in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { _, keyframes, _, _ in keyframes.removeAll() }
        }
    }

    func resetTransform() {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        perform("Reset Transform") { p in
            Self.mutateVisual(&p, sel) { base, keyframes, _, _ in
                let keepOpacity = base.opacity
                base = Transform2D()
                base.opacity = keepOpacity
                keyframes.removeAll()
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Split

    /// Splits the selected item (or the main clip under the playhead) at the playhead.
    func splitAtPlayhead() {
        let t = currentTime
        if let sel = selection {
            switch sel {
            case .clip(let id), .overlayClip(let id):
                if let clip = project.clip(id), splitClip(clip, at: t) { return }
            case .text(let id):
                if let item = project.texts.first(where: { $0.id == id }), t > item.start + 0.05, t < item.end - 0.05 {
                    perform("Split") { p in
                        guard let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                        var b = p.texts[i]
                        b.id = UUID()
                        b.start = t
                        b.duration = item.end - t
                        let (lk, rk) = KeyframeMath.split(item.keyframes, base: item.transform, at: t - item.start)
                        b.keyframes = rk
                        b.animationIn = .none
                        p.texts[i].duration = t - item.start
                        p.texts[i].keyframes = lk
                        p.texts[i].animationOut = .none
                        p.texts.insert(b, at: i + 1)
                    }
                    Haptics.action()
                    return
                }
            case .overlay(let id):
                if let item = project.overlays.first(where: { $0.id == id }), t > item.start + 0.05, t < item.end - 0.05 {
                    perform("Split") { p in
                        guard let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                        var b = p.overlays[i]
                        b.id = UUID()
                        b.start = t
                        b.duration = item.end - t
                        let (lk, rk) = KeyframeMath.split(item.keyframes, base: item.transform, at: t - item.start)
                        b.keyframes = rk
                        b.animationIn = .none
                        p.overlays[i].duration = t - item.start
                        p.overlays[i].keyframes = lk
                        p.overlays[i].animationOut = .none
                        p.overlays.insert(b, at: i + 1)
                    }
                    Haptics.action()
                    return
                }
            case .audio(let id):
                if let a = project.audioClips.first(where: { $0.id == id }), t > a.timelineStart + 0.05, t < a.timelineEnd - 0.05 {
                    perform("Split") { p in
                        guard let i = p.audioClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                        let src = a.sourceTime(atLocal: t - a.timelineStart)
                        var b = p.audioClips[i]
                        b.id = UUID()
                        b.timelineStart = t
                        b.sourceStart = src
                        b.fadeIn = 0
                        p.audioClips[i].sourceEnd = src
                        p.audioClips[i].fadeOut = 0
                        p.audioClips.insert(b, at: i + 1)
                    }
                    Haptics.action()
                    return
                }
            case .caption:
                break
            }
        }
        if let clip = project.clips.first(where: { $0.contains(t) }), splitClip(clip, at: t) { return }
        showToast("Move the playhead over a clip to split")
        Haptics.warning()
    }

    @discardableResult
    func splitClip(_ clip: Clip, at t: Double) -> Bool {
        let local = t - clip.timelineStart
        guard local > 0.05, local < clip.duration - 0.05 else { return false }
        var newID = UUID()
        perform("Split") { p in
            newID = Self.split(&p, clipID: clip.id, at: t) ?? newID
        }
        selection = project.clips.contains(where: { $0.id == newID }) ? .clip(newID)
            : (project.overlayClips.contains(where: { $0.id == newID }) ? .overlayClip(newID) : selection)
        Haptics.action()
        return true
    }

    /// Splits a clip in place, returning the id of the right half.
    static func split(_ p: inout Project, clipID: UUID, at t: Double) -> UUID? {
        func doSplit(_ clip: Clip) -> (Clip, Clip)? {
            let local = t - clip.timelineStart
            guard local > 0.02, local < clip.duration - 0.02 else { return nil }
            var a = clip, b = clip
            b.id = UUID()
            let src = clip.sourceTime(atLocal: local)
            if clip.ramp != .none {
                // Bake ramped speed so both halves keep their exact timeline length.
                a.ramp = .none
                b.ramp = .none
                a.speed = ((src - clip.sourceStart) / local).clamped(0.1, 8)
                b.speed = ((clip.sourceEnd - src) / (clip.duration - local)).clamped(0.1, 8)
            }
            a.sourceEnd = src
            b.sourceStart = src
            b.timelineStart = t
            let (lk, rk) = KeyframeMath.split(clip.keyframes, base: clip.transform, at: local)
            a.keyframes = lk
            b.keyframes = rk
            a.animationOut = .none
            b.animationIn = .none
            a.fadeOut = 0
            b.fadeIn = 0
            b.transitionOut = clip.transitionOut
            a.transitionOut = TransitionSettings()
            return (a, b)
        }
        if let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == clipID }), let pair = doSplit(p.clips[i]) {
            p.clips[i] = pair.0
            p.clips.insert(pair.1, at: i + 1)
            return pair.1.id
        }
        if let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == clipID }), let pair = doSplit(p.overlayClips[i]) {
            p.overlayClips[i] = pair.0
            p.overlayClips.insert(pair.1, at: i + 1)
            return pair.1.id
        }
        return nil
    }

    // MARK: Delete / duplicate

    func deleteSelection() {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        perform("Delete") { p in
            switch sel {
            case .clip(let id):
                p.clips.removeAll { $0.id == id }
                p.normalizeMainTrack()
            case .overlayClip(let id):
                p.overlayClips.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            case .overlay(let id):
                p.overlays.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            case .text(let id):
                p.texts.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            case .audio(let id):
                p.audioClips.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            case .caption(let id):
                p.captions.segments.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            }
        }
        selection = nil
        activePanel = nil
        Haptics.action()
    }

    func duplicateSelection() {
        guard let sel = selection else { return }
        var newSelection: TimelineSelection?
        perform("Duplicate") { p in
            switch sel {
            case .clip(let id):
                guard let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                var copy = p.clips[i]
                copy.id = UUID()
                let shift = copy.duration
                copy.timelineStart = p.clips[i].timelineEnd
                for j in p.clips.indices where j > i { p.clips[j].timelineStart += shift }
                p.clips.insert(copy, at: i + 1)
                p.normalizeMainTrack()
                newSelection = .clip(copy.id)
            case .overlayClip(let id):
                guard let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                var copy = p.overlayClips[i]
                copy.id = UUID()
                copy.timelineStart = p.overlayClips[i].timelineEnd
                p.overlayClips.insert(copy, at: i + 1)
                newSelection = .overlayClip(copy.id)
            case .overlay(let id):
                guard let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                var copy = p.overlays[i]
                copy.id = UUID()
                copy.start = p.overlays[i].end
                p.overlays.insert(copy, at: i + 1)
                newSelection = .overlay(copy.id)
            case .text(let id):
                guard let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                var copy = p.texts[i]
                copy.id = UUID()
                copy.start = p.texts[i].end
                p.texts.insert(copy, at: i + 1)
                newSelection = .text(copy.id)
            case .audio(let id):
                guard let i = p.audioClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
                var copy = p.audioClips[i]
                copy.id = UUID()
                copy.timelineStart = p.audioClips[i].timelineEnd
                p.audioClips.insert(copy, at: i + 1)
                newSelection = .audio(copy.id)
            case .caption:
                break
            }
        }
        if let newSelection { selection = newSelection }
        Haptics.action()
    }

    // MARK: Trim & move (timeline gestures)

    /// Trims `selection` by `delta` seconds at `edge`, computed from `base`.
    static func trim(_ p: inout Project, _ sel: TimelineSelection, edge: TrimEdge, delta: Double) {
        let minDur = 0.1
        func trimClip(_ c: inout Clip, magnetic: Bool, mediaDuration: Double) {
            let speed = c.effectiveSpeed
            if c.isImage {
                if edge == .left {
                    let d = delta.clamped(-c.timelineStart, c.sourceDuration - minDur)
                    c.sourceEnd = max(minDur, c.sourceEnd - d)
                    if !magnetic { c.timelineStart += d }
                } else {
                    c.sourceEnd = max(c.sourceStart + minDur, min(c.sourceEnd + delta, c.sourceStart + 3600))
                }
                return
            }
            if edge == .left {
                var newStart = c.sourceStart + delta * speed
                newStart = newStart.clamped(0, c.sourceEnd - minDur * speed)
                if !magnetic {
                    let shift = (newStart - c.sourceStart) / speed
                    if c.timelineStart + shift < 0 {
                        newStart = c.sourceStart - c.timelineStart * speed
                    }
                    c.timelineStart = max(0, c.timelineStart + (newStart - c.sourceStart) / speed)
                }
                c.sourceStart = newStart
            } else {
                var newEnd = c.sourceEnd + delta * speed
                newEnd = newEnd.clamped(c.sourceStart + minDur * speed, mediaDuration)
                c.sourceEnd = newEnd
            }
        }
        switch sel {
        case .clip(let id):
            guard let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            let d = p.mediaItem(p.clips[i].mediaID)?.duration ?? p.clips[i].sourceEnd
            trimClip(&p.clips[i], magnetic: p.magnetic, mediaDuration: d)
            p.normalizeMainTrack()
        case .overlayClip(let id):
            guard let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            let d = p.mediaItem(p.overlayClips[i].mediaID)?.duration ?? p.overlayClips[i].sourceEnd
            trimClip(&p.overlayClips[i], magnetic: false, mediaDuration: d)
        case .overlay(let id):
            guard let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            if edge == .left {
                let d = delta.clamped(-p.overlays[i].start, p.overlays[i].duration - minDur)
                p.overlays[i].start += d
                p.overlays[i].duration -= d
            } else {
                p.overlays[i].duration = max(minDur, p.overlays[i].duration + delta)
            }
        case .text(let id):
            guard let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            if edge == .left {
                let d = delta.clamped(-p.texts[i].start, p.texts[i].duration - minDur)
                p.texts[i].start += d
                p.texts[i].duration -= d
            } else {
                p.texts[i].duration = max(minDur, p.texts[i].duration + delta)
            }
        case .audio(let id):
            guard let i = p.audioClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            var a = p.audioClips[i]
            let speed = a.speed.clamped(0.1, 8)
            let mediaDuration = p.mediaItem(a.mediaID)?.duration ?? a.sourceEnd
            if edge == .left {
                var newStart = (a.sourceStart + delta * speed).clamped(0, a.sourceEnd - minDur * speed)
                if a.timelineStart + (newStart - a.sourceStart) / speed < 0 {
                    newStart = a.sourceStart - a.timelineStart * speed
                }
                a.timelineStart += (newStart - a.sourceStart) / speed
                a.sourceStart = newStart
            } else {
                a.sourceEnd = (a.sourceEnd + delta * speed).clamped(a.sourceStart + minDur * speed, mediaDuration)
            }
            p.audioClips[i] = a
        case .caption(let id):
            guard let i = p.captions.segments.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
            let old = p.captions.segments[i]
            var seg = old
            if edge == .left {
                seg.start = min(max(0, old.start + delta), old.end - minDur)
            } else {
                seg.end = max(old.start + minDur, old.end + delta)
            }
            // Keep each word's relative timing so karaoke highlighting stays in sync.
            let oldSpan = max(old.end - old.start, 0.001)
            let newSpan = seg.end - seg.start
            seg.words = old.words.map { w in
                CaptionWord(text: w.text,
                            start: seg.start + (w.start - old.start) / oldSpan * newSpan,
                            end: seg.start + (w.end - old.start) / oldSpan * newSpan)
            }
            p.captions.segments[i] = seg
        }
    }

    /// Moves a non-main-track item so it starts at `start`.
    static func move(_ p: inout Project, _ sel: TimelineSelection, to start: Double) {
        let s = max(0, start)
        switch sel {
        case .overlayClip(let id):
            if let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { p.overlayClips[i].timelineStart = s }
        case .overlay(let id):
            if let i = p.overlays.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { p.overlays[i].start = s }
        case .text(let id):
            if let i = p.texts.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { p.texts[i].start = s }
        case .audio(let id):
            if let i = p.audioClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { p.audioClips[i].timelineStart = s }
        case .caption(let id):
            if let i = p.captions.segments.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                let shift = s - p.captions.segments[i].start
                p.captions.segments[i].start += shift
                p.captions.segments[i].end += shift
                for w in p.captions.segments[i].words.indices {
                    p.captions.segments[i].words[w].start += shift
                    p.captions.segments[i].words[w].end += shift
                }
            }
        case .clip(let id):
            if let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                p.clips[i].timelineStart = s
                p.resolveMainTrackOverlaps()
            }
        }
    }

    /// Commits a main-track drag: reorders in magnetic mode, repositions otherwise.
    func commitMainTrackMove(clipID: UUID, dropStart: Double) {
        perform("Move Clip") { p in
            guard let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == clipID }) else { return }
            if p.magnetic {
                let clip = p.clips.remove(at: i)
                let center = dropStart + clip.duration / 2
                let index = p.clips.filter { $0.timelineStart + $0.duration / 2 < center }.count
                p.clips.insert(clip, at: index)
                var cursor = 0.0
                for j in p.clips.indices {
                    p.clips[j].timelineStart = cursor
                    cursor += p.clips[j].duration
                }
            } else {
                p.clips[i].timelineStart = max(0, dropStart)
                let moved = p.clips.remove(at: i)
                let index = p.clips.filter { $0.timelineStart < moved.timelineStart }.count
                p.clips.insert(moved, at: index)
                var cursor = 0.0
                for j in p.clips.indices {
                    if p.clips[j].timelineStart < cursor { p.clips[j].timelineStart = cursor }
                    cursor = p.clips[j].timelineEnd
                }
            }
        }
        Haptics.action()
    }

    /// Snap targets in timeline seconds: playhead, item edges, beats.
    func snapCandidates(excluding id: UUID?) -> [Double] {
        var c: [Double] = [currentTime, 0]
        for x in project.clips where x.id != id { c.append(x.timelineStart); c.append(x.timelineEnd) }
        for x in project.overlayClips where x.id != id { c.append(x.timelineStart); c.append(x.timelineEnd) }
        for x in project.overlays where x.id != id { c.append(x.start); c.append(x.end) }
        for x in project.texts where x.id != id { c.append(x.start); c.append(x.end) }
        for x in project.audioClips where x.id != id { c.append(x.timelineStart); c.append(x.timelineEnd) }
        c.append(contentsOf: timelineBeats)
        return c
    }

    // MARK: Clip operations

    func setMagnetic(_ on: Bool) {
        perform(on ? "Magnetic On" : "Magnetic Off") { p in
            p.magnetic = on
            p.normalizeMainTrack()
        }
        showToast(on ? "Magnetic timeline on — gaps close automatically" : "Magnetic timeline off")
    }

    func setSpeed(_ speed: Double) {
        updateSelectedClip("Speed", coalesce: "speed") { $0.speed = speed.clamped(0.1, 8) }
    }

    func setRamp(_ ramp: SpeedRamp) {
        updateSelectedClip("Speed Ramp") { $0.ramp = ramp }
    }

    func setTransition(clipID: UUID, type: TransitionType, duration: Double? = nil) {
        updateClip(clipID, "Transition", coalesce: duration == nil ? nil : "transition-\(clipID)") { c in
            c.transitionOut.type = type
            if let duration { c.transitionOut.duration = duration.clamped(0.1, 2) }
        }
    }

    func applyTransitionToAll(from clipID: UUID) {
        guard let source = project.clip(clipID) else { return }
        perform("Transition to All") { p in
            for i in p.clips.indices where i < p.clips.count - 1 {
                p.clips[i].transitionOut = source.transitionOut
            }
        }
        showToast("Applied to all cuts")
    }

    func copyEffects() {
        guard let clip = selectedClip else { return }
        copiedEffects = clip.effectsPayload()
        showToast("Effects copied")
        Haptics.tap()
    }

    func pasteEffects() {
        guard let payload = copiedEffects else { return }
        updateSelectedClip("Paste Effects") { $0.apply(payload) }
        showToast("Effects pasted")
        Haptics.success()
    }

    func applyFilterToAllClips() {
        guard let clip = selectedClip else { return }
        perform("Filter to All") { p in
            for i in p.clips.indices { p.clips[i].filter = clip.filter }
            for i in p.overlayClips.indices { p.overlayClips[i].filter = clip.filter }
        }
        showToast("Look applied to every clip")
    }

    func detachAudio() {
        guard let clip = selectedClip, !clip.isImage, let m = media(clip.mediaID), m.hasAudio else {
            showToast("This clip has no audio")
            return
        }
        guard !clip.audioDetached else { return }
        var newID = UUID()
        perform("Detach Audio") { p in
            let audio = AudioClip(mediaID: clip.mediaID, name: "Audio · \(m.originalName)", kind: .extracted,
                                  timelineStart: clip.timelineStart, sourceStart: clip.sourceStart, sourceEnd: clip.sourceEnd,
                                  speed: clip.effectiveSpeed, reversed: clip.reversed, volume: clip.volume, muted: false,
                                  fadeIn: clip.fadeIn, fadeOut: clip.fadeOut, denoise: clip.denoise)
            newID = audio.id
            p.audioClips.append(audio)
            if let i = p.clips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == clip.id }) { p.clips[i].audioDetached = true }
            if let i = p.overlayClips.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == clip.id }) { p.overlayClips[i].audioDetached = true }
        }
        selection = .audio(newID)
        showToast("Audio detached")
        Haptics.action()
    }

    // MARK: Adding items

    func addText() {
        let start = project.isEmpty ? 0 : min(currentTime, max(project.duration - 0.5, 0))
        var item = TextItem(start: start)
        if project.duration > 0 { item.duration = min(3, max(project.duration - start, 1)) }
        perform("Add Text") { $0.texts.append(item) }
        selection = .text(item.id)
        activePanel = .text
        Haptics.action()
    }

    func addSticker(_ emoji: String) {
        var item = OverlayItem(kind: .sticker, start: currentTime)
        item.sticker = emoji
        perform("Add Sticker") { $0.overlays.append(item) }
        selection = .overlay(item.id)
        Haptics.action()
    }

    func addShape(_ shape: ShapeKind, color: RGBAColor) {
        var item = OverlayItem(kind: .shape, start: currentTime)
        item.shape = shape
        item.color = color
        perform("Add Shape") { $0.overlays.append(item) }
        selection = .overlay(item.id)
        Haptics.action()
    }

    func setCanvasAspect(_ aspect: AspectRatio) {
        perform("Canvas") { $0.canvas.aspect = aspect }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/ViewModels/EditorViewModel+Media.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import PhotosUI
import AVFoundation

/// Importing media, recording, captions and derived-media processing.
extension EditorViewModel {
    // MARK: Photos picker

    func presentPicker(_ target: PickerTarget) {
        pickerTarget = target
        pickerItems = []
        afterDismissingPanel { [weak self] in self?.showMediaPicker = true }
    }

    func presentMusicImporter() {
        afterDismissingPanel { [weak self] in self?.showMusicImporter = true }
    }

    /// Runs `action` once any open panel sheet has finished dismissing, so two sheets never collide.
    func afterDismissingPanel(_ action: @escaping @MainActor () -> Void) {
        guard activePanel != nil else {
            action()
            return
        }
        activePanel = nil
        Task { @MainActor in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 450_000_000)
            action()
        }
    }

    func handlePicked(_ items: [PhotosPickerItem]) {
        guard !items.isEmpty else { return }
        let target = pickerTarget
        let paths = self.paths
        runBusy("Importing…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let result = await MediaImportService.importPickerItems(items, into: paths) { index, count in
                self.busy?.message = count > 1 ? "Importing \(min(index + 1, count)) of \(count)…" : "Importing…"
                self.busy?.progress = count > 0 ? Double(index) / Double(count) : nil
            }
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            if !result.items.isEmpty {
                self.insertImported(result.items, target: target)
            }
            if !result.failures.isEmpty {
                self.alert = AlertInfo(title: result.items.isEmpty ? "Couldn't import" : "Some items were skipped",
                                       message: result.failures.joined(separator: "\n"))
            }
        }
        pickerItems = []
    }

    func insertImported(_ items: [MediaItem], target: PickerTarget) {
        let wasEmpty = project.clips.isEmpty
        var newSelection: TimelineSelection?
        perform(target == .mainTrack ? "Add Media" : "Add Overlay") { p in
            p.media.append(contentsOf: items)
            if target == .mainTrack {
                if wasEmpty, autoAspectOnFirstImport, let first = items.first(where: { $0.kind != .audio }) {
                    p.canvas.aspect = AspectRatio.bestMatch(for: first.size)
                }
                var cursor = p.clips.last?.timelineEnd ?? 0
                for m in items where m.kind != .audio {
                    var clip = Clip(media: m, at: cursor)
                    clip.fit = .fit
                    p.clips.append(clip)
                    cursor = clip.timelineEnd
                    if newSelection == nil { newSelection = .clip(clip.id) }
                }
                p.normalizeMainTrack()
            } else {
                let start = currentTime
                for (index, m) in items.enumerated() where m.kind != .audio {
                    if m.kind == .image {
                        var o = OverlayItem(kind: .image, start: start)
                        o.imageMediaID = m.id
                        o.transform.x = Double(index) * 0.05
                        o.transform.y = Double(index) * 0.05
                        p.overlays.append(o)
                        newSelection = .overlay(o.id)
                    } else {
                        var clip = Clip(media: m, at: start)
                        clip.transform.scale = 0.5
                        clip.transform.x = 0.2
                        clip.transform.y = -0.2
                        p.overlayClips.append(clip)
                        newSelection = .overlayClip(clip.id)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        autoAspectOnFirstImport = false
        if target == .overlay, let newSelection { selection = newSelection }
        if wasEmpty && target == .mainTrack {
            playback.currentTime = 0
            showToast("Tap ▶︎ to preview · Tap a clip to edit it")
        }
        Haptics.success()
        makeProxies(for: items)
    }

    func makeProxies(for items: [MediaItem]) {
        let paths = self.paths
        for m in items where m.kind == .video && m.width * m.height > MediaImportService.proxyThreshold {
            Task { [weak self] in
                let made = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
                    await MediaImportService.makeProxyIfNeeded(for: m, paths: paths) != nil
                }.value
                if made { self?.rebuild(force: true) }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Regenerates proxies and beat data that may be missing (e.g. after a crash).
    func ensureDerivedMedia() {
        makeProxies(for: project.media.filter { m in
            !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: paths.cache(MediaResolver.proxyName(m)).path)
        })
        for m in project.media where m.kind == .audio && beats(for: m.id) == nil {
            if project.audioClips.contains(where: { $0.mediaID == m.id && $0.kind == .music }) {
                analyzeBeats(m)
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Music

    func importMusic(_ url: URL) {
        let paths = self.paths
        runBusy("Adding music…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let media = try await MediaImportService.importAudio(from: url, into: paths, move: false)
            let start = self.project.clips.isEmpty ? 0 : min(self.currentTime, max(self.project.duration - 1, 0))
            var audio = AudioClip(mediaID: media.id, name: media.originalName, kind: .music,
                                  timelineStart: start, sourceStart: 0, sourceEnd: media.duration)
            if self.project.duration > 1 {
                audio.sourceEnd = min(media.duration, max(self.project.duration - start, 1))
                audio.fadeOut = min(1.0, audio.duration / 4)
            }
            self.perform("Add Music") { p in
                p.media.append(media)
                p.audioClips.append(audio)
            }
            self.selection = .audio(audio.id)
            Haptics.success()
            self.analyzeBeats(media)
        }
    }

    func analyzeBeats(_ media: MediaItem) {
        let url = paths.media(media.fileName)
        Task { [weak self] in
            let detected: [Double]? = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
                guard let env = try? await WaveformGenerator.envelope(url: url) else { return nil }
                return BeatDetector.detect(envelope: env)
            }.value
            guard let beats = detected else { return }
            do {
                guard let self else { return }
                self.beatCache[media.id] = beats
                self.updateSilently { p in
                    if let i = p.media.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == media.id }) { p.media[i].beats = beats }
                }
                if !beats.isEmpty { self.showToast("\(beats.count) beats found — cuts will snap to them") }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Splits the main-track clip under each beat (within the music's range).
    func cutToBeats() {
        guard let audio = selectedAudio, let beats = beats(for: audio.mediaID), !beats.isEmpty else {
            showToast("No beats detected yet")
            return
        }
        let times = beats.map { audio.timelineStart + ($0 - audio.sourceStart) / audio.speed.clamped(0.1, 8) }
            .filter { $0 > audio.timelineStart && $0 < audio.timelineEnd }
        var cuts = 0
        perform("Cut to Beats") { p in
            for t in times {
                if let clip = p.clips.first(where: { $0.contains(t) }), Self.split(&p, clipID: clip.id, at: t) != nil {
                    cuts += 1
                }
            }
        }
        showToast(cuts > 0 ? "Added \(cuts) cuts on the beat" : "No clips under the beats")
    }

    // MARK: Voiceover

    func toggleVoiceover() {
        if recorder.isRecording {
            stopVoiceover()
            return
        }
        Task { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            guard await Permissions.microphone() else {
                self.show(AppError.permissionDenied(.microphone))
                return
            }
            do {
                try StorageGuard.ensure(20 * 1024 * 1024)
                try self.paths.ensureDirectories()
                let url = self.paths.media("vo_\(UUID().uuidString).m4a")
                self.voiceoverStart = self.currentTime
                try self.recorder.start(to: url)
                self.playback.player.isMuted = true
                if self.playback.hasItem { self.playback.play() }
                Haptics.action()
            } catch {
                self.show(error)
            }
        }
    }

    func stopVoiceover() {
        playback.pause()
        playback.player.isMuted = false
        guard let recording = recorder.stop() else { return }
        let url = recording.0, duration = recording.1
        let media = MediaItem(fileName: url.lastPathComponent, proxyFileName: nil, originalName: "Voiceover",
                              kind: .audio, duration: duration, width: 0, height: 0, hasAudio: true, hasVideo: false,
                              frameRate: 0, beats: nil)
        let audio = AudioClip(mediaID: media.id, name: "Voiceover", kind: .voiceover,
                              timelineStart: voiceoverStart, sourceStart: 0, sourceEnd: duration)
        perform("Record Voiceover") { p in
            p.media.append(media)
            p.audioClips.append(audio)
        }
        selection = .audio(audio.id)
        Haptics.success()
    }

    // MARK: Captions

    func generateCaptions(locale: Locale, style: CaptionStyle) {
        let snapshot = project
        let paths = self.paths
        runBusy("Listening for speech…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let exporter = ExportService()
            let audioURL: URL
            do {
                audioURL = try await exporter.exportAudio(project: snapshot, paths: paths, includeMusic: false)
            } catch {
                throw AppError.speechUnavailable("There's no speech to caption. Add a video with sound or record a voiceover first.")
            }
            defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: audioURL) }
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            self.busy?.message = "Writing captions…"
            let words = try await CaptionService.transcribe(audioURL: audioURL, locale: locale)
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            guard !words.isEmpty else {
                self.alert = AlertInfo(title: "No speech found", message: "Cutline couldn't hear any speech in this project.")
                return
            }
            let segments = CaptionService.segments(from: words)
            self.perform("Auto Captions") { p in
                p.captions.segments = segments
                p.captions.style = style
                p.captions.verticalPosition = style.defaultVerticalPosition
            }
            self.showToast("\(segments.count) captions added")
            Haptics.success()
        }
    }

    func setCaptionStyle(_ style: CaptionStyle) {
        perform("Caption Style") { p in
            p.captions.style = style
            p.captions.verticalPosition = style.defaultVerticalPosition
        }
    }

    func updateCaptionText(_ id: UUID, text: String) {
        perform("Edit Caption", coalesce: "caption-\(id)") { p in
            if let i = p.captions.segments.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                p.captions.segments[i].setText(text)
            }
        }
    }

    func removeAllCaptions() {
        perform("Remove Captions") { $0.captions.segments.removeAll() }
        if case .caption? = selection { selection = nil }
    }

    // MARK: Derived media (reverse, denoise, stabilize, freeze)

    func toggleReverse() {
        guard let clip = selectedClip, !clip.isImage, let m = media(clip.mediaID) else {
            showToast("Photos can't be reversed")
            return
        }
        let paths = self.paths
        let revVideo = paths.cache(MediaResolver.reversedVideoName(m))
        let needsWork = !clip.reversed && !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: revVideo.path)
        let apply: @MainActor () -> Void = { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            self.updateClip(clip.id, clip.reversed ? "Unreverse" : "Reverse") { c in
                let d = m.duration
                let newStart = max(0, d - c.sourceEnd)
                let newEnd = min(d, d - c.sourceStart)
                c.sourceStart = newStart
                c.sourceEnd = max(newEnd, newStart + 0.1)
                c.reversed.toggle()
                c.effects.stabilize = false
                c.effects.stabilization = nil
            }
            self.rebuild(force: true)
            Haptics.success()
        }
        guard needsWork else {
            apply()
            return
        }
        runBusy("Reversing…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            guard let original = MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: self.project.media, preferProxy: false).originalURL(m) else {
                throw AppError.missingMedia(m.originalName)
            }
            let progressSink: @Sendable (Double) -> Void = { value in
                Task { @MainActor [weak self] in self?.busy?.progress = value * 0.85 }
            }
            try await DerivedMediaService.reverseVideo(source: original, destination: revVideo, progress: progressSink)
            let proxy = paths.cache(MediaResolver.proxyName(m))
            if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: proxy.path) {
                try await DerivedMediaService.reverseVideo(source: proxy, destination: paths.cache(MediaResolver.reversedProxyName(m))) { _ in }
            }
            if m.hasAudio {
                self.busy?.message = "Reversing audio…"
                try? await DerivedMediaService.reverseAudio(source: original, destination: paths.cache(MediaResolver.reversedAudioName(m)))
            }
            AssetCache.shared.invalidate(revVideo)
            apply()
        }
    }

    func toggleDenoise() {
        if let audio = selectedAudio {
            setDenoise(mediaID: audio.mediaID, reversed: audio.reversed, enable: !audio.denoise) { [weak self] in
                self?.updateAudio(audio.id, audio.denoise ? "Noise Reduction Off" : "Noise Reduction") { $0.denoise.toggle() }
            }
        } else if let clip = selectedClip, !clip.isImage {
            setDenoise(mediaID: clip.mediaID, reversed: clip.reversed, enable: !clip.denoise) { [weak self] in
                self?.updateClip(clip.id, clip.denoise ? "Noise Reduction Off" : "Noise Reduction") { $0.denoise.toggle() }
            }
        }
    }

    private func setDenoise(mediaID: UUID, reversed: Bool, enable: Bool, apply: @escaping @MainActor () -> Void) {
        guard let m = media(mediaID), m.hasAudio else {
            showToast("This clip has no audio")
            return
        }
        let paths = self.paths
        let dest = paths.cache(MediaResolver.denoisedName(m, reversed: reversed))
        guard enable, !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: dest.path) else {
            apply()
            rebuild(force: true)
            return
        }
        runBusy("Reducing noise…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let source: URL
            if reversed {
                source = paths.cache(MediaResolver.reversedAudioName(m))
            } else {
                source = paths.media(m.fileName)
            }
            guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: source.path) else { throw AppError.missingMedia(m.originalName) }
            try await DerivedMediaService.denoise(source: source, destination: dest)
            apply()
            self.rebuild(force: true)
            self.showToast("Background noise reduced")
            Haptics.success()
        }
    }

    func stabilizeSelected() {
        guard let clip = selectedClip, !clip.isImage else { return }
        if clip.effects.stabilize {
            updateClip(clip.id, "Stabilize Off") { $0.effects.stabilize = false }
            return
        }
        if clip.effects.stabilization != nil {
            updateClip(clip.id, "Stabilize") { $0.effects.stabilize = true }
            return
        }
        guard let url = resolver.videoURL(for: clip) else {
            show(AppError.missingMedia(media(clip.mediaID)?.originalName ?? "clip"))
            return
        }
        runBusy("Analyzing motion…") { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let sink: @Sendable (Double) -> Void = { value in
                Task { @MainActor [weak self] in self?.busy?.progress = value }
            }
            let data = try await StabilizationService.analyze(url: url, sourceStart: clip.sourceStart, sourceEnd: clip.sourceEnd, progress: sink)
            self.updateClip(clip.id, "Stabilize") { c in
                c.effects.stabilize = true
                c.effects.stabilization = data
            }
            self.showToast("Stabilized")
            Haptics.success()
        }
    }

    func freezeFrame() {
        let t = currentTime
        let target: Clip?
        if let c = selectedClip, project.clips.contains(where: { $0.id == c.id }), c.contains(t) {
            target = c
        } else {
            target = project.clips.first { $0.contains(t) }
        }
        guard let clip = target, !clip.isImage else {
            showToast("Move the playhead over a video clip")
            return
        }
        let local = t - clip.timelineStart
        let sourceTime = clip.sourceTime(atLocal: local)
        let fullResolver = MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: project.media, preferProxy: false)
        guard let url = fullResolver.videoURL(for: clip) else {
            show(AppError.missingMedia(media(clip.mediaID)?.originalName ?? "clip"))
            return
        }
        let paths = self.paths
        runBusy("Freezing frame…", cancellable: false) { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            let cg = try await DerivedMediaService.frame(url: url, at: sourceTime)
            let media = try MediaImportService.importImage(cgImage: cg, originalName: "Freeze frame", into: paths)
            var freeze = Clip(media: media, at: t, imageDuration: 2)
            freeze.crop = clip.crop
            freeze.quarterTurns = clip.quarterTurns
            freeze.flipH = clip.flipH
            freeze.flipV = clip.flipV
            freeze.fit = clip.fit
            freeze.filter = clip.filter
            freeze.transform = KeyframeMath.transform(base: clip.transform, keyframes: clip.keyframes, at: local)
            freeze.effects.chromaKey = clip.effects.chromaKey
            freeze.effects.removeBackground = clip.effects.removeBackground
            self.perform("Freeze Frame") { p in
                p.media.append(media)
                if local > 0.02 && local < clip.duration - 0.02 {
                    _ = Self.split(&p, clipID: clip.id, at: t)
                }
                for i in p.clips.indices where p.clips[i].timelineStart >= t - 0.0001 {
                    p.clips[i].timelineStart += freeze.duration
                }
                let index = p.clips.firstIndex { $0.timelineStart >= t } ?? p.clips.count
                freeze.timelineStart = t
                p.clips.insert(freeze, at: index)
                p.normalizeMainTrack()
            }
            self.selection = .clip(freeze.id)
            Haptics.success()
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/ViewModels/EditorViewModel.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import AVFoundation
import Observation
import PhotosUI

enum EditorPanel: String, Identifiable {
    case audio, overlay, captions, filters, canvas
    case speed, volume, crop, animate, effects, trim
    case text, transition, overlayStyle

    var id: String { rawValue }
}

enum PickerTarget {
    case mainTrack
    case overlay
}

enum SafeGuide: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
    case none, tiktok, reels, shorts, youtube
    var id: String { rawValue }
    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .none: return "Off"
        case .tiktok: return "TikTok"
        case .reels: return "Reels"
        case .shorts: return "Shorts"
        case .youtube: return "YouTube"
        }
    }
}

struct BusyState {
    var message: String
    var progress: Double?
    var cancellable: Bool
}

struct DragGhost {
    var selection: TimelineSelection
    var kind: TrackKind
    var start: Double
    var duration: Double
    var insertionTime: Double?
}

@MainActor
@Observable
final class EditorViewModel: Identifiable {
    let id: UUID
    let store: ProjectStore
    let paths: ProjectPaths
    private(set) var project: Project

    let playback = PlaybackController()
    let history = ProjectUndoManager()
    let thumbnails = ThumbnailCache()
    let waveforms = WaveformStore()
    let recorder = VoiceoverRecorder()

    var selection: TimelineSelection?
    var pixelsPerSecond: Double = 60
    var verticalOffset: CGFloat = 0
    var activePanel: EditorPanel?
    var transitionTargetClipID: UUID?
    var alert: AlertInfo?
    var toast: String?
    var busy: BusyState?
    var dragGhost: DragGhost?
    var snapTime: Double?
    var safeGuide: SafeGuide = .none
    var copiedEffects: ClipEffectsPayload?
    var showMediaPicker = false
    var pickerTarget: PickerTarget = .mainTrack
    var pickerItems: [PhotosPickerItem] = []
    var showMusicImporter = false
    var showExport = false
    var missingMediaCount = 0
    var recoveredFromBackup = false

    @ObservationIgnored private(set) var autosave: AutosaveService!
    @ObservationIgnored private var renderBox: RenderStateBox?
    @ObservationIgnored private var lastSignature: Int?
    @ObservationIgnored private var rebuildTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored private(set) var interactiveBase: Project?
    @ObservationIgnored private var toastTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored var busyTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored var voiceoverStart: Double = 0
    @ObservationIgnored var autoAspectOnFirstImport: Bool
    @ObservationIgnored var beatCache: [UUID: [Double]] = [:]
    @ObservationIgnored var openingToast: String?
    @ObservationIgnored private var stateSaveTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored private var thumbnailWriteTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored private(set) var interaction: TimelineInteraction!

    static let sessionKey = "cutline.openProjectID"

    init(project: Project, store: ProjectStore, recovered: Bool = false, autoAspect: Bool = false) {
        self.id = project.id
        self.project = project
        self.store = store
        self.paths = store.paths(for: project.id)
        self.recoveredFromBackup = recovered
        self.autoAspectOnFirstImport = autoAspect
        self.selection = project.editorState.selection
        self.pixelsPerSecond = project.editorState.pixelsPerSecond.clamped(5, 800)
        self.verticalOffset = CGFloat(project.editorState.verticalOffset)
        for m in project.media where m.kind == .audio {
            if let b = m.beats { beatCache[m.id] = b }
        }
        self.autosave = AutosaveService(paths: paths) { [weak self] in
            self?.projectForSaving() ?? project
        }
        self.autosave.onSaved = { [weak self] in self?.scheduleThumbnailWrite() }
        playback.onStop = { [weak self] in self?.scheduleStateSave() }
        interaction = TimelineInteraction(viewModel: self)
    }

    // MARK: Lifecycle

    func onAppear() {
        UserDefaults.standard.set(id.uuidString, forKey: Self.sessionKey)
        playback.currentTime = project.editorState.playhead
        if selection != nil && !selectionExists(selection) { selection = nil }
        rebuild(force: true)
        ensureDerivedMedia()
        if recoveredFromBackup {
            showToast("Recovered from the last good save")
        } else if let message = openingToast {
            showToast(message)
            openingToast = nil
        }
        missingMediaCount = project.media.filter { m in
            !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: paths.media(m.fileName).path)
        }.count
    }

    func close() {
        if recorder.isRecording { stopVoiceover() }
        playback.pause()
        autosave.flush()
        writeThumbnail()
        UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: Self.sessionKey)
        rebuildTask?.cancel()
        busyTask?.cancel()
        playback.clear()
        thumbnails.clearMemory()
        Task { await store.refresh() }
    }

    func flushSave() {
        autosave.flush()
    }

    private func projectForSaving() -> Project {
        var p = project
        p.editorState = EditorState(playhead: playback.currentTime, pixelsPerSecond: pixelsPerSecond,
                                    verticalOffset: Double(verticalOffset), selection: selection)
        for i in p.media.indices where p.media[i].kind == .audio {
            if let b = beatCache[p.media[i].id] { p.media[i].beats = b }
        }
        return p
    }

    func scheduleStateSave() {
        stateSaveTask?.cancel()
        stateSaveTask = Task { [weak self] in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 800_000_000)
            guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
            self?.autosave.schedule(delay: 0.05)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Edit pipeline

    /// Applies an undoable edit. Every edit autosaves and refreshes the preview.
    func perform(_ name: String, coalesce: String? = nil, _ change: (inout Project) -> Void) {
        // During a continuous gesture (slider drag), apply live; one undo step is recorded at the end.
        if interactiveBase != nil {
            change(&project)
            renderBox?.update(project)
            playback.refreshFrame()
            return
        }
        let before = project
        var after = project
        change(&after)
        history.record(before, name: name, coalesceKey: coalesce)
        project = after
        projectDidChange()
    }

    /// Updates analysis data (proxies, beats) without creating an undo step.
    func updateSilently(_ change: (inout Project) -> Void) {
        change(&project)
        projectDidChange()
    }

    func beginInteractive() {
        if interactiveBase == nil {
            interactiveBase = project
            history.breakCoalescing()
        }
    }

    /// Live update during a drag; the change is computed from the state at the start of the gesture.
    func updateInteractive(_ change: (inout Project, Project) -> Void) {
        guard let base = interactiveBase else { return }
        var p = base
        change(&p, base)
        project = p
        renderBox?.update(project)
        playback.refreshFrame()
    }

    func endInteractive(_ name: String) {
        guard let base = interactiveBase else { return }
        interactiveBase = nil
        snapTime = nil
        if Self.signature(base) != Self.signature(project) || Self.visualChanged(base, project) {
            history.record(base, name: name)
        }
        projectDidChange()
    }

    func cancelInteractive() {
        guard let base = interactiveBase else { return }
        interactiveBase = nil
        project = base
        projectDidChange()
    }

    private static func visualChanged(_ a: Project, _ b: Project) -> Bool {
        a.texts != b.texts || a.overlays != b.overlays || a.clips != b.clips || a.overlayClips != b.overlayClips ||
            a.captions != b.captions || a.audioClips != b.audioClips
    }

    func projectDidChange() {
        if let sel = selection, !selectionExists(sel) { selection = nil }
        renderBox?.update(project)
        if interactiveBase == nil {
            rebuild()
        } else {
            playback.refreshFrame()
        }
        autosave.schedule()
    }

    func undo() {
        guard let result = history.undo(current: project) else { return }
        project = result.0
        Haptics.tap()
        showToast("Undo \(result.1)")
        projectDidChange()
    }

    func redo() {
        guard let result = history.redo(current: project) else { return }
        project = result.0
        Haptics.tap()
        showToast("Redo \(result.1)")
        projectDidChange()
    }

    // MARK: Composition

    static func signature(_ p: Project) -> Int {
        var h = Hasher()
        h.combine(p.canvas.aspect)
        func combine(_ c: Clip) {
            h.combine(c.id); h.combine(c.mediaID); h.combine(c.kind)
            h.combine(c.timelineStart); h.combine(c.sourceStart); h.combine(c.sourceEnd)
            h.combine(c.speed); h.combine(c.ramp); h.combine(c.reversed)
            h.combine(c.volume); h.combine(c.muted); h.combine(c.fadeIn); h.combine(c.fadeOut)
            h.combine(c.audioDetached); h.combine(c.denoise); h.combine(c.transitionOut)
        }
        for c in p.clips { combine(c) }
        h.combine(-1)
        for c in p.overlayClips { combine(c) }
        h.combine(-2)
        for a in p.audioClips { h.combine(a) }
        h.combine(Int((p.duration * 600).rounded()))
        for m in p.media { h.combine(m.id); h.combine(m.fileName) }
        return h.finalize()
    }

    /// Rebuilds the AVComposition when timing/media changed; otherwise just re-renders the frame.
    func rebuild(force: Bool = false) {
        let sig = Self.signature(project)
        if !force, sig == lastSignature {
            renderBox?.update(project)
            playback.refreshFrame()
            return
        }
        lastSignature = sig
        rebuildTask?.cancel()
        let snapshot = project
        let paths = self.paths
        rebuildTask = Task { [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            if snapshot.duration <= 0.01 {
                self.playback.clear()
                self.renderBox = nil
                return
            }
            do {
                let built = try await CompositionBuilder.build(project: snapshot, paths: paths, options: .preview(for: snapshot))
                guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
                self.renderBox = built.renderBox
                built.renderBox.update(self.project)
                self.missingMediaCount = built.missingMediaCount
                self.playback.load(built, keepTime: self.playback.currentTime)
            } catch {
                if !Task.isCancelled {
                    self.lastSignature = nil
                    self.alert = AlertInfo(title: "Preview problem", message: "The preview couldn't be updated. \(error.localizedDescription)")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Feedback

    func showToast(_ message: String) {
        toast = message
        toastTask?.cancel()
        toastTask = Task { [weak self] in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 1_600_000_000)
            guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
            withAnimation { self?.toast = nil }
        }
    }

    func show(_ error: Error) {
        if let app = error as? AppError, case .cancelled = app { return }
        alert = AlertInfo(error: error)
        Haptics.warning()
    }

    /// Runs long work behind a progress HUD; the UI stays responsive.
    func runBusy(_ message: String, cancellable: Bool = true, _ work: @escaping @MainActor () async throws -> Void) {
        busyTask?.cancel()
        busy = BusyState(message: message, progress: nil, cancellable: cancellable)
        busyTask = Task { [weak self] in
            do {
                try await work()
            } catch {
                if !(error is CancellationError) { self?.show(error) }
            }
            self?.busy = nil
        }
    }

    func cancelBusy() {
        busyTask?.cancel()
        busy = nil
    }

    func setBusyProgress(_ value: Double) {
        busy?.progress = value
    }

    // MARK: Thumbnails for the home screen

    private func scheduleThumbnailWrite() {
        guard thumbnailWriteTask == nil else { return }
        thumbnailWriteTask = Task { [weak self] in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 4_000_000_000)
            self?.writeThumbnail()
            self?.thumbnailWriteTask = nil
        }
    }

    func writeThumbnail() {
        let resolver = MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: project.media, preferProxy: true)
        let dest = paths.thumbnailFile
        let store = self.store
        guard let first = project.clips.first ?? project.overlayClips.first,
              let url = first.isImage ? resolver.imageURL(for: first.mediaID) : resolver.videoURL(for: first) else {
            try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: dest)
            return
        }
        let time = first.sourceStart + min(0.5, first.sourceDuration / 2)
        let isImage = first.isImage
        Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
            if let img = await ThumbnailCache.posterFrame(url: url, time: time, isImage: isImage),
               let data = img.jpegData(compressionQuality: 0.8) {
                try? data.write(to: dest, options: .atomic)
                await MainActor.run { store.noteThumbnailChanged() }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Selection helpers

    var currentTime: Double { playback.currentTime }

    func selectionExists(_ sel: TimelineSelection?) -> Bool {
        guard let sel else { return false }
        switch sel {
        case .clip(let id): return project.clips.contains { $0.id == id }
        case .overlayClip(let id): return project.overlayClips.contains { $0.id == id }
        case .overlay(let id): return project.overlays.contains { $0.id == id }
        case .text(let id): return project.texts.contains { $0.id == id }
        case .audio(let id): return project.audioClips.contains { $0.id == id }
        case .caption(let id): return project.captions.segments.contains { $0.id == id }
        }
    }

    var selectedClip: Clip? {
        switch selection {
        case .some(.clip(let id)): return project.clips.first { $0.id == id }
        case .some(.overlayClip(let id)): return project.overlayClips.first { $0.id == id }
        default: return nil
        }
    }

    var selectedText: TextItem? {
        if case .text(let id)? = selection { return project.texts.first { $0.id == id } }
        return nil
    }

    var selectedOverlay: OverlayItem? {
        if case .overlay(let id)? = selection { return project.overlays.first { $0.id == id } }
        return nil
    }

    var selectedAudio: AudioClip? {
        if case .audio(let id)? = selection { return project.audioClips.first { $0.id == id } }
        return nil
    }

    var selectedCaption: CaptionSegment? {
        if case .caption(let id)? = selection { return project.captions.segments.first { $0.id == id } }
        return nil
    }

    func media(_ id: UUID?) -> MediaItem? { project.mediaItem(id) }

    func select(_ sel: TimelineSelection?) {
        if sel != selection {
            selection = sel
            if sel != nil { Haptics.tap() }
            scheduleStateSave()
        }
        if sel == nil { activePanel = nil }
    }

    func beats(for mediaID: UUID) -> [Double]? {
        beatCache[mediaID] ?? project.mediaItem(mediaID)?.beats
    }

    /// Beat markers in timeline time across all music clips.
    var timelineBeats: [Double] {
        var result: [Double] = []
        for a in project.audioClips {
            guard let beats = beats(for: a.mediaID) else { continue }
            for b in beats where b >= a.sourceStart && b <= a.sourceEnd {
                result.append(a.timelineStart + (b - a.sourceStart) / a.speed.clamped(0.1, 8))
            }
        }
        return result.sorted()
    }

    var resolver: MediaResolver {
        MediaResolver(paths: paths, media: project.media, preferProxy: true)
    }
}
```
