## `Cutline/ViewModels/PlaybackController.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import AVFoundation
import Observation

/// Owns the AVPlayer, keeps `currentTime` in sync at 60 Hz and performs
/// coalesced ("chase") seeks for instant, smooth scrubbing.
@MainActor
@Observable
final class PlaybackController {
    let player = AVPlayer()
    private(set) var isPlaying = false
    var currentTime: Double = 0
    private(set) var duration: Double = 0
    private(set) var hasItem = false

    @ObservationIgnored private var timeObserver: Any?
    @ObservationIgnored private var endObserver: NSObjectProtocol?
    @ObservationIgnored private var isSeeking = false
    @ObservationIgnored private var pendingSeek: Double?
    @ObservationIgnored private var videoComposition: AVVideoComposition?
    @ObservationIgnored private var refreshScheduled = false
    @ObservationIgnored private var lastRefresh = Date.distantPast
    /// Called whenever playback stops by itself (end reached).
    @ObservationIgnored var onStop: (() -> Void)?

    init() {
        player.automaticallyWaitsToMinimizeStalling = false
        player.actionAtItemEnd = .pause
        timeObserver = player.addPeriodicTimeObserver(forInterval: CMTime(value: 1, timescale: 60), queue: .main) { [weak self] time in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                guard let self, self.isPlaying, !self.isSeeking else { return }
                self.currentTime = min(time.secondsOrZero, self.duration)
            }
        }
        VoiceoverRecorder.restorePlaybackSession()
    }

    func load(_ built: BuiltComposition, keepTime: Double) {
        let wasPlaying = isPlaying
        let item = AVPlayerItem(asset: built.composition)
        item.videoComposition = built.videoComposition
        item.audioMix = built.audioMix
        item.audioTimePitchAlgorithm = .spectral
        item.seekingWaitsForVideoCompositionRendering = true
        videoComposition = built.videoComposition

        if let endObserver { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(endObserver) }
        endObserver = NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: AVPlayerItem.didPlayToEndTimeNotification, object: item, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                guard let self else { return }
                self.isPlaying = false
                self.currentTime = self.duration
                self.onStop?()
            }
        }

        player.replaceCurrentItem(with: item)
        duration = built.duration
        hasItem = true
        let target = min(max(keepTime, 0), max(duration - 0.001, 0))
        currentTime = target
        isSeeking = true
        Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            _ = await self.player.seek(to: target.cmTime, toleranceBefore: .zero, toleranceAfter: .zero)
            self.isSeeking = false
            if wasPlaying {
                self.player.play()
            }
        }
    }

    func clear() {
        player.replaceCurrentItem(with: nil)
        hasItem = false
        duration = 0
        isPlaying = false
        currentTime = 0
    }

    func play() {
        guard hasItem, duration > 0 else { return }
        if currentTime >= duration - 0.05 {
            currentTime = 0
            player.seek(to: .zero, toleranceBefore: .zero, toleranceAfter: .zero)
        }
        player.play()
        isPlaying = true
    }

    func pause() {
        guard isPlaying else { return }
        player.pause()
        isPlaying = false
        let t = player.currentTime().secondsOrZero
        if t.isFinite { currentTime = min(max(t, 0), duration) }
        onStop?()
    }

    func toggle() {
        isPlaying ? pause() : play()
    }

    /// Instant UI update plus coalesced precise seek.
    func scrub(to time: Double) {
        if isPlaying {
            player.pause()
            isPlaying = false
        }
        currentTime = min(max(time, 0), max(duration, 0))
        pendingSeek = currentTime
        if !isSeeking { performPendingSeek() }
    }

    private func performPendingSeek() {
        guard let target = pendingSeek else {
            isSeeking = false
            return
        }
        pendingSeek = nil
        isSeeking = true
        Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
            guard let self else { return }
            _ = await self.player.seek(to: target.cmTime, toleranceBefore: .zero, toleranceAfter: .zero)
            self.performPendingSeek()
        }
    }

    /// Re-renders the current frame after a visual-only change (throttled to ~30 Hz).
    func refreshFrame() {
        guard hasItem, !isPlaying else { return }
        let elapsed = Date().timeIntervalSince(lastRefresh)
        if elapsed >= 1.0 / 30.0 {
            applyRefresh()
        } else if !refreshScheduled {
            refreshScheduled = true
            Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64((1.0 / 30.0 - elapsed) * 1_000_000_000))
                guard let self else { return }
                self.refreshScheduled = false
                self.applyRefresh()
            }
        }
    }

    private func applyRefresh() {
        lastRefresh = Date()
        guard let item = player.currentItem, let vc = videoComposition,
              let copy = vc.mutableCopy() as? AVMutableVideoComposition else { return }
        item.videoComposition = copy
        videoComposition = copy
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Common/Components.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import AVFoundation

enum Theme {
    static let accent = Color(red: 1, green: 0.32, blue: 0.36)
    static let panel = Color(white: 0.11)
    static let card = Color(white: 0.15)
    static let secondaryText = Color.white.opacity(0.6)
}

/// Large icon + label button used in the editor's bottom toolbar.
struct ToolButton: View {
    let title: String
    let icon: String
    var tint: Color = .white
    var isActive = false
    var isEnabled = true
    let action: () -> Void

    var body: some View {
        Button {
            Haptics.tap()
            action()
        } label: {
            VStack(spacing: 5) {
                Image(systemName: icon)
                    .font(.system(size: 21, weight: .medium))
                    .frame(height: 24)
                Text(title)
                    .font(.system(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .lineLimit(1)
                    .minimumScaleFactor(0.8)
            }
            .foregroundStyle(isActive ? Theme.accent : tint)
            .frame(width: 66, height: 60)
            .contentShape(Rectangle())
        }
        .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
        .disabled(!isEnabled)
        .opacity(isEnabled ? 1 : 0.35)
        .accessibilityLabel(title)
    }
}

struct PressableStyle: ButtonStyle {
    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        configuration.label
            .scaleEffect(configuration.isPressed ? 0.92 : 1)
            .opacity(configuration.isPressed ? 0.75 : 1)
            .animation(.easeOut(duration: 0.12), value: configuration.isPressed)
    }
}

/// Chip used for presets (speeds, filters, styles).
struct ChipButton: View {
    let title: String
    var icon: String?
    var isSelected: Bool
    let action: () -> Void

    var body: some View {
        Button {
            Haptics.tap()
            action()
        } label: {
            HStack(spacing: 5) {
                if let icon { Image(systemName: icon).font(.system(size: 12, weight: .semibold)) }
                Text(title).font(.system(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 12)
            .frame(minHeight: 36)
            .background(isSelected ? Theme.accent : Color.white.opacity(0.1), in: Capsule())
            .foregroundStyle(.white)
        }
        .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
    }
}

/// Labeled slider row with a value readout.
struct ValueSlider: View {
    let title: String
    @Binding var value: Double
    var range: ClosedRange<Double>
    var format: (Double) -> String = { String(format: "%.0f", $0 * 100) }
    var onEditingChanged: (Bool) -> Void = { _ in }

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
            HStack {
                Text(title).font(.subheadline)
                Spacer()
                Text(format(value)).font(.subheadline.monospacedDigit()).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            }
            Slider(value: $value, in: range, onEditingChanged: onEditingChanged)
                .tint(Theme.accent)
        }
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }
}

/// Header used at the top of every editor panel.
struct PanelHeader: View {
    let title: String
    var trailing: AnyView?
    let onDone: () -> Void

    var body: some View {
        HStack {
            Text(title).font(.headline)
            Spacer()
            if let trailing { trailing }
            Button("Done") {
                Haptics.tap()
                onDone()
            }
            .font(.headline)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            .frame(minWidth: 44, minHeight: 44)
        }
        .padding(.horizontal)
        .padding(.top, 10)
    }
}

/// Shows an AVPlayer through an AVPlayerLayer.
struct PlayerLayerView: UIViewRepresentable {
    let player: AVPlayer

    final class PlayerUIView: UIView {
        override class var layerClass: AnyClass { AVPlayerLayer.self }
        var playerLayer: AVPlayerLayer { layer as! AVPlayerLayer }
    }

    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> PlayerUIView {
        let view = PlayerUIView()
        view.playerLayer.player = player
        view.playerLayer.videoGravity = .resizeAspect
        view.backgroundColor = .black
        return view
    }

    func updateUIView(_ uiView: PlayerUIView, context: Context) {
        if uiView.playerLayer.player !== player { uiView.playerLayer.player = player }
    }
}

/// UIKit share sheet.
struct ShareSheet: UIViewControllerRepresentable {
    let items: [Any]

    func makeUIViewController(context: Context) -> UIActivityViewController {
        UIActivityViewController(activityItems: items, applicationActivities: nil)
    }

    func updateUIViewController(_ controller: UIActivityViewController, context: Context) {}
}

struct BusyOverlay: View {
    let state: BusyState
    let onCancel: () -> Void

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Color.black.opacity(0.45).ignoresSafeArea()
            VStack(spacing: 14) {
                if let p = state.progress {
                    ProgressView(value: p)
                        .progressViewStyle(.linear)
                        .tint(Theme.accent)
                        .frame(width: 180)
                } else {
                    ProgressView().controlSize(.large).tint(.white)
                }
                Text(state.message).font(.headline)
                if state.cancellable {
                    Button("Cancel", role: .cancel, action: onCancel)
                        .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                }
            }
            .padding(24)
            .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 18))
        }
        .transition(.opacity)
    }
}

struct ToastView: View {
    let message: String
    var body: some View {
        Text(message)
            .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            .padding(.horizontal, 16)
            .padding(.vertical, 10)
            .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: Capsule())
            .transition(.move(edge: .top).combined(with: .opacity))
    }
}

/// Small color row with presets and a full ColorPicker.
struct ColorRow: View {
    let title: String
    @Binding var color: RGBAColor
    var supportsOpacity = false

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            Text(title).font(.subheadline)
            ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                HStack(spacing: 10) {
                    ColorPicker("", selection: Binding(get: { color.color }, set: { color = RGBAColor($0) }),
                                supportsOpacity: supportsOpacity)
                        .labelsHidden()
                        .frame(width: 34, height: 34)
                    ForEach(RGBAColor.palette, id: \.self) { c in
                        Circle()
                            .fill(c.color)
                            .frame(width: 30, height: 30)
                            .overlay(Circle().stroke(Color.white.opacity(sameColor(c) ? 1 : 0.25), lineWidth: sameColor(c) ? 3 : 1))
                            .onTapGesture {
                                Haptics.tap()
                                color = RGBAColor(r: c.r, g: c.g, b: c.b, a: supportsOpacity ? color.a : 1)
                            }
                            .accessibilityLabel("Color")
                    }
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 2)
            }
        }
    }

    private func sameColor(_ c: RGBAColor) -> Bool {
        abs(c.r - color.r) < 0.01 && abs(c.g - color.g) < 0.01 && abs(c.b - color.b) < 0.01
    }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Bottom toolbar whose tools change with the timeline selection.
struct ContextToolbar: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            Divider().overlay(Color.white.opacity(0.08))
            HStack(spacing: 0) {
                if vm.selection != nil {
                    Button {
                        vm.select(nil)
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "chevron.left")
                            .font(.system(size: 17, weight: .semibold))
                            .frame(width: 40, height: 60)
                    }
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    .accessibilityLabel("Back to main tools")
                    Divider().frame(height: 36).overlay(Color.white.opacity(0.15))
                }
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack(spacing: 2) {
                        tools
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 6)
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 70)
        }
        .background(Color(white: 0.05))
        .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.18), value: vm.selection)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var tools: some View {
        switch vm.selection {
        case nil:
            ToolButton(title: "Add Media", icon: "photo.on.rectangle.angled") { vm.presentPicker(.mainTrack) }
            ToolButton(title: "Text", icon: "textformat") { vm.addText() }
            ToolButton(title: "Audio", icon: "music.note") { vm.activePanel = .audio }
            ToolButton(title: "Overlay", icon: "square.on.square") { vm.activePanel = .overlay }
            ToolButton(title: "Captions", icon: "captions.bubble") { vm.activePanel = .captions }
            ToolButton(title: "Filters", icon: "camera.filters") { vm.activePanel = .filters }
            ToolButton(title: "Canvas", icon: "aspectratio") { vm.activePanel = .canvas }
            ToolButton(title: "Export", icon: "square.and.arrow.up", tint: Theme.accent, isEnabled: vm.project.duration > 0.05) {
                vm.playback.pause()
                vm.showExport = true
            }
        case .some(.clip), .some(.overlayClip):
            clipTools
        case .some(.text):
            ToolButton(title: "Edit", icon: "character.cursor.ibeam") { vm.activePanel = .text }
            ToolButton(title: "Animate", icon: "sparkles") { vm.activePanel = .animate }
            ToolButton(title: "Split", icon: "scissors") { vm.splitAtPlayhead() }
            ToolButton(title: "Duplicate", icon: "plus.square.on.square") { vm.duplicateSelection() }
            ToolButton(title: "Delete", icon: "trash", tint: .red) { vm.deleteSelection() }
        case .some(.overlay):
            ToolButton(title: "Style", icon: "paintpalette") { vm.activePanel = .overlayStyle }
            ToolButton(title: "Animate", icon: "sparkles") { vm.activePanel = .animate }
            ToolButton(title: "Split", icon: "scissors") { vm.splitAtPlayhead() }
            ToolButton(title: "Duplicate", icon: "plus.square.on.square") { vm.duplicateSelection() }
            ToolButton(title: "Delete", icon: "trash", tint: .red) { vm.deleteSelection() }
        case .some(.audio):
            ToolButton(title: "Volume", icon: "speaker.wave.2") { vm.activePanel = .volume }
            ToolButton(title: "Split", icon: "scissors") { vm.splitAtPlayhead() }
            ToolButton(title: "Beat Cuts", icon: "metronome") { vm.cutToBeats() }
            ToolButton(title: "Denoise", icon: "waveform.badge.minus", isActive: vm.selectedAudio?.denoise == true) { vm.toggleDenoise() }
            ToolButton(title: "Duplicate", icon: "plus.square.on.square") { vm.duplicateSelection() }
            ToolButton(title: "Delete", icon: "trash", tint: .red) { vm.deleteSelection() }
        case .some(.caption):
            ToolButton(title: "Edit", icon: "character.cursor.ibeam") { vm.activePanel = .captions }
            ToolButton(title: "Delete", icon: "trash", tint: .red) { vm.deleteSelection() }
            ToolButton(title: "Delete All", icon: "trash.slash", tint: .red) { vm.removeAllCaptions() }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var clipTools: some View {
        let clip = vm.selectedClip
        let isVideo = clip?.isImage == false
        ToolButton(title: "Split", icon: "scissors") { vm.splitAtPlayhead() }
        ToolButton(title: "Trim", icon: "timeline.selection") { vm.activePanel = .trim }
        ToolButton(title: "Speed", icon: "speedometer", isEnabled: isVideo) { vm.activePanel = .speed }
        ToolButton(title: "Volume", icon: "speaker.wave.2", isEnabled: isVideo) { vm.activePanel = .volume }
        ToolButton(title: "Crop", icon: "crop.rotate") { vm.activePanel = .crop }
        ToolButton(title: "Animate", icon: "sparkles") { vm.activePanel = .animate }
        ToolButton(title: "Effects", icon: "wand.and.stars") { vm.activePanel = .effects }
        ToolButton(title: "Duplicate", icon: "plus.square.on.square") { vm.duplicateSelection() }
        ToolButton(title: "Delete", icon: "trash", tint: .red) { vm.deleteSelection() }
        ToolButton(title: "Freeze", icon: "snowflake", isEnabled: isVideo) { vm.freezeFrame() }
        ToolButton(title: "Reverse", icon: "arrow.uturn.backward", isActive: clip?.reversed == true, isEnabled: isVideo) { vm.toggleReverse() }
        ToolButton(title: "Detach", icon: "rectangle.and.arrow.up.right.and.arrow.down.left", isEnabled: isVideo && clip?.audioDetached == false) { vm.detachAudio() }
        ToolButton(title: "Copy FX", icon: "doc.on.doc") { vm.copyEffects() }
        ToolButton(title: "Paste FX", icon: "doc.on.clipboard", isEnabled: vm.copiedEffects != nil) { vm.pasteEffects() }
    }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI
import PhotosUI
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

struct EditorView: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    let onClose: () -> Void
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase

    var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                topBar
                if vm.missingMediaCount > 0 { missingBanner }
                PreviewArea(vm: vm)
                    .frame(height: max(200, geo.size.height * 0.42))
                controlRow
                TimelineView(vm: vm)
                    .frame(maxHeight: .infinity)
                ContextToolbar(vm: vm)
            }
        }
        .background(Color.black)
        .overlay(alignment: .top) {
            if let toast = vm.toast {
                ToastView(message: toast)
                    .padding(.top, 54)
                    .allowsHitTesting(false)
            }
        }
        .overlay {
            if let busy = vm.busy {
                BusyOverlay(state: busy) { vm.cancelBusy() }
            }
        }
        .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2), value: vm.toast)
        .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2), value: vm.busy != nil)
        .sheet(item: $vm.activePanel) { panel in
            PanelHost(vm: vm, panel: panel)
                .presentationDetents([.height(340), .medium, .large])
                .presentationBackgroundInteraction(.enabled(upThrough: .height(340)))
                .presentationBackground(Theme.panel)
                .presentationCornerRadius(22)
                .presentationDragIndicator(.visible)
        }
        .photosPicker(isPresented: $vm.showMediaPicker, selection: $vm.pickerItems, maxSelectionCount: 50,
                      selectionBehavior: .ordered,
                      matching: .any(of: [.videos, .images]))
        .onChange(of: vm.pickerItems) { _, items in
            if !items.isEmpty { vm.handlePicked(items) }
        }
        .fileImporter(isPresented: $vm.showMusicImporter, allowedContentTypes: [.audio, .mp3, .mpeg4Audio, .wav, .aiff]) { result in
            switch result {
            case .success(let url): vm.importMusic(url)
            case .failure(let error): vm.show(error)
            }
        }
        .fullScreenCover(isPresented: $vm.showExport) {
            ExportView(vm: vm)
        }
        .alert(vm.alert?.title ?? "", isPresented: Binding(get: { vm.alert != nil }, set: { if !$0 { vm.alert = nil } }),
               presenting: vm.alert) { info in
            if info.showsSettings {
                Button("Open Settings") { Permissions.openSettings() }
            }
            Button("OK", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: { info in
            Text(info.message)
        }
        .onAppear { vm.onAppear() }
        .onChange(of: scenePhase) { _, phase in
            if phase != .active {
                vm.playback.pause()
                vm.flushSave()
            }
        }
        .statusBarHidden(false)
        .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
    }

    // MARK: Top bar

    private var topBar: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            Button {
                Haptics.tap()
                onClose()
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.down")
                    .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .semibold))
                    .frame(width: 44, height: 44)
            }
            .accessibilityLabel("Close project")

            Text(vm.project.name)
                .font(.headline)
                .lineLimit(1)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)

            Menu {
                Picker("Safe area guides", selection: $vm.safeGuide) {
                    ForEach(SafeGuide.allCases) { g in
                        Text(g.title).tag(g)
                    }
                }
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: vm.safeGuide == .none ? "rectangle.dashed" : "rectangle.inset.filled")
                    .font(.system(size: 17, weight: .medium))
                    .frame(width: 44, height: 44)
            }
            .accessibilityLabel("Safe area guides")

            Button {
                Haptics.action()
                vm.playback.pause()
                vm.showExport = true
            } label: {
                Text("Export")
                    .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .bold))
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                    .frame(height: 34)
                    .background(Theme.accent, in: Capsule())
            }
            .disabled(vm.project.duration < 0.05)
            .opacity(vm.project.duration < 0.05 ? 0.4 : 1)
        }
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
        .frame(height: 50)
    }

    private var missingBanner: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
            Text("\(vm.missingMediaCount) media file\(vm.missingMediaCount == 1 ? " is" : "s are") missing. Those clips show a placeholder — delete or replace them.")
                .font(.caption)
            Spacer()
        }
        .padding(8)
        .background(Color.red.opacity(0.25))
    }

    // MARK: Controls

    private var controlRow: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 4) {
            Text("\(TimeFormat.short(vm.playback.currentTime)) / \(TimeFormat.short(vm.project.duration))")
                .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                .frame(minWidth: 110, alignment: .leading)

            Spacer()

            Button {
                Haptics.tap()
                vm.playback.toggle()
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: vm.playback.isPlaying ? "pause.fill" : "play.fill")
                    .font(.system(size: 24))
                    .frame(width: 52, height: 44)
            }
            .accessibilityLabel(vm.playback.isPlaying ? "Pause" : "Play")

            Spacer()

            if vm.selectedVisual != nil {
                Button {
                    vm.toggleKeyframe()
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? "diamond.fill" : "diamond")
                        .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? Theme.accent : .white)
                        .frame(width: 40, height: 44)
                }
                .accessibilityLabel(vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? "Remove keyframe" : "Add keyframe")
            }

            Button {
                Haptics.tap()
                vm.setMagnetic(!vm.project.magnetic)
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: "magnet")
                    .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(vm.project.magnetic ? Theme.accent : .white.opacity(0.6))
                    .frame(width: 40, height: 44)
            }
            .accessibilityLabel(vm.project.magnetic ? "Magnetic timeline on" : "Magnetic timeline off")

            Button { vm.undo() } label: {
                Image(systemName: "arrow.uturn.backward")
                    .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                    .frame(width: 40, height: 44)
            }
            .disabled(!vm.history.canUndo)
            .opacity(vm.history.canUndo ? 1 : 0.35)
            .accessibilityLabel("Undo")

            Button { vm.redo() } label: {
                Image(systemName: "arrow.uturn.forward")
                    .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                    .frame(width: 40, height: 44)
            }
            .disabled(!vm.history.canRedo)
            .opacity(vm.history.canRedo ? 1 : 0.35)
            .accessibilityLabel("Redo")
        }
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
        .padding(.horizontal, 12)
        .frame(height: 46)
    }
}

// MARK: - Preview

struct PreviewArea: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var dragOffset: CGSize = .zero
    @State private var magnification: CGFloat = 1
    @State private var rotation: Angle = .zero
    @State private var dragging = false
    @State private var pinching = false
    @State private var rotating = false
    @State private var snappedCenter = false

    var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let aspect = vm.project.canvas.aspect.ratio
            let fit = fittedSize(container: geo.size, aspect: aspect)
            ZStack {
                Color.black
                ZStack {
                    PlayerLayerView(player: vm.playback.player)
                    if vm.project.isEmpty {
                        emptyState
                    }
                    SafeAreaGuidesView(guide: vm.safeGuide, aspect: vm.project.canvas.aspect)
                        .allowsHitTesting(false)
                    if snappedCenter {
                        Rectangle().fill(Color.yellow).frame(width: 1).allowsHitTesting(false)
                    }
                }
                .frame(width: fit.width, height: fit.height)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4))
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
                .onTapGesture {
                    Haptics.tap()
                    vm.playback.toggle()
                }
                .gesture(dragGesture(size: fit))
                .simultaneousGesture(magnifyGesture)
                .simultaneousGesture(rotateGesture)
                .accessibilityLabel("Preview")
                .accessibilityHint("Tap to play or pause. Drag, pinch or rotate to move the selected item.")
            }
            .frame(width: geo.size.width, height: geo.size.height)
        }
    }

    private var emptyState: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 12) {
            Image(systemName: "plus.rectangle.on.rectangle")
                .font(.system(size: 36))
            Text("Add videos or photos to start")
                .font(.headline)
            Button {
                vm.presentPicker(.mainTrack)
            } label: {
                Label("Add Media", systemImage: "photo.on.rectangle.angled")
                    .font(.headline)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 18)
                    .frame(height: 44)
                    .background(Theme.accent, in: Capsule())
            }
        }
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
    }

    private func fittedSize(container: CGSize, aspect: CGFloat) -> CGSize {
        let padded = CGSize(width: max(container.width - 16, 1), height: max(container.height - 8, 1))
        if padded.width / padded.height > aspect {
            return CGSize(width: padded.height * aspect, height: padded.height)
        }
        return CGSize(width: padded.width, height: padded.width / aspect)
    }

    private func dragGesture(size: CGSize) -> some Gesture {
        DragGesture(minimumDistance: 4)
            .onChanged { value in
                guard vm.selectedVisual != nil else { return }
                dragging = true
                dragOffset = value.translation
                applyLive(size: size)
            }
            .onEnded { _ in
                dragging = false
                finishIfIdle()
            }
    }

    private var magnifyGesture: some Gesture {
        MagnifyGesture()
            .onChanged { value in
                guard vm.selectedVisual != nil else { return }
                pinching = true
                magnification = value.magnification
                applyLive(size: nil)
            }
            .onEnded { _ in
                pinching = false
                finishIfIdle()
            }
    }

    private var rotateGesture: some Gesture {
        RotateGesture()
            .onChanged { value in
                guard vm.selectedVisual != nil else { return }
                rotating = true
                rotation = value.rotation
                applyLive(size: nil)
            }
            .onEnded { _ in
                rotating = false
                finishIfIdle()
            }
    }

    @State private var lastSize: CGSize = CGSize(width: 1, height: 1)

    private func applyLive(size: CGSize?) {
        if let size { lastSize = size }
        let dx = Double(dragOffset.width / max(lastSize.width, 1))
        let dy = Double(dragOffset.height / max(lastSize.height, 1))
        let mag = Double(magnification)
        let rot = rotation.degrees
        var didSnap = false
        vm.interactiveTransform { t in
            var nx = t.x + dx
            if abs(nx) < 0.015 {
                nx = 0
                didSnap = true
            }
            t.x = nx
            t.y += dy
            t.scale = (t.scale * mag).clamped(0.05, 10)
            t.rotation += rot
        }
        if didSnap != snappedCenter {
            snappedCenter = didSnap
            if didSnap { Haptics.snap() }
        }
    }

    private func finishIfIdle() {
        guard !dragging && !pinching && !rotating else { return }
        dragOffset = .zero
        magnification = 1
        rotation = .zero
        snappedCenter = false
        if vm.interactiveBase != nil { vm.endInteractive("Transform") }
    }
}

// MARK: - Safe area guides

struct SafeAreaGuidesView: View {
    let guide: SafeGuide
    let aspect: AspectRatio

    var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let w = geo.size.width, h = geo.size.height
            ForEach(Array(zones(w: w, h: h).enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, zone in
                Rectangle()
                    .fill(Color.red.opacity(0.18))
                    .overlay(Rectangle().stroke(Color.red.opacity(0.6), style: StrokeStyle(lineWidth: 1, dash: [4, 3])))
                    .overlay(alignment: .topLeading) {
                        Text(zone.1).font(.system(size: 8, weight: .bold)).foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.85)).padding(3)
                    }
                    .frame(width: zone.0.width, height: zone.0.height)
                    .position(x: zone.0.midX, y: zone.0.midY)
            }
        }
    }

    private func zones(w: CGFloat, h: CGFloat) -> [(CGRect, String)] {
        switch guide {
        case .none:
            return []
        case .tiktok:
            return [
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: w, height: h * 0.1), "Top bar"),
                (CGRect(x: w * 0.84, y: h * 0.38, width: w * 0.16, height: h * 0.5), "Buttons"),
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: h * 0.8, width: w * 0.84, height: h * 0.2), "Caption")
            ]
        case .reels:
            return [
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: w, height: h * 0.08), "Top bar"),
                (CGRect(x: w * 0.85, y: h * 0.48, width: w * 0.15, height: h * 0.42), "Buttons"),
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: h * 0.82, width: w * 0.85, height: h * 0.18), "Caption")
            ]
        case .shorts:
            return [
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: w, height: h * 0.08), "Top bar"),
                (CGRect(x: w * 0.84, y: h * 0.42, width: w * 0.16, height: h * 0.45), "Buttons"),
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: h * 0.8, width: w * 0.84, height: h * 0.2), "Title")
            ]
        case .youtube:
            return [
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: w, height: h * 0.12), "Title"),
                (CGRect(x: 0, y: h * 0.86, width: w, height: h * 0.14), "Controls"),
                (CGRect(x: w * 0.72, y: h * 0.7, width: w * 0.28, height: h * 0.16), "End card")
            ]
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Export/ExportView.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

@MainActor
@Observable
final class ExportViewModel {
    enum Phase: Equatable {
        case settings
        case exporting
        case done(URL)
        case failed(String)
    }

    var settings = ExportSettings()
    var phase: Phase = .settings
    var progress: Double = 0
    var shareURL: URL?
    var saving = false
    var savedMessage: String?
    var extraBusy: String?

    @ObservationIgnored private var service: ExportService?
    @ObservationIgnored private var task: Task<Void, Never>?
    @ObservationIgnored private var backgroundTask: UIBackgroundTaskIdentifier = .invalid

    func estimate(for project: Project) -> String {
        let bytes = settings.estimatedBytes(duration: project.duration, aspect: project.canvas.aspect)
        let f = ByteCountFormatter()
        f.countStyle = .file
        return f.string(fromByteCount: bytes)
    }

    func start(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths) {
        let service = ExportService()
        self.service = service
        phase = .exporting
        progress = 0
        beginBackgroundTask()
        let settings = self.settings
        UIApplication.shared.isIdleTimerDisabled = true
        task = Task { [weak self] in
            do {
                let sink = ProgressSink { value in self?.progress = value }
                let url = try await service.exportVideo(project: project, paths: paths, settings: settings) { value in
                    Task { @MainActor in sink.send(value) }
                }
                self?.phase = .done(url)
                Haptics.success()
            } catch {
                if let app = error as? AppError, case .cancelled = app {
                    self?.phase = .settings
                } else {
                    self?.phase = .failed(AlertInfo(error: error).message)
                    Haptics.warning()
                }
            }
            UIApplication.shared.isIdleTimerDisabled = false
            self?.endBackgroundTask()
        }
    }

    func cancel() {
        service?.cancel()
        task?.cancel()
    }

    private func beginBackgroundTask() {
        backgroundTask = UIApplication.shared.beginBackgroundTask(withName: "Cutline export") { [weak self] in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                self?.service?.cancel()
                self?.endBackgroundTask()
            }
        }
    }

    private func endBackgroundTask() {
        if backgroundTask != .invalid {
            UIApplication.shared.endBackgroundTask(backgroundTask)
            backgroundTask = .invalid
        }
    }

    func saveToPhotos(_ url: URL) {
        saving = true
        Task { [weak self] in
            do {
                try await ExportService.saveToPhotos(url)
                self?.savedMessage = "Saved to Photos"
                Haptics.success()
            } catch {
                self?.savedMessage = AlertInfo(error: error).message
                Haptics.warning()
            }
            self?.saving = false
        }
    }

    func exportAudio(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths) {
        extraBusy = "Exporting audio…"
        Task { [weak self] in
            do {
                let url = try await ExportService().exportAudio(project: project, paths: paths)
                self?.shareURL = url
            } catch {
                self?.savedMessage = AlertInfo(error: error).message
            }
            self?.extraBusy = nil
        }
    }

    func exportStill(project: Project, paths: ProjectPaths, time: Double, share: Bool) {
        extraBusy = "Rendering frame…"
        let resolution = settings.resolution
        Task { [weak self] in
            do {
                let url = try await ExportService().exportStill(project: project, paths: paths, at: time, resolution: resolution)
                if share {
                    self?.shareURL = url
                } else {
                    try await ExportService.saveToPhotos(url)
                    self?.savedMessage = "Frame saved to Photos"
                    Haptics.success()
                }
            } catch {
                self?.savedMessage = AlertInfo(error: error).message
            }
            self?.extraBusy = nil
        }
    }
}

/// Main-actor progress forwarder that can be captured by the exporter's background callback.
@MainActor
final class ProgressSink {
    private let handler: @MainActor (Double) -> Void
    init(_ handler: @escaping @MainActor (Double) -> Void) { self.handler = handler }
    func send(_ value: Double) { handler(value) }
}

struct ExportView: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var model = ExportViewModel()
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ZStack {
                Color.black.ignoresSafeArea()
                switch model.phase {
                case .settings:
                    settingsView
                case .exporting:
                    progressView
                case .done(let url):
                    doneView(url)
                case .failed(let message):
                    failedView(message)
                }
                if let busy = model.extraBusy {
                    BusyOverlay(state: BusyState(message: busy, progress: nil, cancellable: false)) {}
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Export")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                    if model.phase != .exporting {
                        Button("Close") { dismiss() }
                    }
                }
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: Binding(get: { model.shareURL != nil }, set: { if !$0 { model.shareURL = nil } })) {
                if let url = model.shareURL {
                    ShareSheet(items: [url]).presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
                }
            }
            .alert(model.savedMessage ?? "", isPresented: Binding(get: { model.savedMessage != nil }, set: { if !$0 { model.savedMessage = nil } })) {
                Button("OK", role: .cancel) {}
            }
        }
        .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
        .tint(Theme.accent)
        .interactiveDismissDisabled(model.phase == .exporting)
    }

    // MARK: Settings

    private var settingsView: some View {
        Form {
            Section {
                Picker("Resolution", selection: $model.settings.resolution) {
                    ForEach(ExportResolution.allCases) { r in Text(r.title).tag(r) }
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                Picker("Frame rate", selection: $model.settings.frameRate) {
                    ForEach([24, 30, 60], id: \.self) { f in Text("\(f) fps").tag(f) }
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                Picker("Format", selection: $model.settings.codec) {
                    ForEach(ExportCodec.allCases) { c in Text("MP4 · \(c.title)").tag(c) }
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Quality")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(qualityLabel).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                    }
                    Slider(value: $model.settings.quality, in: 0...1)
                }
            } header: {
                Text("Video")
            } footer: {
                let size = model.settings.renderSize(for: vm.project.canvas.aspect)
                Text("\(Int(size.width))×\(Int(size.height)) · \(TimeFormat.short(vm.project.duration)) · about \(model.estimate(for: vm.project)). No watermark.")
            }

            Section {
                Button {
                    Haptics.action()
                    vm.flushSave()
                    model.start(project: vm.project, paths: vm.paths)
                } label: {
                    Label("Export Video", systemImage: "square.and.arrow.up.fill")
                        .font(.headline)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
            }

            Section("More") {
                Button {
                    model.exportAudio(project: vm.project, paths: vm.paths)
                } label: {
                    Label("Export audio only (M4A)", systemImage: "waveform")
                }
                Button {
                    model.exportStill(project: vm.project, paths: vm.paths, time: vm.currentTime, share: false)
                } label: {
                    Label("Save frame at playhead to Photos", systemImage: "photo")
                }
                Button {
                    model.exportStill(project: vm.project, paths: vm.paths, time: vm.currentTime, share: true)
                } label: {
                    Label("Share frame at playhead", systemImage: "square.and.arrow.up")
                }
            }
        }
        .scrollContentBackground(.hidden)
    }

    private var qualityLabel: String {
        switch model.settings.quality {
        case ..<0.34: return "Smaller file"
        case ..<0.75: return "Recommended"
        default: return "Best"
        }
    }

    // MARK: Progress

    private var progressView: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 24) {
            ZStack {
                Circle().stroke(Color.white.opacity(0.1), lineWidth: 10)
                Circle()
                    .trim(from: 0, to: model.progress)
                    .stroke(Theme.accent, style: StrokeStyle(lineWidth: 10, lineCap: .round))
                    .rotationEffect(.degrees(-90))
                    .animation(.linear(duration: 0.2), value: model.progress)
                Text("\(Int(model.progress * 100))%")
                    .font(.system(size: 34, weight: .bold).monospacedDigit())
            }
            .frame(width: 170, height: 170)
            Text("Exporting your video…").font(.headline)
            Text("Keep Cutline open for the fastest export. If you switch apps, iOS gives it a little time to finish.")
                .font(.footnote)
                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                .padding(.horizontal, 30)
            Button("Cancel", role: .destructive) { model.cancel() }
                .buttonStyle(.bordered)
        }
        .padding()
    }

    // MARK: Done

    private func doneView(_ url: URL) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 18) {
            Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                .font(.system(size: 64))
                .foregroundStyle(.green)
            Text("Your video is ready").font(.title2.bold())
            Text(fileSize(url)).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            Button {
                model.saveToPhotos(url)
            } label: {
                Label(model.saving ? "Saving…" : "Save to Photos", systemImage: "photo.on.rectangle")
                    .font(.headline)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 50)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
            .disabled(model.saving)
            Button {
                model.shareURL = url
            } label: {
                Label("Share…", systemImage: "square.and.arrow.up")
                    .font(.headline)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 50)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            Button("Done") { dismiss() }
                .padding(.top, 8)
        }
        .padding(30)
    }

    private func failedView(_ message: String) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 16) {
            Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                .font(.system(size: 54))
                .foregroundStyle(.yellow)
            Text("Export didn't finish").font(.title3.bold())
            Text(message).multilineTextAlignment(.center).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            Button("Try again") { model.phase = .settings }
                .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
        }
        .padding(30)
    }

    private func fileSize(_ url: URL) -> String {
        let f = ByteCountFormatter()
        f.countStyle = .file
        return f.string(fromByteCount: StorageGuard.fileSize(url))
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Home/HomeView.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI
import PhotosUI

struct HomeView: View {
    @Environment(ProjectStore.self) private var store
    @State private var editor: EditorViewModel?
    @State private var search = ""
    @State private var pickerItems: [PhotosPickerItem] = []
    @State private var showPicker = false
    @State private var pendingAspect: AspectRatio?
    @State private var renaming: ProjectSummary?
    @State private var renameText = ""
    @State private var deleting: ProjectSummary?
    @State private var alert: AlertInfo?
    @State private var didRestoreSession = false

    private var filtered: [ProjectSummary] {
        let q = search.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard !q.isEmpty else { return store.summaries }
        return store.summaries.filter { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(q) }
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 22) {
                    templates
                    if store.hasLoaded && store.summaries.isEmpty {
                        emptyState
                    } else {
                        projectGrid
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .padding(.bottom, 40)
            }
            .background(Color.black)
            .navigationTitle("Cutline")
            .searchable(text: $search, prompt: "Search projects")
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
                    Button {
                        Haptics.action()
                        pendingAspect = nil
                        showPicker = true
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "plus")
                            .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .bold))
                            .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                            .background(Theme.accent, in: Circle())
                            .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    }
                    .accessibilityLabel("New project")
                }
            }
        }
        .photosPicker(isPresented: $showPicker, selection: $pickerItems, maxSelectionCount: 50,
                      selectionBehavior: .ordered, matching: .any(of: [.videos, .images]))
        .onChange(of: pickerItems) { _, items in
            guard !items.isEmpty else { return }
            createProject(with: items)
            pickerItems = []
        }
        .fullScreenCover(item: $editor) { vm in
            EditorView(vm: vm) {
                vm.close()
                editor = nil
            }
        }
        .alert(alert?.title ?? "", isPresented: Binding(get: { alert != nil }, set: { if !$0 { alert = nil } }), presenting: alert) { _ in
            Button("OK", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: { info in
            Text(info.message)
        }
        .alert("Rename project", isPresented: Binding(get: { renaming != nil }, set: { if !$0 { renaming = nil } })) {
            TextField("Name", text: $renameText)
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
            Button("Save") {
                if let p = renaming {
                    Task { await store.rename(p.id, to: renameText) }
                }
            }
        }
        .confirmationDialog("Delete “\(deleting?.name ?? "")”?", isPresented: Binding(get: { deleting != nil }, set: { if !$0 { deleting = nil } }),
                            titleVisibility: .visible) {
            Button("Delete project", role: .destructive) {
                if let p = deleting {
                    Haptics.warning()
                    Task { await store.delete(p.id) }
                }
            }
        } message: {
            Text("The project and its imported media will be removed from this device. Your originals in Photos aren't affected.")
        }
        .task {
            await store.refresh()
            restoreSessionIfNeeded()
        }
        .onChange(of: editor == nil) { _, closed in
            if closed { Task { await store.refresh() } }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Sections

    private var templates: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text("Start a new video").font(.headline)
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                templateButton(.portrait9x16, title: "9:16", subtitle: "TikTok · Reels · Shorts")
                templateButton(.square1x1, title: "1:1", subtitle: "Square")
                templateButton(.landscape16x9, title: "16:9", subtitle: "YouTube")
            }
        }
        .padding(.top, 6)
    }

    private func templateButton(_ aspect: AspectRatio, title: String, subtitle: String) -> some View {
        Button {
            Haptics.action()
            pendingAspect = aspect
            showPicker = true
        } label: {
            VStack(spacing: 8) {
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4)
                    .stroke(Theme.accent, lineWidth: 2.5)
                    .aspectRatio(aspect.ratio, contentMode: .fit)
                    .frame(height: 44)
                Text(title).font(.headline)
                Text(subtitle)
                    .font(.caption2)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                    .lineLimit(2)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 124)
            .padding(.vertical, 6)
            .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
        }
        .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
        .accessibilityLabel("New \(title) project for \(subtitle)")
    }

    private var emptyState: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 14) {
            Image(systemName: "film.stack")
                .font(.system(size: 46))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            Text("Make your first video").font(.title3.bold())
            Text("Pick a format above, or tap + and choose your clips. Cutline lays them out so you can edit right away.")
                .font(.subheadline)
                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            Button {
                pendingAspect = nil
                showPicker = true
            } label: {
                Label("Choose videos & photos", systemImage: "photo.on.rectangle.angled")
                    .font(.headline)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                    .frame(height: 50)
                    .background(Theme.accent, in: Capsule())
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        .padding(.vertical, 40)
    }

    private var projectGrid: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text(search.isEmpty ? "Projects" : "Results").font(.headline)
            if filtered.isEmpty && !search.isEmpty {
                Text("No projects match “\(search)”.").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            }
            LazyVGrid(columns: [GridItem(.adaptive(minimum: 150), spacing: 14)], spacing: 16) {
                ForEach(filtered) { summary in
                    ProjectCard(summary: summary, revision: store.thumbnailRevision)
                        .onTapGesture { open(summary) }
                        .contextMenu {
                            Button {
                                renameText = summary.name
                                renaming = summary
                            } label: { Label("Rename", systemImage: "pencil") }
                            Button {
                                Task {
                                    do { try await store.duplicate(summary.id) } catch { alert = AlertInfo(error: error) }
                                }
                            } label: { Label("Duplicate", systemImage: "plus.square.on.square") }
                            Button(role: .destructive) {
                                deleting = summary
                            } label: { Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash") }
                        }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Actions

    private func open(_ summary: ProjectSummary) {
        Haptics.tap()
        if summary.isCorrupted {
            alert = AlertInfo(title: "Can't open project", message: AppError.corruptedProject.errorDescription ?? "")
            deleting = summary
            return
        }
        do {
            let (project, recovered) = try store.load(summary.id)
            editor = EditorViewModel(project: project, store: store, recovered: recovered)
        } catch {
            alert = AlertInfo(error: error)
        }
    }

    private func createProject(with items: [PhotosPickerItem]) {
        do {
            let project = try store.createProject(aspect: pendingAspect ?? .portrait9x16)
            let vm = EditorViewModel(project: project, store: store, autoAspect: pendingAspect == nil)
            vm.pickerTarget = .mainTrack
            editor = vm
            vm.handlePicked(items)
            Task { await store.refresh() }
        } catch {
            alert = AlertInfo(error: error)
        }
    }

    /// Reopens the project that was open when the app last quit unexpectedly.
    private func restoreSessionIfNeeded() {
        guard !didRestoreSession, editor == nil else { return }
        didRestoreSession = true
        guard let idString = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: EditorViewModel.sessionKey),
              let id = UUID(uuidString: idString) else { return }
        guard store.exists(id), let loaded = try? store.load(id) else {
            UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: EditorViewModel.sessionKey)
            return
        }
        let vm = EditorViewModel(project: loaded.0, store: store, recovered: loaded.1)
        vm.openingToast = "Restored where you left off"
        editor = vm
    }
}

struct ProjectCard: View {
    let summary: ProjectSummary
    let revision: Int
    @State private var image: UIImage?

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            ZStack {
                Theme.card
                if let image {
                    Image(uiImage: image)
                        .resizable()
                        .scaledToFill()
                } else {
                    Image(systemName: summary.isCorrupted ? "exclamationmark.triangle" : "film")
                        .font(.system(size: 30))
                        .foregroundStyle(summary.isCorrupted ? .yellow : Theme.secondaryText)
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 190)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14))
            .overlay(alignment: .topTrailing) {
                Text(summary.aspect.title)
                    .font(.caption2.bold())
                    .padding(.horizontal, 6)
                    .padding(.vertical, 3)
                    .background(.black.opacity(0.6), in: Capsule())
                    .padding(6)
            }
            .overlay(alignment: .bottomTrailing) {
                if summary.duration > 0 {
                    Text(TimeFormat.clock(summary.duration))
                        .font(.caption2.bold().monospacedDigit())
                        .padding(.horizontal, 6)
                        .padding(.vertical, 3)
                        .background(.black.opacity(0.6), in: Capsule())
                        .padding(6)
                }
            }
            Text(summary.name).font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold)).lineLimit(1)
            Text(summary.modifiedAt, format: .relative(presentation: .named))
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
        }
        .contentShape(Rectangle())
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
        .accessibilityHint("Opens the project. Touch and hold for more options.")
        .task(id: "\(summary.id)-\(revision)-\(summary.modifiedAt.timeIntervalSince1970)") {
            let url = summary.thumbnailURL
            image = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) { UIImage(contentsOfFile: url.path) }.value
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Panels/AddPanels.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI

// MARK: - Audio

struct AudioPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Audio") {
                if vm.recorder.isRecording { vm.stopVoiceover() }
                vm.activePanel = nil
            }
            VStack(spacing: 12) {
                if vm.recorder.isRecording {
                    recordingView
                } else {
                    bigButton("Music from Files", subtitle: "MP3, M4A, WAV, AIFF — beats are detected automatically",
                              icon: "music.note.list") {
                        vm.presentMusicImporter()
                    }
                    bigButton("Record Voiceover", subtitle: "Records from the playhead while your video plays",
                              icon: "mic.fill") {
                        vm.toggleVoiceover()
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal)
            Spacer()
        }
    }

    private var recordingView: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 14) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Circle().fill(Color.red).frame(width: 12, height: 12)
                Text("Recording  \(TimeFormat.short(vm.recorder.elapsed))")
                    .font(.headline.monospacedDigit())
            }
            GeometryReader { geo in
                Capsule().fill(Color.white.opacity(0.1))
                    .overlay(alignment: .leading) {
                        Capsule().fill(Color.red).frame(width: geo.size.width * CGFloat(vm.recorder.level))
                    }
            }
            .frame(height: 8)
            Button {
                vm.stopVoiceover()
            } label: {
                Label("Stop", systemImage: "stop.fill")
                    .font(.headline)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 50)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
            .tint(.red)
        }
    }

    private func bigButton(_ title: String, subtitle: String, icon: String, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button {
            Haptics.tap()
            action()
        } label: {
            HStack(spacing: 14) {
                Image(systemName: icon)
                    .font(.system(size: 22))
                    .frame(width: 48, height: 48)
                    .background(Theme.accent.opacity(0.2), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text(title).font(.headline)
                    Text(subtitle).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText).multilineTextAlignment(.leading)
                }
                Spacer()
                Image(systemName: "chevron.right").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            }
            .padding(12)
            .background(Color.white.opacity(0.06), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
        }
        .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
    }
}

// MARK: - Overlay

struct OverlayPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var tab = 0
    @State private var shapeColor: RGBAColor = .accent

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Overlay") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            Picker("", selection: $tab) {
                Text("Stickers").tag(0)
                Text("Shapes").tag(1)
                Text("Photo / Video").tag(2)
            }
            .pickerStyle(.segmented)
            .padding(.horizontal)
            ScrollView {
                switch tab {
                case 0:
                    LazyVGrid(columns: Array(repeating: GridItem(.flexible()), count: 6), spacing: 10) {
                        ForEach(OverlayItem.stickerCatalog, id: \.self) { emoji in
                            Button {
                                vm.addSticker(emoji)
                                vm.activePanel = nil
                            } label: {
                                Text(emoji).font(.system(size: 34)).frame(width: 52, height: 52)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                case 1:
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                        ColorRow(title: "Color", color: $shapeColor)
                        LazyVGrid(columns: Array(repeating: GridItem(.flexible()), count: 4), spacing: 10) {
                            ForEach(ShapeKind.allCases) { shape in
                                Button {
                                    vm.addShape(shape, color: shapeColor)
                                    vm.activePanel = nil
                                } label: {
                                    VStack(spacing: 6) {
                                        Image(systemName: shape.icon).font(.system(size: 26)).foregroundStyle(shapeColor.color)
                                        Text(shape.title).font(.caption2)
                                    }
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 64)
                                    .background(Color.white.opacity(0.06), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
                                .foregroundStyle(.white)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                default:
                    VStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Button {
                            vm.presentPicker(.overlay)
                        } label: {
                            Label("Choose photo or video", systemImage: "pip.enter")
                                .font(.headline)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 50)
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                        .tint(Theme.accent)
                        Text("Picture-in-picture is added at the playhead. Drag it in the preview, pinch to resize. Use Effects › Keying for green screen.")
                            .font(.footnote)
                            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

struct OverlayStylePanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Style") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let item = vm.selectedOverlay {
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        if item.kind == .shape {
                            ColorRow(title: "Color", color: vm.overlayBinding(\.color, default: .accent, name: "Shape Color"))
                            Toggle("Filled", isOn: vm.overlayBinding(\.filled, default: true, name: "Fill"))
                        }
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Opacity", value: vm.transformBinding(\.opacity, name: "Opacity"), range: 0...1)
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Size", value: vm.transformBinding(\.scale, name: "Scale"), range: 0.1...4,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Rotation", value: vm.transformBinding(\.rotation, name: "Rotation"), range: -180...180,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.0f°", $0) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Duration", value: vm.overlayBinding(\.duration, default: 3, name: "Duration"), range: 0.3...30,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                }
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a sticker, shape or photo.")
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Captions

struct CaptionsPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var locale: Locale = CaptionService.defaultLocale()
    @State private var locales: [Locale] = []
    @State private var style: CaptionStyle = .boldCenter

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Captions") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    if vm.project.captions.segments.isEmpty {
                        generator
                    } else {
                        editor
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .padding(.bottom, 20)
            }
        }
        .task {
            let found = await Task.detached { CaptionService.onDeviceLocales() }.value
            locales = found
            if !found.contains(where: { $0.identifier == locale.identifier }), let first = found.first {
                locale = first
            }
        }
    }

    private var stylePicker: some View {
        HStack {
            ForEach(CaptionStyle.allCases) { s in
                ChipButton(title: s.title, isSelected: (vm.project.captions.segments.isEmpty ? style : vm.project.captions.style) == s) {
                    if vm.project.captions.segments.isEmpty { style = s } else { vm.setCaptionStyle(s) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var generator: some View {
        Text("Turn speech into captions with word-by-word timing. Runs fully on your device.")
            .font(.footnote)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
        if !locales.isEmpty {
            Picker("Language", selection: Binding(get: { locale.identifier }, set: { locale = Locale(identifier: $0) })) {
                ForEach(locales, id: \.identifier) { l in
                    Text(Locale.current.localizedString(forIdentifier: l.identifier) ?? l.identifier).tag(l.identifier)
                }
            }
            .pickerStyle(.menu)
        }
        Text("Style").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
        stylePicker
        Button {
            vm.activePanel = nil
            vm.generateCaptions(locale: locale, style: style)
        } label: {
            Label("Generate captions", systemImage: "captions.bubble.fill")
                .font(.headline)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 50)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
        .tint(Theme.accent)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var editor: some View {
        Text("Style").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
        stylePicker
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Position", value: vm.projectBinding(\.captions.verticalPosition, name: "Caption Position"),
                   range: 0.08...0.92, format: { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) })
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Size", value: vm.projectBinding(\.captions.fontScale, name: "Caption Size"),
                   range: 0.5...2, format: { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) })
        ColorRow(title: "Text color", color: vm.projectBinding(\.captions.textColor, name: "Caption Color"))
        if vm.project.captions.style != .minimalBottom {
            ColorRow(title: "Highlight color", color: vm.projectBinding(\.captions.highlightColor, name: "Highlight Color"))
        }
        Text("Edit text").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
        ForEach(vm.project.captions.segments) { segment in
            HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 8) {
                Button {
                    vm.playback.scrub(to: segment.start + 0.01)
                    vm.select(.caption(segment.id))
                } label: {
                    Text(TimeFormat.short(segment.start))
                        .font(.caption.monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                        .frame(width: 56, alignment: .leading)
                        .padding(.top, 10)
                }
                TextField("Caption", text: Binding(get: { segment.text }, set: { vm.updateCaptionText(segment.id, text: $0) }), axis: .vertical)
                    .padding(8)
                    .background(Color.white.opacity(0.06), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
            }
        }
        HStack {
            Button(role: .destructive) {
                vm.removeAllCaptions()
            } label: {
                Label("Remove all", systemImage: "trash").frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            Button {
                vm.activePanel = nil
                vm.generateCaptions(locale: locale, style: vm.project.captions.style)
            } label: {
                Label("Regenerate", systemImage: "arrow.clockwise").frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Canvas

struct CanvasPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Canvas") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    Text("Format").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        ForEach(AspectRatio.allCases) { a in
                            Button {
                                Haptics.tap()
                                vm.setCanvasAspect(a)
                            } label: {
                                VStack(spacing: 6) {
                                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 3)
                                        .stroke(lineWidth: 2)
                                        .aspectRatio(a.ratio, contentMode: .fit)
                                        .frame(height: 30)
                                    Text(a.title).font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                                }
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 66)
                                .background(vm.project.canvas.aspect == a ? Theme.accent.opacity(0.35) : Color.white.opacity(0.06),
                                            in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
                            .foregroundStyle(.white)
                            .accessibilityLabel("\(a.title) \(a.subtitle)")
                        }
                    }
                    Text("Background").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    HStack {
                        ForEach(BackgroundStyle.allCases) { b in
                            ChipButton(title: b.title, isSelected: vm.project.canvas.background == b) {
                                vm.perform("Background") { $0.canvas.background = b }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if vm.project.canvas.background == .blur {
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Blur", value: vm.projectBinding(\.canvas.blurAmount, name: "Blur"), range: 0...1)
                    } else if vm.project.canvas.background == .color {
                        ColorRow(title: "Color", color: vm.projectBinding(\.canvas.backgroundColor, name: "Background Color"))
                    }
                    Toggle(isOn: Binding(get: { vm.project.magnetic }, set: { vm.setMagnetic($0) })) {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                            Text("Magnetic timeline")
                            Text("Clips snap together and gaps close automatically").font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                        }
                    }
                    Text("Per-clip Fit / Fill is under Crop when a clip is selected.")
                        .font(.footnote)
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
            }
        }
    }
}
```
