## `Cutline/Views/Panels/AnimatePanel.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// In/out animations plus transform keyframes (position, scale, rotation, opacity) with easing.
struct AnimatePanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var tab = 0

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Animate") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            Picker("", selection: $tab) {
                Text("In & Out").tag(0)
                Text("Keyframes").tag(1)
            }
            .pickerStyle(.segmented)
            .padding(.horizontal)
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    if tab == 0 { inOut } else { keyframes }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .padding(.bottom, 20)
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: In & Out

    @ViewBuilder
    private var inOut: some View {
        if let text = vm.selectedText {
            Text("In").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            textChips(selected: text.animationIn) { a in vm.updateText(text.id, "Text Animation") { $0.animationIn = a } }
            Text("Out").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            textChips(selected: text.animationOut) { a in vm.updateText(text.id, "Text Animation") { $0.animationOut = a } }
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Animation length", value: vm.textBinding(\.animationDuration, default: 0.4, name: "Animation Length"),
                       range: 0.1...2, format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
        } else if let clip = vm.selectedClip {
            Text("In").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            motionChips(selected: clip.animationIn) { m in vm.updateSelectedClip("Animation") { $0.animationIn = m } }
            Text("Out").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            motionChips(selected: clip.animationOut) { m in vm.updateSelectedClip("Animation") { $0.animationOut = m } }
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Animation length", value: vm.clipBinding(\.animationDuration, default: 0.5, name: "Animation Length"),
                       range: 0.1...3, format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
        } else if let overlay = vm.selectedOverlay {
            Text("In").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            motionChips(selected: overlay.animationIn) { m in vm.updateOverlay(overlay.id, "Animation") { $0.animationIn = m } }
            Text("Out").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
            motionChips(selected: overlay.animationOut) { m in vm.updateOverlay(overlay.id, "Animation") { $0.animationOut = m } }
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Animation length", value: vm.overlayBinding(\.animationDuration, default: 0.35, name: "Animation Length"),
                       range: 0.1...3, format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
        } else {
            EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a clip, sticker or text to animate it.")
        }
    }

    private func motionChips(selected: MotionPreset, onSelect: @escaping (MotionPreset) -> Void) -> some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack {
                ForEach(MotionPreset.allCases) { m in
                    ChipButton(title: m.title, icon: m.icon, isSelected: selected == m) { onSelect(m) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func textChips(selected: TextAnimation, onSelect: @escaping (TextAnimation) -> Void) -> some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack {
                ForEach(TextAnimation.allCases) { a in
                    ChipButton(title: a.title, icon: a.icon, isSelected: selected == a) { onSelect(a) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Keyframes

    @ViewBuilder
    private var keyframes: some View {
        if let visual = vm.selectedVisual {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Button {
                    vm.toggleKeyframe()
                } label: {
                    Label(vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? "Remove keyframe" : "Add keyframe here",
                          systemImage: vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? "diamond.fill" : "diamond")
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                .tint(vm.hasKeyframeAtPlayhead ? .gray : Theme.accent)
                if !visual.keyframes.isEmpty {
                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                        vm.clearKeyframes()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "trash").frame(width: 44, height: 44)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                    .accessibilityLabel("Clear all keyframes")
                }
            }
            Text(visual.keyframes.isEmpty
                 ? "Set the look below, add a keyframe, move the playhead, then change it again. Cutline animates between keyframes."
                 : "\(visual.keyframes.count) keyframe\(visual.keyframes.count == 1 ? "" : "s"). Changes below edit the keyframe at the playhead.")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)

            if !visual.keyframes.isEmpty {
                Text("Easing to next keyframe").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack {
                        ForEach(Easing.allCases) { e in
                            ChipButton(title: e.title, isSelected: currentEasing(visual) == e) { vm.setKeyframeEasing(e) }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Position X", value: vm.transformBinding(\.x, name: "Position"), range: -1...1,
                       format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0 * 100) })
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Position Y", value: vm.transformBinding(\.y, name: "Position"), range: -1...1,
                       format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0 * 100) })
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Scale", value: vm.transformBinding(\.scale, name: "Scale"), range: 0.05...4,
                       format: { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) })
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Rotation", value: vm.transformBinding(\.rotation, name: "Rotation"), range: -180...180,
                       format: { String(format: "%.0f°", $0) })
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Opacity", value: vm.transformBinding(\.opacity, name: "Opacity"), range: 0...1)
            Button("Reset position, scale and rotation") { vm.resetTransform() }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
        } else {
            EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a clip, sticker or text to add keyframes.")
        }
    }

    private func currentEasing(_ visual: EditorViewModel.VisualInfo) -> Easing? {
        let local = vm.currentTime - visual.start
        if let i = KeyframeMath.index(near: local, in: visual.keyframes) { return visual.keyframes[i].easing }
        return visual.keyframes.sorted { $0.time < $1.time }.last { $0.time <= local }?.easing
    }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Slider that groups a whole drag into one undo step and keeps the preview live.
struct EditSlider: View {
    let vm: EditorViewModel
    let title: String
    @Binding var value: Double
    var range: ClosedRange<Double>
    var format: (Double) -> String = { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) }

    var body: some View {
        ValueSlider(title: title, value: $value, range: range, format: format) { editing in
            if editing {
                vm.beginInteractive()
            } else {
                vm.endInteractive(title)
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Speed

struct SpeedPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Speed") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let clip = vm.selectedClip, !clip.isImage {
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Speed",
                                   value: Binding(get: { log2(clip.speed) }, set: { vm.setSpeed(pow(2, $0)) }),
                                   range: log2(0.1)...log2(8),
                                   format: { String(format: "%.2fx", pow(2, $0)) })
                        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                            HStack {
                                ForEach([0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 4, 8], id: \.self) { s in
                                    ChipButton(title: s < 1 ? String(format: "%.2gx", s) : String(format: "%gx", s),
                                               isSelected: abs(clip.speed - s) < 0.01) { vm.setSpeed(s) }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Text("Speed ramps").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                            HStack {
                                ForEach(SpeedRamp.allCases) { r in
                                    ChipButton(title: r.title, icon: r == .none ? "circle.slash" : "chart.xyaxis.line",
                                               isSelected: clip.ramp == r) { vm.setRamp(r) }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Text("Duration \(String(format: "%.1fs", clip.sourceDuration)) → \(String(format: "%.1fs", clip.duration)). Audio keeps its pitch.")
                            .font(.footnote)
                            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                }
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a video clip to change its speed.")
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Volume

struct VolumePanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Audio") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    if let audio = vm.selectedAudio {
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Volume", value: vm.audioBinding(\.volume, default: 1, name: "Volume"), range: 0...1)
                        Toggle("Mute", isOn: vm.audioBinding(\.muted, default: false, name: "Mute"))
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Fade in", value: vm.audioBinding(\.fadeIn, default: 0, name: "Fade In"),
                                   range: 0...min(5, audio.duration / 2), format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Fade out", value: vm.audioBinding(\.fadeOut, default: 0, name: "Fade Out"),
                                   range: 0...min(5, audio.duration / 2), format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                        denoiseButton(active: audio.denoise)
                    } else if let clip = vm.selectedClip, !clip.isImage {
                        if clip.audioDetached {
                            Label("Audio is detached to its own track. Select it on the audio track to edit.", systemImage: "info.circle")
                                .font(.footnote)
                                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                        }
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Volume", value: vm.clipBinding(\.volume, default: 1, name: "Volume"), range: 0...1)
                        Toggle("Mute", isOn: vm.clipBinding(\.muted, default: false, name: "Mute"))
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Fade in", value: vm.clipBinding(\.fadeIn, default: 0, name: "Fade In"),
                                   range: 0...min(5, clip.duration / 2), format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Fade out", value: vm.clipBinding(\.fadeOut, default: 0, name: "Fade Out"),
                                   range: 0...min(5, clip.duration / 2), format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                        HStack {
                            denoiseButton(active: clip.denoise)
                            Button {
                                vm.detachAudio()
                            } label: {
                                Label("Detach audio", systemImage: "rectangle.and.arrow.up.right.and.arrow.down.left")
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                            .disabled(clip.audioDetached)
                        }
                    } else {
                        EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a video or audio clip.")
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
            }
        }
    }

    private func denoiseButton(active: Bool) -> some View {
        Button {
            vm.toggleDenoise()
        } label: {
            Label(active ? "Noise reduction on" : "Reduce noise", systemImage: "waveform.badge.minus")
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.bordered)
        .tint(active ? Theme.accent : .white)
    }
}

// MARK: - Crop

struct CropPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    private let presets: [(String, Double?)] = [("Original", nil), ("9:16", 9.0 / 16.0), ("1:1", 1), ("4:5", 0.8), ("16:9", 16.0 / 9.0), ("4:3", 4.0 / 3.0)]

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Crop & Rotate") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let clip = vm.selectedClip, let media = vm.media(clip.mediaID) {
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                            HStack {
                                ForEach(presets, id: \.0) { preset in
                                    ChipButton(title: preset.0, isSelected: isPreset(clip.crop, preset.1, media.size)) {
                                        vm.updateSelectedClip("Crop") { c in
                                            c.crop = preset.1.map { CropRect.centered(aspect: $0, sourceSize: media.size) } ?? .full
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        HStack(spacing: 10) {
                            actionButton("Rotate", "rotate.right") { vm.updateSelectedClip("Rotate") { $0.quarterTurns = ($0.quarterTurns + 1) % 4 } }
                            actionButton("Flip H", "arrow.left.and.right.righttriangle.left.righttriangle.right") { vm.updateSelectedClip("Flip") { $0.flipH.toggle() } }
                            actionButton("Flip V", "arrow.up.and.down.righttriangle.up.righttriangle.down") { vm.updateSelectedClip("Flip") { $0.flipV.toggle() } }
                        }
                        Picker("Fit", selection: vm.clipBinding(\.fit, default: .fit, name: "Fit")) {
                            Text("Fit (show all)").tag(FitMode.fit)
                            Text("Fill (no bars)").tag(FitMode.fill)
                        }
                        .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Zoom", value: vm.transformBinding(\.scale, name: "Zoom"), range: 0.2...4,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.0f%%", $0 * 100) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Pan horizontal", value: vm.transformBinding(\.x, name: "Pan"), range: -1...1,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0 * 100) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Pan vertical", value: vm.transformBinding(\.y, name: "Pan"), range: -1...1,
                                   format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0 * 100) })
                        Text("Crop edges").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Left", value: cropBinding(.left), range: 0...0.45)
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Right", value: cropBinding(.right), range: 0...0.45)
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Top", value: cropBinding(.top), range: 0...0.45)
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Bottom", value: cropBinding(.bottom), range: 0...0.45)
                        Button("Reset crop & position") {
                            vm.updateSelectedClip("Reset Crop") { c in
                                c.crop = .full
                                c.quarterTurns = 0
                                c.flipH = false
                                c.flipV = false
                            }
                            vm.resetTransform()
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                }
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a clip to crop it.")
            }
        }
    }

    private enum Edge { case left, right, top, bottom }

    private func cropBinding(_ edge: Edge) -> Binding<Double> {
        Binding(
            get: {
                let c = vm.selectedClip?.crop ?? .full
                switch edge {
                case .left: return c.x
                case .right: return 1 - c.x - c.width
                case .top: return c.y
                case .bottom: return 1 - c.y - c.height
                }
            },
            set: { v in
                vm.updateSelectedClip("Crop", coalesce: "crop") { clip in
                    var c = clip.crop
                    var l = c.x, r = 1 - c.x - c.width, t = c.y, b = 1 - c.y - c.height
                    switch edge {
                    case .left: l = v
                    case .right: r = v
                    case .top: t = v
                    case .bottom: b = v
                    }
                    c.x = l
                    c.width = max(0.05, 1 - l - r)
                    c.y = t
                    c.height = max(0.05, 1 - t - b)
                    clip.crop = c
                }
            }
        )
    }

    private func isPreset(_ crop: CropRect, _ aspect: Double?, _ size: CGSize) -> Bool {
        guard let aspect else { return crop.isFull }
        let target = CropRect.centered(aspect: aspect, sourceSize: size)
        return abs(target.width - crop.width) < 0.005 && abs(target.height - crop.height) < 0.005
    }

    private func actionButton(_ title: String, _ icon: String, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            VStack(spacing: 4) {
                Image(systemName: icon).font(.system(size: 18))
                Text(title).font(.caption)
            }
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 54)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.bordered)
        .tint(.white)
    }
}

// MARK: - Trim

struct TrimPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Trim") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let clip = vm.selectedClip {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    if clip.isImage {
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Duration",
                                   value: Binding(get: { clip.sourceDuration }, set: { v in
                                       vm.updateSelectedClip("Duration", coalesce: "duration") { $0.sourceEnd = $0.sourceStart + max(0.2, v) }
                                   }),
                                   range: 0.2...30, format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                    } else {
                        let total = vm.media(clip.mediaID)?.duration ?? clip.sourceEnd
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Start",
                                   value: Binding(get: { clip.sourceStart }, set: { v in
                                       vm.updateSelectedClip("Trim", coalesce: "trim-start") { $0.sourceStart = min(v, $0.sourceEnd - 0.1) }
                                   }),
                                   range: 0...max(total, 0.2), format: { TimeFormat.short($0) })
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "End",
                                   value: Binding(get: { clip.sourceEnd }, set: { v in
                                       vm.updateSelectedClip("Trim", coalesce: "trim-end") { $0.sourceEnd = max(v, $0.sourceStart + 0.1) }
                                   }),
                                   range: 0...max(total, 0.2), format: { TimeFormat.short($0) })
                    }
                    Text("Clip length \(String(format: "%.1fs", clip.duration)). Tip: drag the white handles on the timeline to trim, and double-tap a clip to split it at the playhead.")
                        .font(.footnote)
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                Spacer()
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a clip to trim it.")
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Transition

struct TransitionPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Transition") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let id = vm.transitionTargetClipID, let clip = vm.project.clip(id) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    LazyVGrid(columns: Array(repeating: GridItem(.flexible(), spacing: 10), count: 4), spacing: 10) {
                        ForEach(TransitionType.allCases) { type in
                            Button {
                                Haptics.tap()
                                vm.setTransition(clipID: id, type: type)
                            } label: {
                                VStack(spacing: 6) {
                                    Image(systemName: type.icon).font(.system(size: 20))
                                    Text(type.title).font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                                }
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 62)
                                .background(clip.transitionOut.type == type ? Theme.accent : Color.white.opacity(0.08),
                                            in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
                            .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        }
                    }
                    if clip.transitionOut.type != .cut {
                        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Duration",
                                   value: Binding(get: { clip.transitionOut.duration },
                                                  set: { vm.setTransition(clipID: id, type: clip.transitionOut.type, duration: $0) }),
                                   range: 0.1...2, format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
                    }
                    Button {
                        vm.applyTransitionToAll(from: id)
                    } label: {
                        Label("Apply to all cuts", systemImage: "square.stack.3d.down.right")
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                Spacer()
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Tap the square between two clips to add a transition.")
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Panels/EffectsPanel.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Preset looks + manual adjustments, shared by per-clip and whole-timeline filters.
struct FilterEditor: View {
    let vm: EditorViewModel
    @Binding var filter: FilterSettings
    var showAdjustments = true

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                HStack(spacing: 10) {
                    ForEach(FilterPreset.allCases) { preset in
                        Button {
                            Haptics.tap()
                            filter.preset = preset
                            if filter.intensity < 0.05 { filter.intensity = 1 }
                        } label: {
                            VStack(spacing: 6) {
                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10)
                                    .fill(swatch(preset))
                                    .frame(width: 58, height: 58)
                                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10)
                                        .stroke(filter.preset == preset ? Theme.accent : Color.clear, lineWidth: 3))
                                Text(preset.title).font(.caption2.weight(.semibold))
                            }
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(PressableStyle())
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .accessibilityLabel("\(preset.title) filter")
                    }
                }
            }
            if filter.preset != .none {
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Intensity", value: $filter.intensity, range: 0...1)
            }
            if showAdjustments {
                Text("Adjust").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Brightness", value: $filter.adjustments.brightness, range: -1...1, format: signed)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Contrast", value: $filter.adjustments.contrast, range: -1...1, format: signed)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Saturation", value: $filter.adjustments.saturation, range: -1...1, format: signed)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Warmth", value: $filter.adjustments.warmth, range: -1...1, format: signed)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Tint", value: $filter.adjustments.tint, range: -1...1, format: signed)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Sharpen", value: $filter.adjustments.sharpen, range: 0...1)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Vignette", value: $filter.adjustments.vignette, range: 0...1)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Grain", value: $filter.adjustments.grain, range: 0...1)
                EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Blur", value: $filter.adjustments.blur, range: 0...1)
                if filter != FilterSettings() {
                    Button("Reset look") { filter = FilterSettings() }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func signed(_ v: Double) -> String { String(format: "%+.0f", v * 100) }

    private func swatch(_ p: FilterPreset) -> LinearGradient {
        let colors: [Color]
        switch p {
        case .none: colors = [.gray, .white.opacity(0.7)]
        case .vivid: colors = [.pink, .orange]
        case .warm: colors = [.orange, .yellow]
        case .cool: colors = [.blue, .cyan]
        case .mono, .noir: colors = [.black, .white]
        case .fade: colors = [.gray.opacity(0.6), .white.opacity(0.6)]
        case .chrome: colors = [.red, .blue]
        case .instant: colors = [.yellow.opacity(0.7), .brown]
        case .process: colors = [.teal, .purple]
        case .transfer: colors = [.orange.opacity(0.7), .indigo]
        case .sepia: colors = [.brown, .orange.opacity(0.6)]
        case .cinematic: colors = [.teal, .orange]
        case .vintage: colors = [.brown.opacity(0.8), .yellow.opacity(0.5)]
        case .dramatic: colors = [.black, .gray]
        case .film: colors = [.gray, .orange.opacity(0.5)]
        case .pastel: colors = [.pink.opacity(0.5), .mint.opacity(0.6)]
        case .teal: colors = [.teal, .cyan]
        }
        return LinearGradient(colors: colors, startPoint: .topLeading, endPoint: .bottomTrailing)
    }
}

struct GlobalFiltersPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Filters · Whole video") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                    Text("Applies to everything. To filter one clip, select it and tap Effects.")
                        .font(.footnote)
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                    FilterEditor(vm: vm, filter: vm.projectBinding(\.globalFilter, name: "Filter"))
                }
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .padding(.bottom, 20)
            }
        }
    }
}

struct EffectsPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var tab = 0

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Effects") { vm.activePanel = nil }
            if let clip = vm.selectedClip {
                Picker("", selection: $tab) {
                    Text("Looks").tag(0)
                    Text("Keying").tag(1)
                    Text("Tools").tag(2)
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                .padding(.horizontal)
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        switch tab {
                        case 0:
                            FilterEditor(vm: vm, filter: vm.clipBinding(\.filter, default: FilterSettings(), name: "Filter"))
                            Button {
                                vm.applyFilterToAllClips()
                            } label: {
                                Label("Apply this look to all clips", systemImage: "square.stack.3d.up")
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                        case 1:
                            keying(clip)
                        default:
                            tools(clip)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    .padding(.bottom, 20)
                }
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a clip to add effects.")
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func keying(_ clip: Clip) -> some View {
        Toggle(isOn: vm.clipBinding(\.effects.removeBackground, default: false, name: "Remove Background")) {
            Label("Remove background (people)", systemImage: "person.crop.rectangle")
        }
        Text("Keeps people and removes everything behind them using on-device Vision.")
            .font(.footnote)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
        Divider()
        Toggle(isOn: vm.clipBinding(\.effects.chromaKey.enabled, default: false, name: "Green Screen")) {
            Label("Green screen (chroma key)", systemImage: "drop.halffull")
        }
        if clip.effects.chromaKey.enabled {
            HStack {
                ChipButton(title: "Green", isSelected: clip.effects.chromaKey.color == .green) {
                    vm.updateSelectedClip("Key Color") { $0.effects.chromaKey.color = .green }
                }
                ChipButton(title: "Blue", isSelected: clip.effects.chromaKey.color == RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 0, b: 1)) {
                    vm.updateSelectedClip("Key Color") { $0.effects.chromaKey.color = RGBAColor(r: 0, g: 0, b: 1) }
                }
                Spacer()
                ColorPicker("Custom", selection: Binding(
                    get: { clip.effects.chromaKey.color.color },
                    set: { c in vm.updateSelectedClip("Key Color", coalesce: "keycolor") { $0.effects.chromaKey.color = RGBAColor(c) } }
                ), supportsOpacity: false)
                .fixedSize()
            }
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Tolerance", value: vm.clipBinding(\.effects.chromaKey.tolerance, default: 0.35, name: "Tolerance"), range: 0...1)
            EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Edge softness", value: vm.clipBinding(\.effects.chromaKey.softness, default: 0.1, name: "Softness"), range: 0...1)
            Text("Tip: put this clip on the overlay track (Overlay › Video) to key it over another video.")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func tools(_ clip: Clip) -> some View {
        if !clip.isImage {
            Button {
                vm.stabilizeSelected()
            } label: {
                Label(clip.effects.stabilize ? "Stabilized — tap to turn off" : "Stabilize shaky footage",
                      systemImage: "hand.raised.slash")
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            .tint(clip.effects.stabilize ? Theme.accent : .white)
            Button {
                vm.toggleDenoise()
            } label: {
                Label(clip.denoise ? "Noise reduction on" : "Reduce background noise", systemImage: "waveform.badge.minus")
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            .tint(clip.denoise ? Theme.accent : .white)
        }
        HStack {
            Button {
                vm.copyEffects()
            } label: {
                Label("Copy effects", systemImage: "doc.on.doc").frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            Button {
                vm.pasteEffects()
            } label: {
                Label("Paste", systemImage: "doc.on.clipboard").frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: 44)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
            .disabled(vm.copiedEffects == nil)
        }
        Text("Copy & paste carries the look, keying, speed, volume and animations to another clip.")
            .font(.footnote)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Panels/PanelHost.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Routes an `EditorPanel` to its view.
struct PanelHost: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    let panel: EditorPanel

    var body: some View {
        Group {
            switch panel {
            case .audio: AudioPanel(vm: vm)
            case .overlay: OverlayPanel(vm: vm)
            case .captions: CaptionsPanel(vm: vm)
            case .filters: GlobalFiltersPanel(vm: vm)
            case .canvas: CanvasPanel(vm: vm)
            case .speed: SpeedPanel(vm: vm)
            case .volume: VolumePanel(vm: vm)
            case .crop: CropPanel(vm: vm)
            case .animate: AnimatePanel(vm: vm)
            case .effects: EffectsPanel(vm: vm)
            case .trim: TrimPanel(vm: vm)
            case .text: TextPanel(vm: vm)
            case .transition: TransitionPanel(vm: vm)
            case .overlayStyle: OverlayStylePanel(vm: vm)
            }
        }
        .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
        .tint(Theme.accent)
    }
}

/// Binding helpers that route every change through the undoable edit pipeline.
extension EditorViewModel {
    func clipBinding<T>(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<Clip, T>, default value: T, name: String) -> Binding<T> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.selectedClip?[keyPath: keyPath] ?? value },
            set: { newValue in self.updateSelectedClip(name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue } }
        )
    }

    func textBinding<T>(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<TextItem, T>, default value: T, name: String) -> Binding<T> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.selectedText?[keyPath: keyPath] ?? value },
            set: { newValue in
                guard let id = self.selectedText?.id else { return }
                self.updateText(id, name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue }
            }
        )
    }

    func overlayBinding<T>(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<OverlayItem, T>, default value: T, name: String) -> Binding<T> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.selectedOverlay?[keyPath: keyPath] ?? value },
            set: { newValue in
                guard let id = self.selectedOverlay?.id else { return }
                self.updateOverlay(id, name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue }
            }
        )
    }

    func audioBinding<T>(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<AudioClip, T>, default value: T, name: String) -> Binding<T> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.selectedAudio?[keyPath: keyPath] ?? value },
            set: { newValue in
                guard let id = self.selectedAudio?.id else { return }
                self.updateAudio(id, name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue }
            }
        )
    }

    func projectBinding<T>(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<Project, T>, name: String) -> Binding<T> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.project[keyPath: keyPath] },
            set: { newValue in self.perform(name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue } }
        )
    }

    /// Binding for one component of the selected item's transform at the playhead.
    func transformBinding(_ keyPath: WritableKeyPath<Transform2D, Double>, name: String) -> Binding<Double> {
        Binding(
            get: { self.selectedResolvedTransform?[keyPath: keyPath] ?? Transform2D()[keyPath: keyPath] },
            set: { newValue in self.editTransform(name, coalesce: name) { $0[keyPath: keyPath] = newValue } }
        )
    }
}

struct EmptyPanelMessage: View {
    let text: String
    var body: some View {
        Text(text)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
            .padding()
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Panels/TextPanel.swift`

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import CoreText

struct TextPanel: View {
    @Bindable var vm: EditorViewModel
    @State private var tab = 0
    @FocusState private var editing: Bool

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            PanelHeader(title: "Text") {
                editing = false
                vm.activePanel = nil
            }
            if let item = vm.selectedText {
                TextField("Type something", text: Binding(
                    get: { item.text },
                    set: { newText in vm.updateText(item.id, "Edit Text", coalesce: "text") { $0.text = newText } }
                ), axis: .vertical)
                .lineLimit(1...4)
                .font(.title3.weight(.semibold))
                .padding(12)
                .background(Color.white.opacity(0.08), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                .focused($editing)
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .onAppear {
                    if item.text == "Your text" { editing = true }
                }

                Picker("", selection: $tab) {
                    Text("Font").tag(0)
                    Text("Style").tag(1)
                    Text("Background").tag(2)
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                .padding(.horizontal)

                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        switch tab {
                        case 0: fontTab(item)
                        case 1: styleTab(item)
                        default: boxTab(item)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    .padding(.bottom, 20)
                }
            } else {
                EmptyPanelMessage(text: "Select a text item, or tap Text to add one.")
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func fontTab(_ item: TextItem) -> some View {
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Size", value: vm.textBinding(\.fontSize, default: 72, name: "Text Size"), range: 20...240,
                   format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0) })
        HStack(spacing: 10) {
            toggleChip("Bold", "bold", item.bold) { vm.updateText(item.id, "Bold") { $0.bold.toggle() } }
            toggleChip("Italic", "italic", item.italic) { vm.updateText(item.id, "Italic") { $0.italic.toggle() } }
            Spacer()
            ForEach(TextAlign.allCases) { a in
                Button {
                    Haptics.tap()
                    vm.updateText(item.id, "Alignment") { $0.alignment = a }
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: a.icon)
                        .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                        .background(item.alignment == a ? Theme.accent : Color.white.opacity(0.08), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
                }
                .foregroundStyle(.white)
                .accessibilityLabel("Align \(a.rawValue)")
            }
        }
        FontPickerList(selected: item.fontFamily, sample: item.text) { family in
            vm.updateText(item.id, "Font") { $0.fontFamily = family }
        }
        .frame(height: 260)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func styleTab(_ item: TextItem) -> some View {
        ColorRow(title: "Color", color: vm.textBinding(\.color, default: .white, name: "Text Color"))
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Outline", value: vm.textBinding(\.outlineWidth, default: 0, name: "Outline"), range: 0...10,
                   format: { String(format: "%.0f", $0) })
        if item.outlineWidth > 0 {
            ColorRow(title: "Outline color", color: vm.textBinding(\.outlineColor, default: .black, name: "Outline Color"))
        }
        Toggle("Shadow", isOn: vm.textBinding(\.shadow, default: true, name: "Shadow"))
        if item.shadow {
            ColorRow(title: "Shadow color", color: vm.textBinding(\.shadowColor, default: .black, name: "Shadow Color"), supportsOpacity: true)
        }
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Opacity", value: vm.transformBinding(\.opacity, name: "Opacity"), range: 0...1)
        EditSlider(vm: vm, title: "Duration", value: vm.textBinding(\.duration, default: 3, name: "Text Duration"), range: 0.3...30,
                   format: { String(format: "%.1fs", $0) })
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func boxTab(_ item: TextItem) -> some View {
        Toggle("Background box", isOn: vm.textBinding(\.backgroundBox, default: false, name: "Text Box"))
        if item.backgroundBox {
            ColorRow(title: "Box color", color: vm.textBinding(\.boxColor, default: .black, name: "Box Color"), supportsOpacity: true)
        }
        Text("Drag the text in the preview to move it. Pinch to resize, twist to rotate.")
            .font(.footnote)
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
    }

    private func toggleChip(_ title: String, _ icon: String, _ on: Bool, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button {
            Haptics.tap()
            action()
        } label: {
            Image(systemName: icon)
                .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                .background(on ? Theme.accent : Color.white.opacity(0.08), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
        }
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
        .accessibilityLabel(title)
    }
}

/// Searchable list of system font families, each previewed in its own face.
struct FontPickerList: View {
    let selected: String
    let sample: String
    let onSelect: (String) -> Void
    @State private var query = ""

    private var families: [String] {
        let all = FontResolver.families
        guard !query.isEmpty else { return all }
        return all.filter { $0.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(query) }
    }

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 8) {
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                TextField("Search fonts", text: $query)
                    .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                    .autocorrectionDisabled()
            }
            .padding(10)
            .background(Color.white.opacity(0.08), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10))
            ScrollView {
                LazyVStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
                    ForEach(families, id: \.self) { family in
                        Button {
                            Haptics.tap()
                            onSelect(family)
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                    Text(preview)
                                        .font(Font(FontResolver.font(family: family, size: 20, bold: false, italic: false) as CTFont))
                                        .lineLimit(1)
                                    Text(family == "System" ? "System (San Francisco)" : family)
                                        .font(.caption2)
                                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondaryText)
                                }
                                Spacer()
                                if family == selected {
                                    Image(systemName: "checkmark").foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(.vertical, 8)
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        Divider().overlay(Color.white.opacity(0.06))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private var preview: String {
        let trimmed = sample.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        return trimmed.isEmpty || trimmed == "Your text" ? "The quick brown fox" : String(trimmed.prefix(28))
    }
}
```

## `Cutline/Views/Timeline/TimelineInteraction.swift`

```swift
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Translates touches on the timeline into scrubbing, trimming, moving, zooming and selection.
@MainActor
final class TimelineInteraction: NSObject {
    private weak var vm: EditorViewModel?
    var viewSize: CGSize = .zero

    private enum PanMode {
        case none
        case scrub(startTime: Double)
        case vertical(startOffset: CGFloat)
        case trim(selection: TimelineSelection, edge: TrimEdge, edgeTime: Double, entryID: UUID)
    }

    private var panMode: PanMode = .none
    private var pinchBase: Double = 60
    private var moveState: (selection: TimelineSelection, kind: TrackKind, grab: Double, duration: Double, id: UUID)?
    private var lastSnap: Double?

    // Momentum scrolling
    private var displayLink: CADisplayLink?
    private var velocity: Double = 0
    private var lastTimestamp: CFTimeInterval = 0

    init(viewModel: EditorViewModel) {
        self.vm = viewModel
        super.init()
    }

    // MARK: Geometry

    private var geometry: TimelineGeometry? {
        guard let vm else { return nil }
        return TimelineGeometry(width: viewSize.width, currentTime: vm.playback.currentTime, pixelsPerSecond: vm.pixelsPerSecond)
    }

    private func contentY(_ point: CGPoint) -> CGFloat {
        point.y - TimelineLayout.rulerHeight + (vm?.verticalOffset ?? 0)
    }

    private var layout: TimelineLayout? {
        guard let vm else { return nil }
        return TimelineLayout(project: vm.project)
    }

    private func maxVerticalOffset(_ layout: TimelineLayout) -> CGFloat {
        max(0, layout.contentHeight - (viewSize.height - TimelineLayout.rulerHeight))
    }

    // MARK: Tap

    func tap(at point: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm, let geo = geometry, let layout else { return }
        stopMomentum()
        let time = geo.time(for: point.x)
        if point.y < TimelineLayout.rulerHeight {
            vm.playback.scrub(to: time)
            Haptics.tap()
            return
        }
        let y = contentY(point)

        // Transition junctions on the main track
        if let videoRow = layout.rows.first(where: { $0.kind == .video }), videoRow.contains(y: y) {
            let clips = vm.project.clips
            for i in 0..<max(0, clips.count - 1) where abs(clips[i].timelineEnd - clips[i + 1].timelineStart) < 0.02 {
                let jx = geo.x(for: clips[i].timelineEnd)
                if abs(point.x - jx) < 14 {
                    vm.transitionTargetClipID = clips[i].id
                    vm.activePanel = .transition
                    Haptics.tap()
                    return
                }
            }
            // "+" button after the last clip
            let endX = geo.x(for: clips.last?.timelineEnd ?? 0)
            if point.x > endX + 6 && point.x < endX + 56 {
                vm.presentPicker(.mainTrack)
                return
            }
        }

        let slop = 6 / vm.pixelsPerSecond
        if let entry = layout.entry(atContentY: y, time: time, slop: slop) {
            vm.select(entry.selection)
            return
        }
        if let row = layout.row(atContentY: y), layout.entries(in: row.kind).isEmpty {
            switch row.kind {
            case .video: vm.presentPicker(.mainTrack)
            case .overlay: vm.activePanel = .overlay
            case .text: vm.addText()
            case .audio: vm.activePanel = .audio
            case .caption: break
            }
            return
        }
        vm.select(nil)
    }

    func doubleTap(at point: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm, let geo = geometry, let layout else { return }
        let time = geo.time(for: point.x)
        let y = contentY(point)
        if let entry = layout.entry(atContentY: y, time: time, slop: 4 / vm.pixelsPerSecond) {
            vm.select(entry.selection)
            vm.splitAtPlayhead()
        } else {
            vm.splitAtPlayhead()
        }
    }

    // MARK: Pan

    func panBegan(at point: CGPoint, velocity v: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm, let geo = geometry, let layout else { return }
        stopMomentum()
        let y = contentY(point)

        // Trim handles of the selected item
        if point.y >= TimelineLayout.rulerHeight, let entry = layout.entry(for: vm.selection), entry.row.contains(y: y) || abs(y - (entry.row.y + entry.row.height / 2)) < entry.row.height {
            let sx = geo.x(for: entry.start), ex = geo.x(for: entry.end)
            if point.x > sx - 26 && point.x < sx + 12 {
                beginTrim(entry, edge: .left, edgeTime: entry.start)
                return
            }
            if point.x > ex - 12 && point.x < ex + 26 {
                beginTrim(entry, edge: .right, edgeTime: entry.end)
                return
            }
        }

        if abs(v.y) > abs(v.x) * 1.3 && maxVerticalOffset(layout) > 0 && point.y >= TimelineLayout.rulerHeight {
            panMode = .vertical(startOffset: vm.verticalOffset)
            return
        }
        if vm.playback.isPlaying { vm.playback.pause() }
        panMode = .scrub(startTime: vm.playback.currentTime)
    }

    private func beginTrim(_ entry: TimelineEntry, edge: TrimEdge, edgeTime: Double) {
        guard let vm else { return }
        if vm.playback.isPlaying { vm.playback.pause() }
        vm.beginInteractive()
        panMode = .trim(selection: entry.selection, edge: edge, edgeTime: edgeTime, entryID: entry.id)
        lastSnap = nil
        Haptics.tap()
    }

    func panChanged(translation: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm else { return }
        switch panMode {
        case .none:
            break
        case .scrub(let start):
            let t = start - Double(translation.x) / vm.pixelsPerSecond
            vm.playback.scrub(to: t)
        case .vertical(let startOffset):
            guard let layout else { return }
            vm.verticalOffset = (startOffset - translation.y).clamped(0, maxVerticalOffset(layout))
        case .trim(let selection, let edge, let edgeTime, let entryID):
            var delta = Double(translation.x) / vm.pixelsPerSecond
            let target = edgeTime + delta
            if let snapped = snap(target, excluding: entryID) {
                delta = snapped - edgeTime
                vm.snapTime = snapped
            } else {
                vm.snapTime = nil
            }
            vm.updateInteractive { p, _ in
                EditorViewModel.trim(&p, selection, edge: edge, delta: delta)
            }
        }
    }

    func panEnded(velocity v: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm else { return }
        switch panMode {
        case .scrub:
            let vx = Double(v.x)
            if abs(vx) > 250 {
                startMomentum(velocity: -vx / vm.pixelsPerSecond)
            }
        case .trim:
            vm.endInteractive("Trim")
            vm.snapTime = nil
        default:
            break
        }
        panMode = .none
    }

    // MARK: Snapping

    private func snap(_ time: Double, excluding id: UUID?) -> Double? {
        guard let vm else { return nil }
        let threshold = 10 / vm.pixelsPerSecond
        let candidates = vm.snapCandidates(excluding: id)
        guard let best = candidates.min(by: { abs($0 - time) < abs($1 - time) }), abs(best - time) <= threshold else {
            lastSnap = nil
            return nil
        }
        if lastSnap == nil || abs(lastSnap! - best) > 0.0001 {
            Haptics.snap()
        }
        lastSnap = best
        return best
    }

    // MARK: Pinch zoom

    func pinch(scale: CGFloat, state: UIGestureRecognizer.State) {
        guard let vm else { return }
        switch state {
        case .began:
            stopMomentum()
            pinchBase = vm.pixelsPerSecond
        case .changed:
            let newValue = (pinchBase * Double(scale)).clamped(5, 800)
            if (newValue == 5 || newValue == 800) && vm.pixelsPerSecond != newValue { Haptics.snap() }
            vm.pixelsPerSecond = newValue
        default:
            vm.scheduleStateSave()
        }
    }

    // MARK: Long-press move

    func longPressBegan(at point: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm, let geo = geometry, let layout else { return }
        stopMomentum()
        let time = geo.time(for: point.x)
        guard let entry = layout.entry(atContentY: contentY(point), time: time, slop: 0) else { return }
        if vm.playback.isPlaying { vm.playback.pause() }
        vm.select(entry.selection)
        moveState = (entry.selection, entry.kind, time - entry.start, entry.end - entry.start, entry.id)
        lastSnap = nil
        Haptics.action()
        if entry.kind == .video {
            vm.dragGhost = DragGhost(selection: entry.selection, kind: .video, start: entry.start, duration: entry.end - entry.start)
        } else {
            vm.beginInteractive()
        }
    }

    func longPressChanged(at point: CGPoint) {
        guard let vm, let geo = geometry, let state = moveState else { return }
        let fingerTime = geo.time(for: point.x)
        var start = max(0, fingerTime - state.grab)
        if let s = snap(start, excluding: state.id) {
            start = s
            vm.snapTime = s
        } else if let e = snap(start + state.duration, excluding: state.id) {
            start = max(0, e - state.duration)
            vm.snapTime = e
        } else {
            vm.snapTime = nil
        }
        if state.kind == .video {
            vm.dragGhost?.start = start
        } else {
            let selection = state.selection
            vm.updateInteractive { p, _ in
                EditorViewModel.move(&p, selection, to: start)
            }
        }
    }

    func longPressEnded(cancelled: Bool) {
        guard let vm, let state = moveState else { return }
        moveState = nil
        vm.snapTime = nil
        if state.kind == .video {
            let ghost = vm.dragGhost
            vm.dragGhost = nil
            if !cancelled, let ghost, case .clip(let id) = state.selection {
                vm.commitMainTrackMove(clipID: id, dropStart: ghost.start)
            }
        } else if cancelled {
            vm.cancelInteractive()
        } else {
            vm.endInteractive("Move")
        }
    }

    // MARK: Momentum

    private func startMomentum(velocity v: Double) {
        velocity = v
        lastTimestamp = 0
        let link = CADisplayLink(target: self, selector: #selector(step(_:)))
        link.add(to: .main, forMode: .common)
        displayLink = link
    }

    func stopMomentum() {
        displayLink?.invalidate()
        displayLink = nil
        velocity = 0
    }

    @objc private func step(_ link: CADisplayLink) {
        guard let vm else {
            stopMomentum()
            return
        }
        if lastTimestamp == 0 {
            lastTimestamp = link.timestamp
            return
        }
        let dt = link.timestamp - lastTimestamp
        lastTimestamp = link.timestamp
        let next = vm.playback.currentTime + velocity * dt
        vm.playback.scrub(to: next)
        velocity *= pow(0.998, dt * 1000)
        if abs(velocity * vm.pixelsPerSecond) < 20 || next <= 0 || next >= vm.playback.duration {
            stopMomentum()
        }
    }
}

/// Transparent UIKit layer that recognizes timeline gestures precisely and simultaneously.
struct TimelineGestureLayer: UIViewRepresentable {
    let interaction: TimelineInteraction

    func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator(interaction: interaction) }

    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> UIView {
        let view = UIView()
        view.backgroundColor = .clear
        let c = context.coordinator

        let pan = UIPanGestureRecognizer(target: c, action: #selector(Coordinator.handlePan(_:)))
        pan.maximumNumberOfTouches = 1
        pan.delegate = c
        let pinch = UIPinchGestureRecognizer(target: c, action: #selector(Coordinator.handlePinch(_:)))
        pinch.delegate = c
        let doubleTap = UITapGestureRecognizer(target: c, action: #selector(Coordinator.handleDoubleTap(_:)))
        doubleTap.numberOfTapsRequired = 2
        let tap = UITapGestureRecognizer(target: c, action: #selector(Coordinator.handleTap(_:)))
        tap.require(toFail: doubleTap)
        let long = UILongPressGestureRecognizer(target: c, action: #selector(Coordinator.handleLongPress(_:)))
        long.minimumPressDuration = 0.35
        long.delegate = c
        pan.require(toFail: long)

        [pan, pinch, doubleTap, tap, long].forEach { view.addGestureRecognizer($0) }
        return view
    }

    func updateUIView(_ uiView: UIView, context: Context) {
        context.coordinator.interaction = interaction
    }

    @MainActor
    final class Coordinator: NSObject, UIGestureRecognizerDelegate {
        var interaction: TimelineInteraction

        init(interaction: TimelineInteraction) {
            self.interaction = interaction
        }

        @objc func handleTap(_ g: UITapGestureRecognizer) {
            interaction.tap(at: g.location(in: g.view))
        }

        @objc func handleDoubleTap(_ g: UITapGestureRecognizer) {
            interaction.doubleTap(at: g.location(in: g.view))
        }

        @objc func handlePan(_ g: UIPanGestureRecognizer) {
            switch g.state {
            case .began:
                let t = g.translation(in: g.view)
                let start = CGPoint(x: g.location(in: g.view).x - t.x, y: g.location(in: g.view).y - t.y)
                interaction.panBegan(at: start, velocity: g.velocity(in: g.view))
                interaction.panChanged(translation: t)
            case .changed:
                interaction.panChanged(translation: g.translation(in: g.view))
            case .ended, .cancelled, .failed:
                interaction.panEnded(velocity: g.velocity(in: g.view))
            default:
                break
            }
        }

        @objc func handlePinch(_ g: UIPinchGestureRecognizer) {
            interaction.pinch(scale: g.scale, state: g.state)
        }

        @objc func handleLongPress(_ g: UILongPressGestureRecognizer) {
            switch g.state {
            case .began:
                interaction.longPressBegan(at: g.location(in: g.view))
            case .changed:
                interaction.longPressChanged(at: g.location(in: g.view))
            case .ended:
                interaction.longPressEnded(cancelled: false)
            case .cancelled, .failed:
                interaction.longPressEnded(cancelled: true)
            default:
                break
            }
        }

        nonisolated func gestureRecognizer(_ gestureRecognizer: UIGestureRecognizer,
                                           shouldRecognizeSimultaneouslyWith other: UIGestureRecognizer) -> Bool {
            (gestureRecognizer is UIPinchGestureRecognizer) || (other is UIPinchGestureRecognizer)
        }
    }
}
```
